 /**
  * @brief Read data from guest RAM pointed by the linked list configured in the given DmaConfigState index.

+uint32_t esp_gdma_get_out_transfer_size(ESPGdmaState *s, uint32_t chan,
+                                        uint32_t max_size)
+{
+    DmaConfigState *state = &s->ch_conf[ESP_GDMA_OUT_IDX][chan];
+    uint32_t addr = ((ESP_GDMA_RAM_ADDR >> 20) << 20)
+                  | FIELD_EX32(state->link, GDMA_OUT_LINK, ADDR);
+    uint32_t total = 0;
+
+    for (int i = 0; addr && i < 256; i++) {
+        GdmaLinkedList desc;
+        if (!esp_gdma_read_descr(s, addr, &desc)) {
+            break;
+        }
+        total += MIN(desc.config.length, max_size - total);
+        if (total >= max_size || desc.config.suc_eof) {
+            break;
+        }
+        addr = desc.next_addr;
+    }
+    return total;
+}
+
+uint32_t esp_gdma_snapshot_out_channel(ESPGdmaState *s, uint32_t chan,
+                                       uint8_t *buffer, uint32_t size)
+{
+    DmaConfigState *state = &s->ch_conf[ESP_GDMA_OUT_IDX][chan];
+    uint32_t addr = ((ESP_GDMA_RAM_ADDR >> 20) << 20)
+                  | FIELD_EX32(state->link, GDMA_OUT_LINK, ADDR);
+    const uint32_t start = addr;
+    uint32_t consumed = 0;
+
+    /*
+     * This is a read-only view of the descriptor ring. Peripheral models use
+     * it to observe data written by the guest without clearing owner bits or
+     * advancing GDMA state.
+     */
+    for (int i = 0; addr && i < 256 && consumed < size; i++) {
+        GdmaLinkedList desc;
+        uint32_t copy;
+
+        if (!esp_gdma_read_descr(s, addr, &desc)) {
+            break;
+        }
+        copy = MIN(desc.config.length, size - consumed);
+        if (getenv("M5SW_GDMA_SNAPSHOT_TRACE")) {
+            uint8_t head[16] = { 0 };
+            uint32_t head_len = MIN(copy, sizeof(head));
+            if (head_len) {
+                esp_gdma_read_guest(s, desc.buf_addr, head, head_len);
+            }
+            fprintf(stderr,
+                    "[gdma-snapshot] dscr=%08x buf=%08x len=%u owner=%u eof=%u head=",
+                    addr, desc.buf_addr, desc.config.length,
+                    desc.config.owner, desc.config.suc_eof);
+            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < head_len; i++) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "%02x", head[i]);
+            }
+            fprintf(stderr, "\n");
+        }
+        if (copy && !esp_gdma_read_guest(s, desc.buf_addr,
+                                         buffer + consumed, copy)) {
+            break;
+        }
+        consumed += copy;
+        if (!desc.next_addr || desc.next_addr == start) {
+            break;
+        }
+        addr = desc.next_addr;
+    }
+    return consumed;
+}
+
+/*
+ * Consume one descriptor from a running peripheral-to-memory OUT channel
+ * without clearing START. Cyclic DMA keeps advancing through the linked ring;
+ * peripheral models use this to expose each completed buffer and raise OUT_EOF
+ * exactly as the controller does at the end of a descriptor.
+ */
+uint32_t esp_gdma_complete_out_descriptor(ESPGdmaState *s, uint32_t chan,
+                                          uint8_t *buffer, uint32_t size)
+{
+    DmaConfigState *state = &s->ch_conf[ESP_GDMA_OUT_IDX][chan];
+    const uint32_t high = (ESP_GDMA_RAM_ADDR >> 20) << 20;
+    uint32_t addr = state->current_desc_addr
+                  ? state->current_desc_addr
+                  : high | FIELD_EX32(state->link, GDMA_OUT_LINK, ADDR);
+    GdmaLinkedList desc;
+    uint32_t copy;
+    uint32_t next;
+
+    if (!addr || !esp_gdma_read_descr(s, addr, &desc)) {
+        esp_gdma_set_status(&state->int_state,
+                            R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_OUT_DSCR_ERR_MASK);
+        return 0;
+    }
+    copy = MIN(desc.config.length, size);
+    if (copy && !esp_gdma_read_guest(s, desc.buf_addr, buffer, copy)) {
+        esp_gdma_set_status(&state->int_state,
+                            R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_OUT_DSCR_ERR_MASK);
+        return 0;
+    }
+    state->suc_eof_desc_addr = addr;
+    next = desc.next_addr;
+    if (next) {
+        esp_gdma_push_descriptor(s, chan, ESP_GDMA_OUT_IDX, next);
+    }
+    esp_gdma_set_status(&state->int_state,
+                        R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_OUT_EOF_MASK);
+    return copy;
+}
+
+/*
+ * Deliver one descriptor worth of data to a running peripheral-to-memory IN
+ * channel and advance through its cyclic linked ring.
+ */
+uint32_t esp_gdma_complete_in_descriptor(ESPGdmaState *s, uint32_t chan,
+                                         const uint8_t *buffer, uint32_t size)
+{
+    DmaConfigState *state = &s->ch_conf[ESP_GDMA_IN_IDX][chan];
+    const uint32_t high = (ESP_GDMA_RAM_ADDR >> 20) << 20;
+    uint32_t addr = state->current_desc_addr
+                  ? state->current_desc_addr
+                  : high | FIELD_EX32(state->link, GDMA_IN_LINK, ADDR);
+    GdmaLinkedList desc;
+    uint32_t copy;
+    uint32_t next;
+
+    if (!addr || !esp_gdma_read_descr(s, addr, &desc)) {
+        esp_gdma_set_status(&state->int_state,
+                            R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_IN_DSCR_ERR_MASK);
+        return 0;
+    }
+    copy = MIN(desc.config.size, size);
+    if (copy && !esp_gdma_write_guest(s, desc.buf_addr, (void *)buffer,
+                                      copy)) {
+        esp_gdma_set_status(&state->int_state,
+                            R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_IN_DSCR_ERR_MASK);
+        return 0;
+    }
+    desc.config.length = copy;
+    desc.config.owner = 0;
+    if (!esp_gdma_write_descr(s, addr, &desc)) {
+        esp_gdma_set_status(&state->int_state,
+                            R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_IN_DSCR_ERR_MASK);
+        return 0;
+    }
+    state->suc_eof_desc_addr = addr;
+    next = desc.next_addr;
+    if (next) {
+        esp_gdma_push_descriptor(s, chan, ESP_GDMA_IN_IDX, next);
+    }
+    esp_gdma_set_status(&state->int_state,
+                        R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_IN_SUC_EOF_MASK |
+                        R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_IN_DONE_MASK);
+    return copy;
+}

+void esp_gdma_set_chan_interrupt(ESPGdmaState *s, uint32_t dir, uint32_t chan,
+                                 uint32_t mask)
+{
+    ESPGdmaClass *class = ESP_GDMA_GET_CLASS(s);
+    assert(s != NULL && chan < class->m_channel_count && dir < ESP_GDMA_CONF_COUNT);
+    if (getenv("M5SW_GDMA_TRACE")) {
+        DmaIntState *st = &s->ch_conf[dir][chan].int_state;
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gdma] set irq dir=%u chan=%u mask=%02x ena=%02x raw=%02x\n",
+                dir, chan, mask, st->ena, st->raw);
+    }
+    esp_gdma_set_status(&s->ch_conf[dir][chan].int_state, mask);
+}
 
 void esp_gdma_write_register(ESPGdmaState* s, DmaRegister reg, uint32_t value)
 {

 #include "hw/irq.h"
 #include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
 #include "hw/gpio/esp32_gpio.h"
+#include "../m5stopwatch/m5stopwatch.h"
 
 
 
+/*
+ * Minimal functional GPIO model: register storage, OUT/ENABLE W1TS/W1TC,
+ * IN/IN1 computed from outputs and externally driven inputs (default: pulled up).
+ * Output-change listeners let board models observe e.g. software chip-selects.
+ */
+#define GPIO_NPINS 54
+#define GPIO_MAX_LISTENERS 8
+static uint64_t g_ext_in = ~0ULL;      /* externally driven input levels */
+static uint32_t g_regs[0x200];         /* 0x000..0x7ff */
+static uint64_t g_open_drain;          /* pins whose output stage is open-drain */
+static uint64_t g_force_mask;          /* passive board lines forced by a model */
+static uint64_t g_force_level;
+static struct {
+    esp32_gpio_out_cb cb;
+    void *opaque;
+} g_listeners[GPIO_NPINS][GPIO_MAX_LISTENERS];
+static uint8_t g_listener_count[GPIO_NPINS];
+static int g_trace_last_level = -1;
+
+static uint64_t gpio_line64(void);
+
+static int gpio_trace_pin(void)
+{
+    const char *value = getenv("M5SW_GPIO_TRACE_PIN");
+    int pin = value ? atoi(value) : 18;
+
+    return pin >= 0 && pin < GPIO_NPINS ? pin : 18;
+}
+
+void esp32_gpio_set_input(int pin, int level)
+{
+    if (pin < 0 || pin >= GPIO_NPINS) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (level) {
+        g_ext_in |= 1ULL << pin;
+    } else {
+        g_ext_in &= ~(1ULL << pin);
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_GPIO_TRACE") &&
+        (pin == 11 || pin == 12 || pin == gpio_trace_pin())) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpio] ext input pin=%d level=%d line=%d\n",
+                pin, level, esp32_gpio_get_line(pin));
+    }
+}
+
+void esp32_gpio_add_out_listener(int pin, esp32_gpio_out_cb cb, void *opaque)
+{
+    if (pin < 0 || pin >= GPIO_NPINS || !cb) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (g_listener_count[pin] >= GPIO_MAX_LISTENERS) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpio] listener overflow pin=%d\n", pin);
+        return;
+    }
+    unsigned slot = g_listener_count[pin]++;
+    g_listeners[pin][slot].cb = cb;
+    g_listeners[pin][slot].opaque = opaque;
+}
+
+void esp32_gpio_set_open_drain(int pin, bool enable)
+{
+    if (pin < 0 || pin >= GPIO_NPINS) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (enable) {
+        g_open_drain |= 1ULL << pin;
+    } else {
+        g_open_drain &= ~(1ULL << pin);
+    }
+}
+
+void esp32_gpio_force_line(int pin, int level)
+{
+    if (pin < 0 || pin >= GPIO_NPINS) {
+        return;
+    }
+    g_force_mask |= 1ULL << pin;
+    if (level) {
+        g_force_level |= 1ULL << pin;
+    } else {
+        g_force_level &= ~(1ULL << pin);
+    }
+}
+
+int esp32_gpio_get_line(int pin)
+{
+    if (pin < 0 || pin >= GPIO_NPINS) {
+        return 1;
+    }
+    int level = (gpio_line64() >> pin) & 1;
+    if (getenv("M5SW_GPIO_TRACE") && pin == gpio_trace_pin()) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpio] get pin=%d level=%d force=%d\n",
+                pin, level, !!(g_force_mask & (1ULL << pin)));
+    }
+    return level;
+}
+
+static uint64_t gpio_out64(void)
+{
+    return (uint64_t)g_regs[0x04 / 4] | ((uint64_t)g_regs[0x10 / 4] << 32);
+}
+
+static uint64_t gpio_en64(void)
+{
+    return (uint64_t)g_regs[0x20 / 4] | ((uint64_t)g_regs[0x2c / 4] << 32);
+}
+
+/*
+ * Effective pin level: driven output when the pin is enabled, otherwise
+ * the board's external pull-up.  Software-I2C listeners need this rather
+ * than the raw OUT register, because switching a pin between output-low
+ * and input is a real bus transition.
+ */
+static uint64_t gpio_line64(void)
+{
+    uint64_t out = gpio_out64();
+    uint64_t en = gpio_en64();
+    uint64_t driven = out & en;
+
+    /*
+     * An open-drain output can only pull low: when its latch is high the
+     * actual line level is determined by the external pull-up/devices.
+     * This is what makes software-I2C acknowledge bits visible to the
+     * master while it is releasing SDA.
+     */
+    uint64_t line = (driven & ~g_open_drain)
+                  | ((driven & g_ext_in) & g_open_drain)
+                  | (~en & g_ext_in);
+    return (line & ~g_force_mask) | (g_force_level & g_force_mask);
+}
+
+static void gpio_notify(uint64_t before, uint64_t after)
+{
+    uint64_t diff = before ^ after;
+    for (int i = 0; diff && i < GPIO_NPINS; i++) {
+        if ((diff >> i) & 1) {
+            for (int j = 0; j < g_listener_count[i]; j++) {
+                if (g_listeners[i][j].cb) {
+                    g_listeners[i][j].cb(g_listeners[i][j].opaque, i,
+                                         (after >> i) & 1);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
 static uint64_t esp32_gpio_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned int size)
 {
     Esp32GpioState *s = ESP32_GPIO(opaque);
-    uint64_t r = 0;
+    uint64_t in = gpio_line64();
     switch (addr) {
     case A_GPIO_STRAP:
-        r = s->strap_mode;
-        break;
+        return s->strap_mode;
+    case 0x3c: {
+        int trace_pin = gpio_trace_pin();
+        int trace_level = (in >> trace_pin) & 1;
 
+        if (getenv("M5SW_GPIO_TRACE") &&
+            trace_level != g_trace_last_level) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[gpio] in0=0x%08x pin%d=%d\n",
+                    (uint32_t)in, trace_pin, trace_level);
+            g_trace_last_level = trace_level;
+        }
+        return (uint32_t)in;
+    }
+    case 0x40:
+        return (uint32_t)(in >> 32) & 0x3fffff;
+    case 0x08: case 0x0c: case 0x14: case 0x18:
+    case 0x24: case 0x28: case 0x30: case 0x34:
+        return 0;
     default:
-        break;
+        if (addr < sizeof(g_regs)) {
+            return g_regs[addr / 4];
+        }
+        return 0;
     }
-    return r;
 }
 
 static void esp32_gpio_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr,
                        uint64_t value, unsigned int size)
 {
+    uint64_t before = gpio_line64();
+    if (getenv("M5SW_GPIO_REG_TRACE") && addr >= 0x100 && addr < 0x140) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpio] wr addr=0x%03" HWADDR_PRIx
+                " value=0x%08" PRIx64 "\n", addr, value);
+    }
+    switch (addr) {
+    case 0x08: g_regs[0x04 / 4] |= value; break;
+    case 0x0c: g_regs[0x04 / 4] &= ~value; break;
+    case 0x14: g_regs[0x10 / 4] |= value; break;
+    case 0x18: g_regs[0x10 / 4] &= ~value; break;
+    case 0x24: g_regs[0x20 / 4] |= value; break;
+    case 0x28: g_regs[0x20 / 4] &= ~value; break;
+    case 0x30: g_regs[0x2c / 4] |= value; break;
+    case 0x34: g_regs[0x2c / 4] &= ~value; break;
+    case 0x48: g_regs[0x44 / 4] |= value; break;
+    case 0x4c: g_regs[0x44 / 4] &= ~value; break;
+    case 0x54: g_regs[0x50 / 4] |= value; break;
+    case 0x58: g_regs[0x50 / 4] &= ~value; break;
+    case A_GPIO_STRAP: case 0x3c: case 0x40:
+        break;
+    default:
+        if (addr < sizeof(g_regs)) {
+            g_regs[addr / 4] = value;
+            /*
+             * GPIO_PIN0_REG starts at 0x74 on ESP32-S3.  M5GFX's
+             * pinMode(input*) uses gpio_ll_od_enable(), which leaves the
+             * output enable set and relies on open-drain + latch-high
+             * semantics so an external low can still pull the line down.
+             */
+            if (addr >= 0x74 && addr < 0x74 + GPIO_NPINS * 4) {
+                uint64_t pin_bit = 1ULL << ((addr - 0x74) / 4);
+                if (value & (1u << 2)) {
+                    g_open_drain |= pin_bit;
+                } else {
+                    g_open_drain &= ~pin_bit;
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    gpio_notify(before, gpio_line64());
 }

@@ -261,6 +267,11 @@ static void esp32_i2c_init(Object * obj)

 }
 
+void esp32_i2c_set_bus(DeviceState *dev, I2CBus *bus)
+{

+}
+
 static void esp32_i2c_class_init(ObjectClass * klass, void * data)
 {
     ResettableClass *rc = RESETTABLE_CLASS(klass);
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/co5300.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/co5300.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d263d67
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/co5300.c
@@ -0,0 +1,386 @@
+/*
+ * CO5300 466x466 AMOLED controller (QSPI command set as used by M5GFX Panel_AMOLED)
+ * Frames are delimited by the software chip-select on GPIO39.
+ *   [0x02, 0x00, cmd, 0x00, params...]      register write (1-line)
+ *   [0x32|0x12, 0x00, 0x2C|0x3C, 0x00, px]  memory write (4-line), RGB565 big-endian
+ * Also hosts the input handler: keyboard -> KEYA(G2)/KEYB(G1)/PWR, mouse -> CST820.
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/log.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-core.h"
+#include "ui/console.h"
+#include "ui/input.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define MEM_W 480
+#define MEM_H 480
+#define VIS_W 466
+#define VIS_H 466
+#define VIS_X0 6
+#define VIS_Y0 0
+#define PIN_CS 39
+#define PIN_KEYA 2
+#define PIN_KEYB 1
+#define PIN_TE 38
+
+struct Co5300State {
+    DeviceState parent_obj;
+    QemuConsole *con;
+    uint16_t mem[MEM_W * MEM_H];
+    bool dirty;
+    /* frame parser */
+    bool selected;
+    uint32_t nbytes;
+    uint8_t hdr[4];
+    uint8_t cmd;
+    uint32_t pidx;
+    uint8_t params[8];
+    bool pixel_mode;
+    uint8_t pix_hi;
+    bool pix_half;
+    /* registers */
+    uint16_t xs, xe, ys, ye, cx, cy;
+    uint8_t madctl, colmod, bright;
+    bool disp_on, sleep, invert;
+    uint64_t pixels_written;
+    int mouse_x, mouse_y, mouse_btn;
+    bool full;
+    QEMUTimer *te_timer;
+    bool te_level;
+};
+
+static void co_reset_window(Co5300State *s)
+{
+    s->cx = s->xs;
+    s->cy = s->ys;
+}
+
+static void co_put_pixel(Co5300State *s, uint16_t px)
+{
+    int x = s->cx, y = s->cy;
+    if (s->madctl & 0x20) { int t = x; x = y; y = t; }
+    if (s->madctl & 0x40) { x = MEM_W - 1 - x; }
+    if (s->madctl & 0x80) { y = MEM_H - 1 - y; }
+    if (x >= 0 && x < MEM_W && y >= 0 && y < MEM_H) {
+        s->mem[y * MEM_W + x] = px;
+    }
+    s->pixels_written++;
+    if (++s->cx > s->xe) {
+        s->cx = s->xs;
+        if (++s->cy > s->ye) {
+            s->cy = s->ys;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void co_param(Co5300State *s, uint8_t b)
+{
+    if (s->pidx < sizeof(s->params)) {
+        s->params[s->pidx] = b;
+    }
+    s->pidx++;
+    switch (s->cmd) {
+    case 0x2A:
+        if (s->pidx == 4) {
+            s->xs = (s->params[0] << 8) | s->params[1];
+            s->xe = (s->params[2] << 8) | s->params[3];
+        }
+        break;
+    case 0x2B:
+        if (s->pidx == 4) {
+            s->ys = (s->params[0] << 8) | s->params[1];
+            s->ye = (s->params[2] << 8) | s->params[3];
+        }
+        break;
+    case 0x36: s->madctl = b; break;
+    case 0x3A: s->colmod = b; break;
+    case 0x51: s->bright = b; s->dirty = true; break;
+    default: break;
+    }
+}
+
+static void co_command(Co5300State *s, uint8_t cmd)
+{
+    s->cmd = cmd;
+    s->pidx = 0;
+    switch (cmd) {
+    case 0x28: s->disp_on = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+    case 0x29: s->disp_on = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+    case 0x10: s->sleep = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+    case 0x11: s->sleep = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+    case 0x20: s->invert = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+    case 0x21: s->invert = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+    case 0x01: /* SWRESET */
+        s->disp_on = false; s->sleep = true; s->bright = 0; s->madctl = 0;
+        break;
+    default: break;
+    }
+}
+
+static int co_trace = -1;
+
+void co5300_cs(Co5300State *s, int level)
+{
+    if (co_trace < 0) {
+        co_trace = getenv("M5SW_LCD_TRACE") != NULL;
+    }
+    if (co_trace && level && s->selected) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[co5300] frame op=%02x cmd=%02x bytes=%u params=%u win=%u,%u-%u,%u pos=%u,%u half=%d\n",
+                s->hdr[0], s->hdr[2], s->nbytes, s->pidx, s->xs, s->ys, s->xe, s->ye, s->cx, s->cy, s->pix_half);
+    }
+    if (!level) {
+        s->selected = true;
+        s->nbytes = 0;
+        s->pixel_mode = false;
+        s->pix_half = false;
+        s->cmd = 0;
+        s->pidx = 0;
+    } else {
+        s->selected = false;
+    }
+}
+
+void co5300_write(Co5300State *s, const uint8_t *buf, uint32_t len)
+{
+    /*
+     * IDF's 32-bit command encoding uses [opcode, 0, command, 0] and the
+     * QSPI device CS is controlled by the controller rather than the GPIO
+     * model.  Treat a complete four-byte opcode header as a transaction
+     * boundary so a preceding RAMWR burst cannot swallow the next command
+     * as pixel data.
+     */
+    if (len == 4 && buf[1] == 0 && buf[3] == 0
+        && (buf[0] == 0x02 || buf[0] == 0x12 || buf[0] == 0x32)) {
+        s->nbytes = 0;
+        s->pixel_mode = false;
+        s->pix_half = false;
+        s->cmd = 0;
+        s->pidx = 0;
+    }
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
+        uint8_t b = buf[i];
+        if (s->nbytes < 4) {
+            s->hdr[s->nbytes++] = b;
+            if (s->nbytes == 4) {
+                uint8_t op = s->hdr[0], c = s->hdr[2];
+                /*
+                 * M5GFX releases differ in where the 8-bit CO5300 command
+                 * sits inside the QSPI address phase: the validated UIFlow2
+                 * path sends [op, 00, cmd, 00], while MotoNav sends
+                 * [op, cmd, 00, 00].  Accept both wire forms.
+                 */
+                if (!c) {
+                    c = s->hdr[1];
+                }
+                if (op == 0x02) {
+                    co_command(s, c);
+                } else if (op == 0x32 || op == 0x12) {
+                    if (c == 0x2C) {
+                        co_reset_window(s);
+                        s->pixel_mode = true;
+                    } else if (c == 0x3C) {
+                        s->pixel_mode = true;
+                    } else {
+                        s->pixel_mode = false;
+                    }
+                    s->pix_half = false;
+                    s->cmd = 0;
+                }
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        s->nbytes++;
+        if (s->pixel_mode) {
+            if (!s->pix_half) {
+                s->pix_hi = b;
+                s->pix_half = true;
+            } else {
+                co_put_pixel(s, (s->pix_hi << 8) | b);
+                s->pix_half = false;
+                s->dirty = true;
+            }
+        } else if (s->hdr[0] == 0x02) {
+            co_param(s, b);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void co_cs_cb(void *opaque, int pin, int level)
+{
+    co5300_cs(opaque, level);
+}
+
+static void co_update(void *opaque)
+{
+    Co5300State *s = opaque;
+    DisplaySurface *surf = qemu_console_surface(s->con);
+    int want_w = s->full ? MEM_W : VIS_W, want_h = s->full ? MEM_H : VIS_H;
+    if (!surf || surface_bits_per_pixel(surf) != 32 ||
+        surface_width(surf) != want_w || surface_height(surf) != want_h) {
+        surf = qemu_create_displaysurface(want_w, want_h);
+        dpy_gfx_replace_surface(s->con, surf);
+        s->dirty = true;
+    }
+    if (!s->dirty) {
+        return;
+    }
+    s->dirty = false;
+    uint32_t *dst = (uint32_t *)surface_data(surf);
+    int stride = surface_stride(surf) / 4;
+    bool on = s->disp_on && !s->sleep;
+    uint32_t br = s->bright;
+    int vw = want_w, vh = want_h;
+    int x0 = s->full ? 0 : VIS_X0, y0 = s->full ? 0 : VIS_Y0;
+    /*
+     * The CO5300 controller has a square 466x466 framebuffer, but the
+     * StopWatch glass is circular.  Mask the corners in the QEMU console
+     * output so screendumps show the same visible area as the real panel.
+     * The memory dump itself remains untouched.
+     */
+    int cx = vw / 2, cy = vh / 2;
+    int radius2 = (vw / 2) * (vw / 2);
+    for (int y = 0; y < vh; y++) {
+        const uint16_t *src = &s->mem[(y + y0) * MEM_W + x0];
+        uint32_t *d = dst + y * stride;
+        for (int x = 0; x < vw; x++) {
+            int dx = x - cx, dy = y - cy;
+            if (!s->full && dx * dx + dy * dy > radius2) {
+                d[x] = 0xff000000u;
+                continue;
+            }
+            uint16_t p = src[x];
+            if (s->invert) {
+                p = ~p;
+            }
+            uint32_t r = ((p >> 11) & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+            uint32_t g = ((p >> 5) & 0x3f) * 255 / 63;
+            uint32_t b = (p & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+            if (!on) {
+                r = g = b = 0;
+            } else {
+                r = r * br / 255; g = g * br / 255; b = b * br / 255;
+            }
+            d[x] = 0xff000000u | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
+        }
+    }
+    dpy_gfx_update(s->con, 0, 0, vw, vh);
+}
+
+static void co_invalidate(void *opaque)
+{
+    ((Co5300State *)opaque)->dirty = true;
+}
+
+static const GraphicHwOps co_ops = {
+    .invalidate = co_invalidate,
+    .gfx_update = co_update,
+};
+
+/* ---- input ---- */
+static void co_input_event(DeviceState *dev, QemuConsole *src, InputEvent *evt)
+{
+    Co5300State *s = CO5300(dev);
+    switch (evt->type) {
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_KEY: {
+        InputKeyEvent *key = evt->u.key.data;
+        int q = qemu_input_key_value_to_qcode(key->key);
+        int down = key->down;
+        switch (q) {
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_A: case Q_KEY_CODE_LEFT: case Q_KEY_CODE_1:
+            esp32_gpio_set_input(PIN_KEYA, !down);
+            break;
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_B: case Q_KEY_CODE_RIGHT: case Q_KEY_CODE_2:
+            esp32_gpio_set_input(PIN_KEYB, !down);
+            break;
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_P: case Q_KEY_CODE_3:
+            m5sw_pwr_button(down);
+            break;
+        default:
+            break;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_ABS: {
+        InputMoveEvent *m = evt->u.abs.data;
+        int v = qemu_input_scale_axis(m->value, INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MIN, INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MAX, 0, VIS_W - 1);
+        if (m->axis == INPUT_AXIS_X) {
+            s->mouse_x = v;
+        } else if (m->axis == INPUT_AXIS_Y) {
+            s->mouse_y = v;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_BTN: {
+        InputBtnEvent *b = evt->u.btn.data;
+        if (b->button == INPUT_BUTTON_LEFT) {
+            s->mouse_btn = b->down;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static void co_input_sync(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    Co5300State *s = CO5300(dev);
+    m5sw_touch_set(s->mouse_btn, s->mouse_x, s->mouse_y);
+}
+
+static const QemuInputHandler co_input_handler = {
+    .name = "M5StopWatch touch+keys",
+    .mask = INPUT_EVENT_MASK_KEY | INPUT_EVENT_MASK_BTN | INPUT_EVENT_MASK_ABS,
+    .event = co_input_event,
+    .sync = co_input_sync,
+};
+
+/* Tearing-effect output: ~60 Hz, short high pulse (TE active high) */
+static void co_te_tick(void *opaque)
+{
+    Co5300State *s = opaque;
+    int64_t now = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL);
+    s->te_level = !s->te_level;
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(PIN_TE, s->te_level);
+    timer_mod(s->te_timer, now + (s->te_level ? 500000 : 16166667));
+}
+
+static void co_realize(DeviceState *dev, Error **errp)
+{
+    Co5300State *s = CO5300(dev);
+    s->con = graphic_console_init(dev, 0, &co_ops, s);
+    s->full = getenv("M5SW_LCD_FULL") != NULL;   /* debug: show whole 480x480 GRAM */
+    qemu_console_resize(s->con, s->full ? MEM_W : VIS_W, s->full ? MEM_H : VIS_H);
+    s->colmod = 0x55;
+    s->sleep = true;
+    s->xe = MEM_W - 1;
+    s->ye = MEM_H - 1;
+    esp32_gpio_add_out_listener(PIN_CS, co_cs_cb, s);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(PIN_TE, 0);
+    s->te_timer = timer_new_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL, co_te_tick, s);
+    timer_mod(s->te_timer, qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + 16666667);
+    QemuInputHandlerState *hs = qemu_input_handler_register(dev, &co_input_handler);
+    qemu_input_handler_activate(hs);
+}
+
+static void co_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    dc->realize = co_realize;
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo co_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_CO5300,
+    .parent = TYPE_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(Co5300State),
+    .class_init = co_class_init,
+};
+
+static void co_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&co_info);
+}
+type_init(co_register)
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_fe.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_fe.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..4068f0e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_fe.c
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+/*
+ * ESP32-S3 front-end (FE) bring-up stub.
+ *
+ * The UIFlow2 bootloader waits for the PHY front-end to signal completion
+ * through FE offset 0x174, bit 16, before it continues with the rest of
+ * the system startup.  The upstream ESP32-S3 machine has no RF/PHY model,
+ * so that bit never changes and the bootloader spins in its polling loop.
+ *
+ * Keep the register file for writes and report the front-end as ready.
+ * This is intentionally a boot-progress stub, not an RF model.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "exec/memory.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define FE_BASE       0x60006000
+#define FE_SIZE       0x1000
+#define FE_STATUS     0x174
+#define FE_READY      (1u << 16)
+
+typedef struct M5StopwatchFeState {
+    uint32_t regs[FE_SIZE / sizeof(uint32_t)];
+} M5StopwatchFeState;
+
+static uint64_t fe_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    M5StopwatchFeState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t value;
+
+    if (addr + size > FE_SIZE || size != 4) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    value = s->regs[addr / 4];
+    if (addr == FE_STATUS) {
+        value |= FE_READY;
+    }
+    return value;
+}
+
+static void fe_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t value64,
+                     unsigned size)
+{
+    M5StopwatchFeState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (addr + size > FE_SIZE || size != 4) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    s->regs[addr / 4] = value64;
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps fe_ops = {
+    .read = fe_read,
+    .write = fe_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 4,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+void m5stopwatch_fe_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem)
+{
+    M5StopwatchFeState *s = g_new0(M5StopwatchFeState, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *mr = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+
+    memory_region_init_io(mr, NULL, &fe_ops, s, "esp32s3.fe", FE_SIZE);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, FE_BASE, mr, 1);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_gpspi.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_gpspi.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..815ce6b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_gpspi.c
@@ -0,0 +1,649 @@
+/*
+ * ESP32-S3 GP-SPI2 master (subset): user-defined transactions with
+ * command/address/MOSI phases, W0..W15 buffer or GDMA out-link as source.
+ * Quad/dual line modes do not change the byte stream, so they are accepted
+ * transparently. Bytes are forwarded to an attached CO5300 panel model.
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/log.h"
+#include "hw/sysbus.h"
+#include "hw/irq.h"
+#include "exec/address-spaces.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define R_CMD      0x00
+#define R_ADDR     0x04
+#define R_USER     0x10
+#define R_USER1    0x14
+#define R_USER2    0x18
+#define R_MS_DLEN  0x1c
+#define R_DMA_CONF 0x30
+#define R_DMA_INT_ENA 0x34
+#define R_DMA_INT_CLR 0x38
+#define R_DMA_INT_RAW 0x3c
+#define R_DMA_INT_ST  0x40
+#define R_DMA_INT_SET 0x44
+#define R_W0       0x98
+#define R_DATE     0xf0
+
+#define CMD_USR      (1u << 24)
+#define USER_MOSI    (1u << 27)
+#define USER_MISO    (1u << 28)
+#define USER_ADDR    (1u << 30)
+#define USER_COMMAND (1u << 31)
+#define DMA_RX_ENA   (1u << 27)
+#define DMA_TX_ENA   (1u << 28)
+#define INT_TRANS_DONE (1u << 12)
+
+#define M5_GPSPI_MAX_SLAVES 4
+
+typedef struct M5GpspiSlave {
+    const M5SpiSlaveOps *ops;
+    void *opaque;
+    int cs_pin;
+    int cs_level;
+    int gdma_periph;
+} M5GpspiSlave;
+
+struct Esp32s3GpspiState {
+    SysBusDevice parent_obj;
+    MemoryRegion iomem;
+    qemu_irq irq;
+    qemu_irq irq_alias;
+    uint32_t regs[0x40];
+    ESPGdmaState *gdma;
+    Co5300State *panel;
+    M5SpiPanelState *spi_panel;
+    M5EpdPanelState *epd_panel;
+    uint32_t gdma_periph;
+    int dc_pin;
+    int panel_cs_pin;
+    int panel_cs_level;
+    M5GpspiSlave slaves[M5_GPSPI_MAX_SLAVES];
+    int slave_count;
+    int active_slave;
+    int last_slave;
+    uint8_t rx[4096];
+    uint32_t rx_len;
+    /* DMA cursor */
+    bool dma_reload;
+    int debug_id;
+    uint32_t desc_addr;
+    uint32_t desc_off;
+    uint8_t *buf;
+    uint32_t buf_size;
+};
+
+static void gpspi_update_irq(Esp32s3GpspiState *s)
+{
+    int level = !!(s->regs[R_DMA_INT_RAW / 4] &
+                   s->regs[R_DMA_INT_ENA / 4]);
+
+    if (getenv("M5SW_GPSPI_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpspi-irq] level=%d raw=%08x ena=%08x\n",
+                level, s->regs[R_DMA_INT_RAW / 4],
+                s->regs[R_DMA_INT_ENA / 4]);
+    }
+    qemu_set_irq(s->irq, level);
+    qemu_set_irq(s->irq_alias, level);
+}
+
+static void sink(Esp32s3GpspiState *s, const uint8_t *b, uint32_t n,
+                 bool command_phase)
+{
+    if (s->dc_pin >= 0) {
+        command_phase = !esp32_gpio_get_line(s->dc_pin);
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE") && n) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[spi-xfer] %c len=%u first=%02x\n",
+                command_phase ? 'C' : 'D', n, b[0]);
+    }
+    if (s->panel && n && s->active_slave < 0 &&
+        (s->panel_cs_pin < 0 || !s->panel_cs_level)) {
+        co5300_write(s->panel, b, n);
+    }
+    if (s->spi_panel && n && s->active_slave < 0 &&
+        (s->panel_cs_pin < 0 || !s->panel_cs_level)) {
+        m5_spi_panel_write(s->spi_panel, b, n, command_phase);
+    }
+    if (s->epd_panel && n && s->active_slave < 0 &&
+        (s->panel_cs_pin < 0 || !s->panel_cs_level)) {
+        m5_epd_panel_write(s->epd_panel, b, n, command_phase);
+    }
+}
+
+static void gpspi_forward_slave(Esp32s3GpspiState *s,
+                                const uint8_t *tx, uint32_t n,
+                                uint8_t *rx, bool capture)
+{
+    M5GpspiSlave *slave;
+
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpspi-forward] active=%d n=%u capture=%d\n",
+                s->active_slave, n, capture);
+    }
+    if (s->active_slave < 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    slave = &s->slaves[s->active_slave];
+    if (!slave->ops || slave->cs_level != 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {
+        uint8_t value = slave->ops->transfer(slave->opaque, tx[i]);
+        if (capture) {
+            rx[i] = value;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static uint32_t gpspi_active_dma_periph(Esp32s3GpspiState *s)
+{
+    if (s->active_slave >= 0 &&
+        s->slaves[s->active_slave].gdma_periph >= 0) {
+        return s->slaves[s->active_slave].gdma_periph;
+    }
+    return s->gdma_periph;
+}
+
+static void gpspi_cs_line(void *opaque, int pin, int level)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = opaque;
+    M5GpspiSlave *slave = NULL;
+    int index;
+
+    for (index = 0; index < s->slave_count; index++) {
+        if (s->slaves[index].cs_pin == pin) {
+            slave = &s->slaves[index];
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+    if (!slave) {
+        if (pin == s->panel_cs_pin) {
+            s->panel_cs_level = level;
+            if (!level) {
+                s->active_slave = -1;
+            }
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpspi-cs] pin=%d level=%d index=%d slave=%p\n",
+                pin, level, index, slave->opaque);
+    }
+    if (level == slave->cs_level) {
+        return;
+    }
+    slave->cs_level = level;
+    s->active_slave = level ? -1 : index;
+    if (slave->ops && slave->ops->set_cs) {
+        slave->ops->set_cs(slave->opaque, level);
+    }
+}
+
+/* Pull n bytes from the GDMA out-link chain bound to SPI2 */
+static uint32_t gpspi_dma_pull(Esp32s3GpspiState *s, uint8_t *dst, uint32_t n)
+{
+    uint32_t got = 0;
+    const uint32_t periph = gpspi_active_dma_periph(s);
+    if (!s->gdma) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (s->dma_reload) {
+        uint32_t ch;
+        if (!esp_gdma_get_channel_periph(s->gdma, periph,
+                                         ESP_GDMA_OUT_IDX, &ch)) {
+            qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR, "gpspi: no GDMA out channel\n");
+            return 0;
+        }
+        uint32_t link = esp_gdma_read_chan_register(s->gdma, ESP_GDMA_OUT_IDX, ch, GDMA_LINK_REG);
+        s->desc_addr = ((ESP_GDMA_RAM_ADDR >> 20) << 20) | (link & 0xFFFFF);
+        s->desc_off = 0;
+        s->dma_reload = false;
+    }
+    while (got < n && s->desc_addr) {
+        uint32_t d[3];
+        if (address_space_read(&address_space_memory, s->desc_addr, MEMTXATTRS_UNSPECIFIED,
+                               d, sizeof(d)) != MEMTX_OK) {
+            break;
+        }
+        uint32_t len = (d[0] >> 12) & 0xfff;
+        bool eof = (d[0] >> 30) & 1;
+        uint32_t avail = len > s->desc_off ? len - s->desc_off : 0;
+        uint32_t take = MIN(avail, n - got);
+        if (take) {
+            address_space_read(&address_space_memory, d[1] + s->desc_off,
+                               MEMTXATTRS_UNSPECIFIED, dst + got, take);
+            got += take;
+            s->desc_off += take;
+        }
+        if (s->desc_off >= len) {
+            s->desc_off = 0;
+            s->desc_addr = eof ? 0 : d[2];
+        }
+    }
+    return got;
+}
+
+static void gpspi_exec(Esp32s3GpspiState *s)
+{
+    uint32_t user = s->regs[R_USER / 4];
+    uint8_t tmp[8];
+    uint8_t xfer[4096];
+    uint32_t xfer_len = 0;
+    if (s->active_slave < 0) {
+        s->last_slave = -1;
+    }
+    memset(xfer, 0xff, sizeof(xfer));
+    if (user & USER_COMMAND) {
+        uint32_t bits = ((s->regs[R_USER2 / 4] >> 28) & 0xf) + 1;
+        uint32_t v = s->regs[R_USER2 / 4] & 0xffff;
+        uint32_t nb = (bits + 7) / 8;
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < nb; i++) {
+            tmp[i] = v >> (8 * i);
+        }
+        sink(s, tmp, nb, true);
+        gpspi_forward_slave(s, tmp, nb, NULL, false);
+    }
+    if (user & USER_ADDR) {
+        uint32_t bits = ((s->regs[R_USER1 / 4] >> 27) & 0x1f) + 1;
+        uint32_t v = s->regs[R_ADDR / 4];
+        uint32_t nb = (bits + 7) / 8;
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < nb; i++) {
+            tmp[i] = v >> (8 * (nb - 1 - i));
+        }
+        sink(s, tmp, nb, false);
+        gpspi_forward_slave(s, tmp, nb, NULL, false);
+    }
+    if (user & USER_MOSI) {
+        uint32_t bits = (s->regs[R_MS_DLEN / 4] & 0x3ffff) + 1;
+        uint32_t nb = (bits + 7) / 8;
+        if (s->regs[R_DMA_CONF / 4] & DMA_TX_ENA) {
+            /* Each user transaction starts from the DMA link head again. */
+            s->dma_reload = true;
+            if (nb > s->buf_size) {
+                s->buf = g_realloc(s->buf, nb);
+                s->buf_size = nb;
+            }
+            uint32_t got = gpspi_dma_pull(s, s->buf, nb);
+            xfer_len = MIN(got, sizeof(xfer));
+            memcpy(xfer, s->buf, xfer_len);
+            sink(s, s->buf, got, false);
+        } else {
+            for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
+                uint32_t v = s->regs[R_W0 / 4 + i];
+                xfer[i * 4 + 0] = v; xfer[i * 4 + 1] = v >> 8;
+                xfer[i * 4 + 2] = v >> 16; xfer[i * 4 + 3] = v >> 24;
+            }
+            xfer_len = MIN(nb, sizeof(xfer));
+            sink(s, xfer, xfer_len, false);
+        }
+    }
+    if (user & USER_MISO) {
+        uint32_t bits = (s->regs[R_MS_DLEN / 4] & 0x3ffff) + 1;
+        uint32_t nb = (bits + 7) / 8;
+        uint32_t copy = MIN(nb, sizeof(xfer));
+
+        memset(s->rx, 0, sizeof(s->rx));
+        if (s->active_slave >= 0) {
+            s->last_slave = s->active_slave;
+            gpspi_forward_slave(s, xfer, copy, s->rx, true);
+            s->rx_len = copy;
+            bool rx_dma = s->regs[R_DMA_CONF / 4] & DMA_RX_ENA;
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[spi-dma] conf=%08x gdma=%p rx_dma=%d\n",
+                        s->regs[R_DMA_CONF / 4], (void *)s->gdma, rx_dma);
+            }
+            if (s->gdma && rx_dma) {
+                uint32_t ch;
+                const uint32_t periph = gpspi_active_dma_periph(s);
+                if (esp_gdma_get_channel_periph(s->gdma, periph,
+                                                ESP_GDMA_IN_IDX, &ch)) {
+                    bool ok = esp_gdma_write_channel(s->gdma, ch,
+                                                     s->rx, copy);
+                    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE")) {
+                        fprintf(stderr,
+                                "[spi-dma-rx] ch=%u len=%u ok=%d\n",
+                                ch, copy, ok);
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE")) {
+                        fprintf(stderr, "[spi-dma-rx] no in channel\n");
+                    }
+                    qemu_log_mask(LOG_GUEST_ERROR,
+                                  "gpspi: no GDMA in channel\n");
+                }
+            }
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE") && copy) {
+                fprintf(stderr,
+                        "[spi-rx] len=%u first=%02x last=%02x"
+                        " tx0=%02x tx1=%02x tx2=%02x tx3=%02x\n",
+                        copy, s->rx[0], s->rx[copy - 1],
+                        xfer[0], xfer[1], xfer[2], xfer[3]);
+            }
+        }
+    } else if (user & USER_MOSI) {
+        gpspi_forward_slave(s, xfer, xfer_len, NULL, false);
+    }
+    s->regs[R_DMA_INT_RAW / 4] |= INT_TRANS_DONE;
+    gpspi_update_irq(s);
+}
+
+static uint64_t gpspi_read_reg(Esp32s3GpspiState *s, hwaddr addr)
+{
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE") && s->last_slave >= 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpspi-mmio] rd %03x\n", (unsigned)addr);
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_GPSPI_POLL_TRACE") &&
+        (addr == R_CMD || addr == R_DMA_INT_RAW || addr == R_DMA_INT_ST)) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpspi-poll] id=%d rd %03x\n",
+                s->debug_id, (unsigned)addr);
+    }
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_CMD:
+        return s->regs[0] & ~CMD_USR;
+    case R_DMA_INT_ST:
+        return s->regs[R_DMA_INT_RAW / 4] & s->regs[R_DMA_INT_ENA / 4];
+    case R_DATE:
+        return 0x2101190;
+    default:
+        if (s->last_slave >= 0 && addr >= R_W0 && addr < R_W0 + 0x40
+            && s->rx_len) {
+            uint32_t offset = addr - R_W0;
+            uint32_t value = 0;
+            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < 4 && offset + i < s->rx_len; i++) {
+                value |= (uint32_t)s->rx[offset + i] << (8 * i);
+            }
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[spi-rx] read W0+%02x=%08x\n",
+                        offset, value);
+            }
+            return value;
+        }
+        /*
+         * A direct SPI panel must only answer while its CS pin is asserted.
+         * Returning the panel ID for every W0..W15 read makes unrelated
+         * M5GFX autodetect probes observe the same ST7789 ID and select the
+         * wrong board (for example Cardputer becomes DinMeter).
+         */
+        if (s->spi_panel
+            && (s->panel_cs_pin < 0 || !s->panel_cs_level)
+            && addr >= R_W0 && addr < R_W0 + 0x40) {
+            return m5_spi_panel_read_id(s->spi_panel);
+        }
+        return addr < 0x100 ? s->regs[addr / 4] : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint64_t gpspi_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = opaque;
+    uint64_t value = gpspi_read_reg(s, addr & ~3ULL);
+    unsigned shift = (addr & 3) * 8;
+    unsigned bits = size * 8;
+
+    value >>= shift;
+    if (bits < 64) {
+        value &= MAKE_64BIT_MASK(0, bits);
+    }
+    return value;
+}
+
+static void gpspi_write_reg(Esp32s3GpspiState *s, hwaddr addr, uint64_t val)
+{
+    if (getenv("M5SW_GPSPI_TRACE") &&
+        (addr == R_CMD || addr == R_USER || addr == R_USER1 ||
+         addr == R_USER2 || addr == R_MS_DLEN || addr == R_DMA_CONF ||
+         addr == R_DMA_INT_ENA || addr == R_DMA_INT_CLR ||
+         addr == R_DMA_INT_RAW || addr == R_DMA_INT_SET)) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpspi-trace] write %03x=%08x\n",
+                (unsigned)addr, (unsigned)val);
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE") && s->slave_count
+        && (s->active_slave >= 0 || addr == R_CMD || addr == R_DMA_CONF
+            || addr == R_MS_DLEN || addr == R_USER
+            || addr == R_USER1 || addr == R_USER2)) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[gpspi-mmio] wr %03x <- %08x\n",
+                (unsigned)addr, (unsigned)val);
+    }
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_CMD:
+        s->regs[0] = val & ~(CMD_USR | (1u << 23));
+        if (val & CMD_USR) {
+            gpspi_exec(s);
+        }
+        break;
+    case R_DMA_CONF:
+        if ((val & DMA_TX_ENA) && !(s->regs[R_DMA_CONF / 4] & DMA_TX_ENA)) {
+            s->dma_reload = true;
+        }
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = val;
+        break;
+    case R_DMA_INT_CLR:
+        s->regs[R_DMA_INT_RAW / 4] &= ~val;
+        gpspi_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    case R_DMA_INT_SET:
+        s->regs[R_DMA_INT_RAW / 4] |= val;
+        gpspi_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    case R_DMA_INT_ENA:
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = val;
+        gpspi_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    default:
+        if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE") && s->slave_count &&
+            addr >= R_W0 && addr < R_W0 + 0x40 && val != 0xffffffff) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[spi-tx] W0+%02x <- %08x\n",
+                    (unsigned)(addr - R_W0), (unsigned)val);
+        }
+        if (addr < 0x100) {
+            s->regs[addr / 4] = val;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static void gpspi_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t val, unsigned size)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = opaque;
+    hwaddr aligned = addr & ~3ULL;
+    unsigned shift = (addr & 3) * 8;
+    unsigned bits = size * 8;
+
+    if (size != 4 || aligned != addr) {
+        uint64_t merged = gpspi_read_reg(s, aligned);
+        uint64_t mask = MAKE_64BIT_MASK(shift, bits);
+
+        merged = (merged & ~mask) | ((val << shift) & mask);
+        val = merged;
+        addr = aligned;
+    }
+    gpspi_write_reg(s, addr, val);
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps gpspi_ops = {
+    .read = gpspi_read,
+    .write = gpspi_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 1,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 1,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+static void gpspi_init(Object *obj)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = ESP32S3_GPSPI(obj);
+    static int next_debug_id;
+    memory_region_init_io(&s->iomem, obj, &gpspi_ops, s, TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI, 0x1000);
+    sysbus_init_mmio(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj), &s->iomem);
+    sysbus_init_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj), &s->irq);
+    sysbus_init_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj), &s->irq_alias);
+    s->active_slave = -1;
+    s->last_slave = -1;
+    s->panel_cs_pin = -1;
+    s->panel_cs_level = 1;
+    s->debug_id = next_debug_id++;
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo gpspi_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI,
+    .parent = TYPE_SYS_BUS_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(Esp32s3GpspiState),
+    .instance_init = gpspi_init,
+};
+
+static void gpspi_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&gpspi_info);
+}
+type_init(gpspi_register)
+
+void esp32s3_gpspi_attach(DeviceState *dev, ESPGdmaState *gdma,
+                          Co5300State *co, M5SpiPanelState *spi,
+                          uint32_t gdma_periph)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = ESP32S3_GPSPI(dev);
+    s->gdma = gdma;
+    s->panel = co;
+    s->spi_panel = spi;
+    s->epd_panel = NULL;
+    s->gdma_periph = gdma_periph;
+    s->dc_pin = -1;
+}
+
+void esp32s3_gpspi_attach_epd(DeviceState *dev, ESPGdmaState *gdma,
+                              M5EpdPanelState *panel, uint32_t gdma_periph)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = ESP32S3_GPSPI(dev);
+    s->gdma = gdma;
+    s->panel = NULL;
+    s->spi_panel = NULL;
+    s->epd_panel = panel;
+    s->gdma_periph = gdma_periph;
+    s->dc_pin = -1;
+}
+
+void esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(DeviceState *dev, int pin)
+{
+    ESP32S3_GPSPI(dev)->dc_pin = pin;
+}
+
+void esp32s3_gpspi_set_panel_cs_pin(DeviceState *dev, int pin)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = ESP32S3_GPSPI(dev);
+
+    s->panel_cs_pin = pin;
+    s->panel_cs_level = 1;
+    if (pin >= 0) {
+        esp32_gpio_add_out_listener(pin, gpspi_cs_line, s);
+    }
+}
+
+void esp32s3_gpspi_attach_spi_slave(DeviceState *dev,
+                                    const M5SpiSlaveOps *ops,
+                                    void *opaque, int cs_pin)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = ESP32S3_GPSPI(dev);
+    int index = s->slave_count++;
+
+    assert(index < M5_GPSPI_MAX_SLAVES);
+    s->slaves[index].ops = ops;
+    s->slaves[index].opaque = opaque;
+    s->slaves[index].cs_pin = cs_pin;
+    s->slaves[index].cs_level = 1;
+    s->slaves[index].gdma_periph = -1;
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(cs_pin, 1);
+    esp32_gpio_add_out_listener(cs_pin, gpspi_cs_line, s);
+}
+
+void esp32s3_gpspi_set_spi_slave_dma(DeviceState *dev, int cs_pin,
+                                     uint32_t gdma_periph)
+{
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *s = ESP32S3_GPSPI(dev);
+
+    for (int index = 0; index < s->slave_count; index++) {
+        if (s->slaves[index].cs_pin == cs_pin) {
+            s->slaves[index].gdma_periph = gdma_periph;
+            return;
+        }
+    }
+    assert(0 && "GP-SPI slave CS is not attached");
+}
+
+/* ---- board glue ---- */
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+
+void esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(DeviceState *dev, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                                int irq_a, int irq_b)
+{
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, irq_a));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 1,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, irq_b));
+}
+
+void m5stopwatch_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                            ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    /* I2C0 @ 0x60013000 with the board's sensors/PMIC/expander */
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000,
+        i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    /*
+     * I2C1 @ 0x60027000. M5Unified uses port 1 for the on-board bus,
+     * while other firmwares (including the factory UserDemo) use port 0.
+     * Give each port its own register file and interrupt source, but let
+     * both controllers address the same device bus.
+     */
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000,
+        i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "m5pm1", 0x6E }, { "m5ioe1", 0x4F }, { "cst820", 0x15 },
+        { "bmi270", 0x68 }, { "rx8130", 0x32 }, { "es8311", 0x18 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        /* stable QOM paths for QMP: /machine/m5pm1, /machine/bmi270, ... */
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init(bus);
+
+    /* GP-SPI2 @ 0x60024000 driving the CO5300 AMOLED */
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    Esp32s3GpspiState *ss = ESP32S3_GPSPI(spi);
+    ss->gdma = gdma;
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0), 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE));
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_CO5300);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    ss->panel = CO5300(panel);
+}

+/*
+ * ESP32-S3 I2C master controller (subset sufficient for ESP-IDF i2c_master driver)
+ * Part of the M5Stack StopWatch board support.
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/log.h"
+#include "qemu/fifo8.h"
+#include "hw/sysbus.h"
+#include "hw/irq.h"
+#include "hw/i2c/i2c.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define I2C_FIFO_LEN 32
+
+#define R_CTR        0x04
+#define R_SR         0x08
+#define R_FIFO_ST    0x14
+#define R_FIFO_CONF  0x18
+#define R_DATA       0x1c
+#define R_INT_RAW    0x20
+#define R_INT_CLR    0x24
+#define R_INT_ENA    0x28
+#define R_INT_ST     0x2c
+#define R_COMD0      0x58
+#define R_DATE       0xf8
+#define R_TXFIFO_MEM 0x100
+#define R_RXFIFO_MEM 0x180
+
+#define CTR_TRANS_START (1u << 5)
+#define CTR_FSM_RST     (1u << 10)
+#define CTR_CONF_UPGATE (1u << 11)
+#define FIFO_CONF_RX_RST (1u << 12)
+#define FIFO_CONF_TX_RST (1u << 13)
+
+#define INT_RXFIFO_WM     (1u << 0)
+#define INT_TXFIFO_WM     (1u << 1)
+#define INT_END_DETECT    (1u << 3)
+#define INT_BYTE_DONE     (1u << 4)
+#define INT_TRANS_COMPLETE (1u << 7)
+#define INT_TRANS_START   (1u << 9)
+#define INT_NACK          (1u << 10)
+
+#define OP_WRITE   1
+#define OP_STOP    2
+#define OP_READ    3
+#define OP_END     4
+#define OP_RSTART  6
+
+struct Esp32s3I2CState {
+    SysBusDevice parent_obj;
+    MemoryRegion iomem;
+    qemu_irq irq;
+    I2CBus *bus;
+    uint32_t regs[0x40];      /* plain storage for unspecified regs (0x00..0xff) */
+    uint32_t cmd[8];
+    uint32_t int_raw, int_ena;
+    uint8_t txfifo[I2C_FIFO_LEN];
+    uint32_t tx_rd, tx_wr;     /* tx fifo is linear: refilled after each start */
+    uint8_t rxfifo[I2C_FIFO_LEN];
+    uint32_t rx_rd, rx_wr;
+    bool need_addr;           /* next written byte is an address byte */
+    bool in_xfer;             /* i2c_start_transfer succeeded and not ended */
+    bool nacked;
+};
+
+static void s3i2c_update_irq(Esp32s3I2CState *s)
+{
+    qemu_set_irq(s->irq, !!(s->int_raw & s->int_ena));
+}
+
+static uint32_t rx_cnt(Esp32s3I2CState *s) { return s->rx_wr - s->rx_rd; }
+static uint32_t tx_cnt(Esp32s3I2CState *s) { return s->tx_wr - s->tx_rd; }
+
+static void s3i2c_end_xfer(Esp32s3I2CState *s)
+{
+    if (s->in_xfer) {
+        i2c_end_transfer(s->bus);
+        s->in_xfer = false;
+    }
+    s->need_addr = true;
+}
+
+static void s3i2c_run(Esp32s3I2CState *s)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_set_hardware_active();
+    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
+        uint32_t c = s->cmd[i];
+        int op = (c >> 11) & 7;
+        int n = c & 0xff;
+        bool ack_check = (c >> 8) & 1;
+        switch (op) {
+        case OP_RSTART:
+            if (s->in_xfer) {
+                /*
+                 * QEMU has no distinct repeated-start callback.  A FINISH
+                 * followed by a fresh START keeps the slave register pointer
+                 * intact while clearing any stale bus ownership from a
+                 * preceding NACK/read transaction.
+                 */
+                i2c_end_transfer(s->bus);
+                s->in_xfer = false;
+            }
+            s->need_addr = true;
+            s->nacked = false;
+            break;
+        case OP_WRITE:
+            for (int k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+                uint8_t b = 0xff;
+                if (tx_cnt(s)) {
+                    b = s->txfifo[s->tx_rd++ % I2C_FIFO_LEN];
+                }
+                if (getenv("M5SW_I2C_CTRL_TRACE")) {
+                    fprintf(stderr, "[i2c-ctrl] write[%d/%d]=%02x need_addr=%d tx=%u/%u\n",
+                            k, n, b, s->need_addr, s->tx_rd, s->tx_wr);
+                }
+                if (s->need_addr) {
+                    s->need_addr = false;
+                    bool recv = b & 1;
+                    if (getenv("M5SW_I2C_CTRL_TRACE")) {
+                        fprintf(stderr, "[i2c-ctrl] addr-byte=%02x recv=%d\n",
+                                b, recv);
+                    }
+                    if (i2c_start_transfer(s->bus, b >> 1, recv) != 0) {
+                        s->in_xfer = false;
+                        s->nacked = true;
+                    } else {
+                        s->in_xfer = true;
+                    }
+                } else if (s->in_xfer) {
+                    if (i2c_send(s->bus, b) != 0) {
+                        s->nacked = true;
+                    }
+                }
+                if (s->nacked && ack_check) {
+                    s->cmd[i] |= 1u << 31;
+                    s->int_raw |= INT_NACK;
+                    s3i2c_end_xfer(s);
+                    s->tx_rd = s->tx_wr = 0;
+                    /*
+                     * Keep processing the remaining command words.  ESP-IDF
+                     * programs a trailing END/STOP after a checked write; on
+                     * a missing device the controller still completes that
+                     * command list instead of stranding it after NACK.
+                     */
+                    break;
+                }
+            }
+            break;
+        case OP_READ:
+            for (int k = 0; k < n; k++) {
+                uint8_t b = s->in_xfer ? i2c_recv(s->bus) : 0xff;
+                if (rx_cnt(s) < I2C_FIFO_LEN) {
+                    s->rxfifo[s->rx_wr++ % I2C_FIFO_LEN] = b;
+                }
+            }
+            break;
+        case OP_STOP:
+            s->cmd[i] |= 1u << 31;
+            s3i2c_end_xfer(s);
+            s->int_raw |= INT_TRANS_COMPLETE;
+            s->tx_rd = s->tx_wr = 0;
+            return;
+        case OP_END:
+            s->cmd[i] |= 1u << 31;
+            s->int_raw |= INT_END_DETECT;
+            /* hardware continues to own the bus; tx fifo is refilled by sw */
+            s->tx_rd = s->tx_wr = 0;
+            return;
+        default:
+            break;
+        }
+        s->cmd[i] |= 1u << 31;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint64_t s3i2c_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    Esp32s3I2CState *s = opaque;
+    if (getenv("M5SW_I2C_CTRL_TRACE")) {
+        char *path = object_get_canonical_path(OBJECT(s));
+        fprintf(stderr, "[i2c-ctrl] %s rd %03" HWADDR_PRIx " size=%u\n",
+                path, addr, size);
+        g_free(path);
+    }
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_SR:
+        return ((rx_cnt(s) & 0x3f) << 8) | ((tx_cnt(s) & 0x3f) << 18);
+    case R_DATA:
+        if (rx_cnt(s)) {
+            return s->rxfifo[s->rx_rd++ % I2C_FIFO_LEN];
+        }
+        return 0;
+    case R_INT_RAW:
+        return s->int_raw;
+    case R_INT_ENA:
+        return s->int_ena;
+    case R_INT_ST:
+        return s->int_raw & s->int_ena;
+    case R_COMD0 ... R_COMD0 + 0x1c:
+        return s->cmd[(addr - R_COMD0) / 4];
+    case R_DATE:
+        return 0x20070201;
+    case R_TXFIFO_MEM ... R_TXFIFO_MEM + 0x7f:
+        /*
+         * The FIFO RAM is byte-addressed even though ESP-IDF normally accesses
+         * it with 32-bit loads/stores.  Return a little-endian word so a driver
+         * can move up to four queued bytes per access.
+         */
+        {
+            hwaddr off = addr - R_TXFIFO_MEM;
+            uint32_t value = 0;
+            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4 && off + i < I2C_FIFO_LEN; i++) {
+                value |= (uint32_t)s->txfifo[off + i] << (i * 8);
+            }
+            return value;
+        }
+    case R_RXFIFO_MEM ... R_RXFIFO_MEM + 0x7f:
+        {
+            hwaddr off = addr - R_RXFIFO_MEM;
+            uint32_t value = 0;
+            for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4 && off + i < s->rx_wr; i++) {
+                value |= (uint32_t)s->rxfifo[off + i] << (i * 8);
+            }
+            return value;
+        }
+    default:
+        if (addr < 0x100) {
+            return s->regs[addr / 4];
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static void s3i2c_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t val, unsigned size)
+{
+    Esp32s3I2CState *s = opaque;
+    if (getenv("M5SW_I2C_CTRL_TRACE")) {
+        char *path = object_get_canonical_path(OBJECT(s));
+        fprintf(stderr,
+                "[i2c-ctrl] %s wr %03" HWADDR_PRIx
+                "=%08" PRIx64 " size=%u\n",
+                path, addr, val, size);
+        g_free(path);
+    }
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_CTR:
+        if (val & CTR_FSM_RST) {
+            s3i2c_end_xfer(s);
+        }
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = val & ~(CTR_TRANS_START | CTR_CONF_UPGATE | CTR_FSM_RST);
+        if (val & CTR_TRANS_START) {
+            if (getenv("M5SW_I2C_CTRL_TRACE")) {
+                char *path = object_get_canonical_path(OBJECT(s));
+                fprintf(stderr,
+                        "[i2c-ctrl] %s start cmd=%08x,%08x,%08x,%08x\n",
+                        path, s->cmd[0], s->cmd[1], s->cmd[2], s->cmd[3]);
+                g_free(path);
+            }
+            s3i2c_run(s);
+            s3i2c_update_irq(s);
+        }
+        break;
+    case R_FIFO_CONF:
+        if (val & FIFO_CONF_RX_RST) {
+            s->rx_rd = s->rx_wr = 0;
+        }
+        if (val & FIFO_CONF_TX_RST) {
+            s->tx_rd = s->tx_wr = 0;
+        }
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = val;
+        break;
+    case R_DATA:
+        if (tx_cnt(s) < I2C_FIFO_LEN) {
+            s->txfifo[s->tx_wr++ % I2C_FIFO_LEN] = val & 0xff;
+        }
+        break;
+    case R_INT_CLR:
+        s->int_raw &= ~val;
+        s3i2c_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    case R_INT_ENA:
+        s->int_ena = val;
+        s3i2c_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    case R_COMD0 ... R_COMD0 + 0x1c:
+        s->cmd[(addr - R_COMD0) / 4] = val;
+        break;
+    case R_TXFIFO_MEM ... R_TXFIFO_MEM + 0x7f: {
+        hwaddr off = addr - R_TXFIFO_MEM;
+        if (getenv("M5SW_I2C_CTRL_TRACE")) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[i2c-ctrl] txfifo[%03lx]=%08lx\n",
+                    (unsigned long)off, (unsigned long)val);
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4 && off + i < I2C_FIFO_LEN; i++) {
+            s->txfifo[off + i] = (val >> (i * 8)) & 0xff;
+            if (off + i + 1 > s->tx_wr) {
+                s->tx_wr = off + i + 1;
+            }
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    default:
+        if (addr < 0x100) {
+            s->regs[addr / 4] = val;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps s3i2c_ops = {
+    .read = s3i2c_read,
+    .write = s3i2c_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 1,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+static void s3i2c_reset(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    Esp32s3I2CState *s = ESP32S3_I2C(dev);
+    memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+    memset(s->cmd, 0, sizeof(s->cmd));
+    s->int_raw = s->int_ena = 0;
+    s->tx_rd = s->tx_wr = s->rx_rd = s->rx_wr = 0;
+    s->need_addr = true;
+    s->in_xfer = false;
+}
+
+static void s3i2c_init(Object *obj)
+{
+    Esp32s3I2CState *s = ESP32S3_I2C(obj);
+    SysBusDevice *sbd = SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj);
+    memory_region_init_io(&s->iomem, obj, &s3i2c_ops, s, TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C, 0x200);
+    sysbus_init_mmio(sbd, &s->iomem);
+    sysbus_init_irq(sbd, &s->irq);
+    s->bus = i2c_init_bus(DEVICE(obj), "i2c");
+}
+
+void esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(DeviceState *dev, I2CBus *bus)
+{
+    ESP32S3_I2C(dev)->bus = bus;
+}
+
+static void s3i2c_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    device_class_set_legacy_reset(dc, s3i2c_reset);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo s3i2c_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C,
+    .parent = TYPE_SYS_BUS_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(Esp32s3I2CState),
+    .instance_init = s3i2c_init,
+    .class_init = s3i2c_class_init,
+};
+
+static void s3i2c_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&s3i2c_info);
+}
+type_init(s3i2c_register)
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_phy.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_phy.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ea5a0b5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_phy.c
@@ -0,0 +1,101 @@
+/*
+ * ESP32-S3 internal PHY/modem register-window stub.
+ *
+ * The UIFlow2 boot ROM's PHY setup touches the undocumented internal
+ * window at 0x6002f000..0x60037fff.  One of its ready handshakes is the
+ * bit-0 poll of 0x60033d14; without a model that bit stays zero and the
+ * bootloader never reaches the application.
+ *
+ * This is a bring-up register-file stub, not an RF or PHY simulation.
+ * It preserves writes and reports the specific ready bit at 0x60033d14.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "exec/memory.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define PHY_BASE       0x6002f000
+#define PHY_SIZE       0x9000
+#define PHY_READY_REG  0x4d14
+#define PHY_READY      (1u << 0)
+#define PHY_COUNTER_REG 0x607c
+#define PHY_RWIP_CTRL_REG 0x2000
+#define PHY_RWIP_BUSY (1u << 31)
+#define PHY_RWIP_ID_REG 0x2004
+#define PHY_RWIP_ID 0x09001b00U
+#define PHY_RWIP_TIME_REG 0x201c
+#define PHY_RWIP_TIME_MASK 0x0fffffffU
+#define PHY_RWIP_HALF_SLOT_NS 312500ULL
+
+typedef struct M5StopwatchPhyState {
+    uint32_t regs[PHY_SIZE / sizeof(uint32_t)];
+    uint32_t counter;
+} M5StopwatchPhyState;
+
+static uint64_t phy_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    M5StopwatchPhyState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t value;
+
+    if (addr + size > PHY_SIZE || size != 4) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    value = s->regs[addr / 4];
+    if (addr == PHY_READY_REG) {
+        value |= PHY_READY;
+    } else if (addr == PHY_COUNTER_REG) {
+        value = s->counter++;
+    } else if (addr == PHY_RWIP_CTRL_REG) {
+        /*
+         * Firmware starts an RWIP control transfer by setting bit 31 and
+         * polls until the baseband clears it.  The transfer completes
+         * synchronously in this model while the low 31 bits retain state.
+         */
+        value &= ~PHY_RWIP_BUSY;
+    } else if (addr == PHY_RWIP_ID_REG) {
+        value = PHY_RWIP_ID;
+    } else if (addr == PHY_RWIP_TIME_REG) {
+        /*
+         * r_rwip_time_get() starts a sample by setting bit 31 and polls
+         * until the hardware returns the current 28-bit RWIP time with the
+         * busy bit cleared.  BLE time advances in 312.5 us half-slots.
+         */
+        value = (qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) /
+                 PHY_RWIP_HALF_SLOT_NS) & PHY_RWIP_TIME_MASK;
+    }
+    return value;
+}
+
+static void phy_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t value64,
+                      unsigned size)
+{
+    M5StopwatchPhyState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (addr + size > PHY_SIZE || size != 4) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    s->regs[addr / 4] = value64;
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps phy_ops = {
+    .read = phy_read,
+    .write = phy_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 4,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+void m5stopwatch_phy_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem)
+{
+    M5StopwatchPhyState *s = g_new0(M5StopwatchPhyState, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *mr = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+
+    memory_region_init_io(mr, NULL, &phy_ops, s, "esp32s3.phy", PHY_SIZE);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, PHY_BASE, mr, 1);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_regi2c.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_regi2c.c
