index 0000000..9542722
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_regi2c.c
@@ -0,0 +1,98 @@
+/*
+ * ESP32-S3 ROM analog-I2C ("regi2c") compatibility model.
+ *
+ * The ESP32-S3 boot ROM uses the word-indexed register window at
+ * 0x6000e000 to talk to the on-chip analog blocks (BOD, BBPLL, APLL and
+ * SAR ADC).  The window is not described in the public peripheral address
+ * map, but the ROM and ESP-IDF's S2 regi2c implementation use the same
+ * layout:
+ *
+ *   word[host_id] = block | (data << 8) | I2C_RTC_WR_CNTL
+ *   word[host_id].bit25 = busy
+ *
+ * The master-reset path additionally polls the RTC-I2C state field in
+ * word 20 (0x6000e050), bits [26:24], until it reads 7 (idle).
+ *
+ * This model keeps the register file and reports every transaction as
+ * immediately idle.  It is a bring-up model: it does not emulate the
+ * analog blocks or their calibration values, but it lets the ROM finish
+ * its clock/PHY setup instead of spinning forever on an unimplemented
+ * address.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "exec/memory.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define REGI2C_BASE       0x6000e000
+#define REGI2C_SIZE       0x100
+#define REGI2C_WR_CNTL    (1u << 24)
+#define REGI2C_BUSY       (1u << 25)
+#define REGI2C_STATE_MASK (7u << 24)
+#define REGI2C_STATE_IDLE (7u << 24)
+
+typedef struct M5StopwatchRegi2cState {
+    uint32_t regs[REGI2C_SIZE / sizeof(uint32_t)];
+} M5StopwatchRegi2cState;
+
+static uint64_t regi2c_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    M5StopwatchRegi2cState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t value;
+
+    if (addr + size > REGI2C_SIZE || size != 4) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    value = s->regs[addr / 4];
+
+    /*
+     * Data-host words use bit 25 as the transaction-busy flag.  Word 20
+     * (0x6000e050) is the RTC-I2C master-state register instead: its
+     * bits [26:24] read 7 when the master is idle.  Keep the two
+     * semantics separate so the ROM's busy-wait and state-wait loops
+     * both terminate.
+     */
+    value &= ~REGI2C_BUSY;
+    if (addr == 0x4c) {
+        /* Analog-block command/status word: report completion. */
+        value |= (1u << 24);
+    } else if (addr == 0x50) {
+        value &= ~REGI2C_STATE_MASK;
+        value |= REGI2C_STATE_IDLE;
+    }
+    return value;
+}
+
+static void regi2c_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t value64,
+                         unsigned size)
+{
+    M5StopwatchRegi2cState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (addr + size > REGI2C_SIZE || size != 4) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    s->regs[addr / 4] = value64;
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps regi2c_ops = {
+    .read = regi2c_read,
+    .write = regi2c_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 4,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+void m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem)
+{
+    M5StopwatchRegi2cState *s = g_new0(M5StopwatchRegi2cState, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *mr = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+
+    memory_region_init_io(mr, NULL, &regi2c_ops, s,
+                          "esp32s3.regi2c", REGI2C_SIZE);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, REGI2C_BASE, mr, 1);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_sens.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_sens.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b8bf3a1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_sens.c
@@ -0,0 +1,113 @@
+/*
+ * ESP32-S3 SENS/SAR-ADC compatibility model.
+ *
+ * UIFlow2's early boot path starts an SAR conversion and waits for
+ * SENS_MEAS1_DONE_SAR (SAR_MEAS1_CTRL2[16]).  The upstream ESP32-S3
+ * model leaves this address unimplemented, so the read returns zero and
+ * the firmware spins forever before reaching the application.
+ *
+ * This is intentionally a small register-file model: it preserves writes,
+ * reports conversion completion after a software start, and returns a
+ * neutral ADC sample.  It is sufficient for firmware bring-up and does not
+ * claim calibrated ADC accuracy.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "exec/memory.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define SENS_SIZE       0x200
+#define SENS_MEAS1_CTRL2 0x0c
+#define SENS_MEAS2_CTRL2 0x30
+#define SENS_TSENS_CTRL  0x50
+#define MEAS_START_SAR  (1u << 17)
+#define MEAS_DONE_SAR   (1u << 16)
+#define MEAS_DATA_MASK  0xffffu
+#define MEAS_NEUTRAL    0x0800u
+#define TSENS_DUMP_OUT  (1u << 24)
+#define TSENS_POWER_UP  (1u << 22)
+#define TSENS_READY     (1u << 8)
+#define TSENS_DATA_MASK 0xffu
+#define TSENS_NEUTRAL   0x80u
+
+typedef struct M5StopwatchSensState {
+    uint32_t regs[SENS_SIZE / sizeof(uint32_t)];
+    bool meas1_started;
+    bool meas2_started;
+} M5StopwatchSensState;
+
+static bool sens_started(M5StopwatchSensState *s, hwaddr addr)
+{
+    if (addr == SENS_MEAS1_CTRL2) {
+        return s->meas1_started;
+    }
+    if (addr == SENS_MEAS2_CTRL2) {
+        return s->meas2_started;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+static uint64_t sens_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    M5StopwatchSensState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t value;
+
+    if (addr + size > SENS_SIZE || size != 4) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    value = s->regs[addr / 4];
+    if (sens_started(s, addr)) {
+        value |= MEAS_DONE_SAR;
+        if (!(value & MEAS_DATA_MASK)) {
+            value |= MEAS_NEUTRAL;
+        }
+    }
+    if (addr == SENS_TSENS_CTRL &&
+        (value & (TSENS_DUMP_OUT | TSENS_POWER_UP))) {
+        value |= TSENS_READY;
+        if (!(value & TSENS_DATA_MASK)) {
+            value |= TSENS_NEUTRAL;
+        }
+    }
+    return value;
+}
+
+static void sens_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t value64,
+                       unsigned size)
+{
+    M5StopwatchSensState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t value = value64;
+
+    if (addr + size > SENS_SIZE || size != 4) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (addr == SENS_MEAS1_CTRL2 && (value & MEAS_START_SAR)) {
+        s->meas1_started = true;
+    }
+    if (addr == SENS_MEAS2_CTRL2 && (value & MEAS_START_SAR)) {
+        s->meas2_started = true;
+    }
+    s->regs[addr / 4] = value;
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps sens_ops = {
+    .read = sens_read,
+    .write = sens_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 4,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+void m5stopwatch_sens_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem)
+{
+    M5StopwatchSensState *s = g_new0(M5StopwatchSensState, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *mr = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+
+    memory_region_init_io(mr, NULL, &sens_ops, s, "esp32s3.sens", SENS_SIZE);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60008800, mr, 1);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_soft_i2c.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_soft_i2c.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1ced298
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/esp32s3_soft_i2c.c
@@ -0,0 +1,304 @@
+/*
+ * Software-I2C bridge for the M5Stack StopWatch board.
+ *
+ * M5GFX identifies the StopWatch before it opens the hardware I2C
+ * controller.  Its autodetect path bit-bangs SDA/SCL on GPIO47/GPIO48
+ * (probe_i2c_port == -1) and only hands the pins over to the ESP32-S3
+ * I2C1 controller after the board has been identified.
+ *
+ * QEMU's GPIO model only tracks pin levels; it does not connect a
+ * bit-banged bus to the I2C device models.  Without this bridge the
+ * autodetect probes all miss, M5GFX never selects board_M5StopWatch,
+ * and the AMOLED power/reset sequence is never run.
+ *
+ * This module decodes the GPIO-level I2C waveform and forwards complete
+ * bytes to the real QEMU I2CBus, so the existing M5PM1/M5IOE1/RX8130/
+ * BMI270/CST820 models answer the probe exactly as they do on hardware.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/i2c/i2c.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+typedef enum {
+    SOFT_I2C_IDLE,
+    SOFT_I2C_ADDR,
+    SOFT_I2C_TX,
+    SOFT_I2C_RX,
+    SOFT_I2C_ACK_ADDR,
+    SOFT_I2C_ACK_TX,
+    SOFT_I2C_ACK_RX,
+} SoftI2CState;
+
+typedef struct M5StopwatchSoftI2C {
+    I2CBus *bus;
+    int sda_pin;
+    int scl_pin;
+    int sda;
+    int scl;
+    bool active;
+    bool in_xfer;
+    bool read;
+    SoftI2CState state;
+    bool ack_pending;
+    bool master_ack;
+    unsigned ack_step;
+    unsigned bit;
+    uint8_t shift;
+    uint8_t rx;
+} M5StopwatchSoftI2C;
+
+static bool soft_i2c_hardware_active;
+static M5StopwatchSoftI2C *soft_i2c_instance;
+
+static void soft_i2c_trace(const char *fmt, ...)
+{
+    va_list ap;
+
+    if (!getenv("M5SW_SOFT_I2C_TRACE")) {
+        return;
+    }
+    va_start(ap, fmt);
+    vfprintf(stderr, fmt, ap);
+    va_end(ap);
+}
+
+static void soft_i2c_release_sda(M5StopwatchSoftI2C *s)
+{
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(s->sda_pin, 1);
+    s->sda = esp32_gpio_get_line(s->sda_pin);
+}
+
+static void soft_i2c_drive_sda(M5StopwatchSoftI2C *s, int level)
+{
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(s->sda_pin, level ? 1 : 0);
+    s->sda = esp32_gpio_get_line(s->sda_pin);
+}
+
+static void soft_i2c_end(M5StopwatchSoftI2C *s)
+{
+    if (s->in_xfer) {
+        soft_i2c_trace("[soft-i2c] end\n");
+        i2c_end_transfer(s->bus);
+        s->in_xfer = false;
+    }
+    soft_i2c_release_sda(s);
+}
+
+static void soft_i2c_line(void *opaque, int pin, int level)
+{
+    M5StopwatchSoftI2C *s = opaque;
+    int old_sda = s->sda;
+    int old_scl = s->scl;
+
+    /*
+     * M5GFX first bit-bangs the bus to identify the board, then hands the
+     * same pins to the ESP32-S3 I2C controller.  Keep decoding only through
+     * the hand-off: otherwise every hardware waveform is mirrored into the
+     * same QEMU I2CBus and corrupts the controller transaction.
+     */
+    if (soft_i2c_hardware_active) {
+        if (pin == s->sda_pin) {
+            s->sda = level;
+        } else if (pin == s->scl_pin) {
+            s->scl = level;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (pin == s->sda_pin) {
+        s->sda = level;
+    } else if (pin == s->scl_pin) {
+        s->scl = level;
+    } else {
+        return;
+    }
+    soft_i2c_trace("[soft-i2c] %s=%d sda=%d scl=%d\n",
+                   pin == s->sda_pin ? "sda" : "scl",
+                   level, s->sda, s->scl);
+
+    /* START: SDA falls while SCL is high. */
+    if (old_scl && old_sda && !s->sda && s->scl) {
+        soft_i2c_end(s);
+        s->active = true;
+        s->state = SOFT_I2C_ADDR;
+        s->bit = 0;
+        s->shift = 0;
+        s->ack_step = false;
+        soft_i2c_release_sda(s);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    /* STOP: SDA rises while SCL is high. */
+    if (old_scl && !old_sda && s->sda && s->scl) {
+        soft_i2c_end(s);
+        s->active = false;
+        s->state = SOFT_I2C_IDLE;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (!s->active) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (!old_scl && s->scl) {
+        /* Rising edge: master samples SDA. */
+        switch (s->state) {
+        case SOFT_I2C_ADDR:
+        case SOFT_I2C_TX:
+            s->shift = (s->shift << 1) | (s->sda ? 1 : 0);
+            if (++s->bit == 8) {
+                if (s->state == SOFT_I2C_ADDR) {
+                    uint8_t addr = s->shift >> 1;
+                    s->read = s->shift & 1;
+                    soft_i2c_trace("[soft-i2c] addr=%02x read=%d\n",
+                                   addr, s->read);
+                    s->in_xfer = (i2c_start_transfer(s->bus, addr,
+                                                     s->read) == 0);
+                    soft_i2c_trace("[soft-i2c] start-transfer=%d\n",
+                                   s->in_xfer);
+                    s->ack_pending = s->in_xfer;
+                    s->ack_step = false;
+                    soft_i2c_release_sda(s);
+                    s->state = SOFT_I2C_ACK_ADDR;
+                } else {
+                    bool ack = s->in_xfer &&
+                               i2c_send(s->bus, s->shift) == 0;
+                    soft_i2c_trace("[soft-i2c] tx=%02x ack=%d\n",
+                                   s->shift, ack);
+                    s->ack_pending = ack;
+                    s->ack_step = false;
+                    soft_i2c_release_sda(s);
+                    s->state = SOFT_I2C_ACK_TX;
+                }
+                s->bit = 0;
+            }
+            break;
+        case SOFT_I2C_RX:
+            s->shift = (s->shift << 1) | (s->sda ? 1 : 0);
+            if (++s->bit == 8) {
+                s->state = SOFT_I2C_ACK_RX;
+                s->ack_step = false;
+            }
+            break;
+        case SOFT_I2C_ACK_RX:
+            /* Ninth clock: sample the master's ACK/NACK. */
+            if (s->ack_step == 1) {
+                s->master_ack = s->sda;
+                s->ack_step = 2;
+                s->bit = 0;
+            }
+            break;
+        default:
+            break;
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (old_scl && !s->scl) {
+        /* Falling edge: slave changes SDA for the next bit / ACK phase. */
+        switch (s->state) {
+        case SOFT_I2C_ACK_ADDR:
+            if (!s->ack_step) {
+                /* First falling edge after the address: drive ACK/NACK. */
+                soft_i2c_drive_sda(s, s->ack_pending ? 0 : 1);
+                s->ack_step = true;
+            } else {
+                /* Falling edge of the ACK clock: enter data phase. */
+                soft_i2c_release_sda(s);
+                if (s->read) {
+                    s->rx = s->in_xfer ? i2c_recv(s->bus) : 0xff;
+                    soft_i2c_trace("[soft-i2c] rx=%02x\n", s->rx);
+                    s->state = SOFT_I2C_RX;
+                    s->bit = 0;
+                    soft_i2c_drive_sda(s, (s->rx >> 7) & 1);
+                } else {
+                    s->state = SOFT_I2C_TX;
+                    s->bit = 0;
+                }
+            }
+            break;
+        case SOFT_I2C_ACK_TX:
+            if (!s->ack_step) {
+                soft_i2c_drive_sda(s, s->ack_pending ? 0 : 1);
+                s->ack_step = true;
+            } else {
+                soft_i2c_release_sda(s);
+                s->state = SOFT_I2C_TX;
+                s->bit = 0;
+            }
+            break;
+        case SOFT_I2C_RX:
+            if (s->bit > 0 && s->bit < 8) {
+                soft_i2c_drive_sda(s, (s->rx >> (7 - s->bit)) & 1);
+            }
+            break;
+        case SOFT_I2C_ACK_RX:
+            if (s->ack_step == 0) {
+                /*
+                 * The master has sampled the last data bit; release SDA
+                 * only now so the final bit is not lost on the rising
+                 * edge.  The master drives ACK/NACK on this phase.
+                 */
+                soft_i2c_release_sda(s);
+                s->ack_step = 1;
+                break;
+            }
+            if (s->ack_step != 2) {
+                break;
+            }
+            if (!s->master_ack && s->in_xfer) {
+                s->rx = i2c_recv(s->bus);
+                s->state = SOFT_I2C_RX;
+                s->bit = 0;
+                soft_i2c_drive_sda(s, (s->rx >> 7) & 1);
+            } else {
+                if (s->master_ack && s->in_xfer) {
+                    i2c_nack(s->bus);
+                }
+                s->state = SOFT_I2C_IDLE;
+            }
+            break;
+        default:
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init_pins(I2CBus *bus, int sda_pin, int scl_pin)
+{
+    M5StopwatchSoftI2C *s = g_new0(M5StopwatchSoftI2C, 1);
+
+    s->bus = bus;
+    s->sda_pin = sda_pin;
+    s->scl_pin = scl_pin;
+    s->sda = 1;
+    s->scl = 1;
+    esp32_gpio_set_open_drain(sda_pin, true);
+    esp32_gpio_set_open_drain(scl_pin, true);
+    esp32_gpio_add_out_listener(sda_pin, soft_i2c_line, s);
+    esp32_gpio_add_out_listener(scl_pin, soft_i2c_line, s);
+    soft_i2c_instance = s;
+}
+
+void m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init(I2CBus *bus)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init_pins(bus, 47, 48);
+}
+
+void m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_set_hardware_active(void)
+{
+    if (!soft_i2c_hardware_active && soft_i2c_instance) {
+        /*
+         * QEMU can still have current_devs after a partially decoded
+         * bit-banged transaction even if the decoder no longer thinks it is
+         * in a transfer.  Always close the QEMU bus at the hand-off.
+         */
+        i2c_end_transfer(soft_i2c_instance->bus);
+        soft_i2c_instance->in_xfer = false;
+        soft_i2c_instance->active = false;
+        soft_i2c_instance->state = SOFT_I2C_IDLE;
+    }
+    soft_i2c_hardware_active = true;
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_bcm_spi_panel.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_bcm_spi_panel.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d7a542d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_bcm_spi_panel.c
@@ -0,0 +1,216 @@
+/*
+ * M5Stack BCM2835 SPI panel sink.
+ *
+ * Linux panel-mipi-dbi drives the CardputerZero ST7789v3 through BCM SPI0.
+ * This SSI peripheral snoops the DCS command/data stream using the panel D/C
+ * GPIO and reconstructs the RGB565 framebuffer for deterministic UI capture.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/gpio/bcm2835_gpio.h"
+#include "hw/misc/m5_bcm_spi_panel.h"
+#include "qemu/module.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+
+#define M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH   320
+#define M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT  170
+#define M5_BCM_PANEL_DC_PIN  25
+
+struct M5BcmSpiPanelState {
+    SSIPeripheral parent_obj;
+
+    uint16_t *mem;
+    QEMUTimer *dump_timer;
+    char *dump_path;
+    uint8_t cmd;
+    uint8_t params[4];
+    unsigned pidx;
+    uint16_t xs, xe, ys, ye, cx, cy;
+    uint8_t pix_hi;
+    bool pix_half;
+    bool dirty;
+    uint32_t pixel_count;
+};
+
+static uint32_t m5_bcm_panel_pixel_rgb(M5BcmSpiPanelState *s, int x, int y)
+{
+    uint16_t value = s->mem[y * M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH + x];
+    uint32_t r = ((value >> 11) & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+    uint32_t g = ((value >> 5) & 0x3f) * 255 / 63;
+    uint32_t b = (value & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+
+    return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
+}
+
+static void m5_bcm_panel_dump(M5BcmSpiPanelState *s)
+{
+    FILE *file;
+
+    if (!s->dump_path || !s->dirty) {
+        return;
+    }
+    file = fopen(s->dump_path, "wb");
+    if (!file) {
+        return;
+    }
+    fprintf(file, "P6\n%d %d\n255\n",
+            M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH, M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT);
+    for (int y = 0; y < M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT; y++) {
+        for (int x = 0; x < M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH; x++) {
+            uint32_t rgb = m5_bcm_panel_pixel_rgb(s, x, y);
+            uint8_t bytes[3] = { rgb >> 16, rgb >> 8, rgb };
+            fwrite(bytes, sizeof(bytes), 1, file);
+        }
+    }
+    fclose(file);
+    s->dirty = false;
+}
+
+static void m5_bcm_panel_dump_tick(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5BcmSpiPanelState *s = opaque;
+
+    m5_bcm_panel_dump(s);
+    timer_mod(s->dump_timer,
+              qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + 250000000);
+}
+
+static void m5_bcm_panel_put_pixel(M5BcmSpiPanelState *s, uint16_t value)
+{
+    int x = s->cx;
+    int y = s->cy;
+
+    if (x >= 0 && x < M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH &&
+        y >= 0 && y < M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT) {
+        s->mem[y * M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH + x] = value;
+        s->pixel_count++;
+        s->dirty = true;
+    }
+    if (++s->cx > s->xe) {
+        s->cx = s->xs;
+        if (++s->cy > s->ye) {
+            s->cy = s->ys;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static uint32_t m5_bcm_panel_transfer(SSIPeripheral *dev, uint32_t value)
+{
+    M5BcmSpiPanelState *s = M5_BCM_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+    uint8_t byte = value;
+    bool dc = bcm2835_gpio_get_level(M5_BCM_PANEL_DC_PIN);
+
+    if (!dc) {
+        if (getenv("M5SW_BCM_SPI_PANEL_TRACE")) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[bcm-panel] cmd=%02x\n", byte);
+        }
+        s->cmd = byte;
+        s->pidx = 0;
+        s->pix_half = false;
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    switch (s->cmd) {
+    case 0x2a:
+    case 0x2b:
+        if (s->pidx < 4) {
+            s->params[s->pidx++] = byte;
+        }
+        if (s->pidx == 4) {
+            uint16_t start = (s->params[0] << 8) | s->params[1];
+            uint16_t end = (s->params[2] << 8) | s->params[3];
+            if (s->cmd == 0x2a) {
+                s->xs = MIN(start, M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH - 1);
+                s->xe = MIN(end, M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH - 1);
+                s->cx = s->xs;
+            } else {
+                s->ys = MIN(start, M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT - 1);
+                s->ye = MIN(end, M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT - 1);
+                s->cy = s->ys;
+            }
+        }
+        break;
+    case 0x2c:
+        if (!s->pix_half) {
+            s->pix_hi = byte;
+            s->pix_half = true;
+        } else {
+            m5_bcm_panel_put_pixel(s, (s->pix_hi << 8) | byte);
+            if (getenv("M5SW_BCM_SPI_PANEL_TRACE") &&
+                (s->pixel_count == 1 || !(s->pixel_count % 50000))) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[bcm-panel] pixels=%u cmd=%02x "
+                        "window=(%u,%u)-(%u,%u)\n",
+                        s->pixel_count, s->cmd, s->xs, s->ys, s->xe, s->ye);
+            }
+            s->pix_half = false;
+        }
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+    return 0;
+}
+
+static void m5_bcm_panel_realize(SSIPeripheral *dev, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5BcmSpiPanelState *s = M5_BCM_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+    const char *dump_path = getenv("M5SW_BCM_SPI_PANEL_DUMP");
+
+    s->mem = g_new0(uint16_t, M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH * M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT);
+    s->xe = M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH - 1;
+    s->ye = M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT - 1;
+    if (dump_path && *dump_path) {
+        s->dump_path = g_strdup(dump_path);
+        s->dump_timer = timer_new_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL,
+                                     m5_bcm_panel_dump_tick, s);
+        timer_mod(s->dump_timer,
+                  qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + 250000000);
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_bcm_panel_reset(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5BcmSpiPanelState *s = M5_BCM_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+
+    memset(s->mem, 0, M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH * M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT *
+           sizeof(*s->mem));
+    s->cmd = 0;
+    s->pidx = 0;
+    s->xs = 0;
+    s->ys = 0;
+    s->xe = M5_BCM_PANEL_WIDTH - 1;
+    s->ye = M5_BCM_PANEL_HEIGHT - 1;
+    s->cx = 0;
+    s->cy = 0;
+    s->pix_half = false;
+    s->dirty = false;
+    s->pixel_count = 0;
+}
+
+static void m5_bcm_panel_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    SSIPeripheralClass *ssc = SSI_PERIPHERAL_CLASS(klass);
+
+    dc->desc = "M5Stack BCM2835 SPI panel sink";
+    device_class_set_legacy_reset(dc, m5_bcm_panel_reset);
+    ssc->realize = m5_bcm_panel_realize;
+    ssc->transfer = m5_bcm_panel_transfer;
+    ssc->cs_polarity = SSI_CS_NONE;
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo m5_bcm_panel_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_BCM_SPI_PANEL,
+    .parent = TYPE_SSI_PERIPHERAL,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5BcmSpiPanelState),
+    .class_init = m5_bcm_panel_class_init,
+};
+
+static void m5_bcm_panel_register_types(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&m5_bcm_panel_info);
+}
+
+type_init(m5_bcm_panel_register_types)

+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"

+M5Board m5_board;
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_can_peer.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_can_peer.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2f5323b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_can_peer.c

+/*
+ * Virtual CAN peer for M5Stack board models.
+ *
+ * The peer is a real client on QEMU's CAN bus.  When enabled, it echoes every
+ * frame back to the controller, allowing the unmodified guest TWAI driver to
+ * exercise TX, RX, filtering, interrupts and its receive FIFOs.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "net/can_emu.h"
+#include "qom/object.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+typedef struct M5CanPeer {
+    CanBusClientState client;
+    bool echo;
+} M5CanPeer;
+
+static bool m5_can_peer_can_receive(CanBusClientState *client)
+{
+    return true;
+}
+
+static ssize_t m5_can_peer_receive(CanBusClientState *client,
+                                   const struct qemu_can_frame *frames,
+                                   size_t frames_cnt)
+{
+    M5CanPeer *peer = container_of(client, M5CanPeer, client);
+
+    if (peer->echo && frames_cnt) {
+        can_bus_client_send(client, frames, frames_cnt);
+    }
+    return frames_cnt;
+}
+
+static CanBusClientInfo m5_can_peer_info = {
+    .can_receive = m5_can_peer_can_receive,
+    .receive = m5_can_peer_receive,
+};
+
+struct CanBusState *m5_can_bus_create(void)
+{
+    CanBusState *bus = CAN_BUS(object_new(TYPE_CAN_BUS));
+    M5CanPeer *peer = g_new0(M5CanPeer, 1);
+
+    object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), "m5-canbus", OBJECT(bus));
+    peer->echo = getenv("M5SW_CAN_ECHO") != NULL;
+    peer->client.info = &m5_can_peer_info;
+    can_bus_insert_client(bus, &peer->client);
+    return bus;
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_cc1101_spi.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_cc1101_spi.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..a7369a5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_cc1101_spi.c
@@ -0,0 +1,244 @@
+/*
+ * CC1101 Sub-GHz SPI transceiver model for UIFlow2 Cap CC1101.
+ *
+ * The model implements the configuration/status register map, command
+ * strobes, FIFO transfers and a deterministic local TX-to-RX loopback. RF
+ * propagation is intentionally out of scope; the SPI transport and packet
+ * FIFO behavior are real guest-visible paths.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/log.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "hw/gpio/esp32_gpio.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define CC1101_FIFO_SIZE 64
+
+enum {
+    CC_IDLE,
+    CC_REG_WRITE,
+    CC_REG_READ,
+    CC_FIFO_WRITE,
+    CC_FIFO_READ,
+};
+
+typedef struct M5Cc1101SpiState {
+    uint8_t regs[0x40];
+    uint8_t tx_fifo[CC1101_FIFO_SIZE];
+    uint32_t tx_len;
+    uint8_t air_fifo[CC1101_FIFO_SIZE];
+    uint32_t air_len;
+    bool air_pending;
+    uint8_t rx_fifo[CC1101_FIFO_SIZE + 2];
+    uint32_t rx_len;
+    uint32_t rx_pos;
+    uint8_t phase;
+    uint8_t reg;
+    uint8_t marc_state;
+    uint8_t tx_poll_count;
+    int gdo0_pin;
+    bool packet_ready;
+    QEMUTimer *rx_timer;
+} M5Cc1101SpiState;
+
+static void cc1101_rx_timer(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5Cc1101SpiState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (s->packet_ready && s->gdo0_pin >= 0) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(s->gdo0_pin, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+static void cc1101_reset(M5Cc1101SpiState *s)
+{
+    memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+    s->regs[0x31] = 0x14; /* VERSION */
+    s->regs[0x18] = 0x18; /* MCSM0 */
+    s->regs[0x22] = 0x11; /* FREND0 */
+    s->marc_state = 0x01; /* IDLE */
+    s->tx_len = 0;
+    s->air_len = 0;
+    s->air_pending = false;
+    s->rx_len = 0;
+    s->rx_pos = 0;
+    s->packet_ready = false;
+    if (s->gdo0_pin >= 0) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(s->gdo0_pin, 0);
+    }
+    if (s->rx_timer) {
+        timer_del(s->rx_timer);
+    }
+}
+
+static void cc1101_strobe(M5Cc1101SpiState *s, uint8_t command)
+{
+    if (getenv("M5SW_CC1101_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[cc1101] strobe=%02x state=%02x tx=%u rx=%u\n",
+                command, s->marc_state, s->tx_len, s->rx_len);
+    }
+    switch (command) {
+    case 0x30: /* SRES */
+        cc1101_reset(s);
+        break;
+    case 0x31: /* SFSTXON */
+        s->marc_state = 0x12;
+        break;
+    case 0x34: /* SRX */
+        s->marc_state = 0x0d;
+        if (s->air_pending) {
+            s->rx_len = MIN(s->air_len + 2, sizeof(s->rx_fifo));
+            memcpy(s->rx_fifo, s->air_fifo, s->air_len);
+            if (s->rx_len >= 2) {
+                s->rx_fifo[s->rx_len - 2] = 0x80;
+                s->rx_fifo[s->rx_len - 1] = 0x80;
+            }
+            s->rx_pos = 0;
+            s->packet_ready = true;
+        }
+        if (s->packet_ready && s->gdo0_pin >= 0) {
+            esp32_gpio_force_line(s->gdo0_pin, 0);
+            timer_mod(s->rx_timer,
+                      qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 5);
+        }
+        break;
+    case 0x35: /* STX */
+        s->marc_state = 0x13;
+        s->tx_poll_count = 4;
+        s->air_len = MIN(s->tx_len, sizeof(s->air_fifo));
+        if (s->tx_len) {
+            memcpy(s->air_fifo, s->tx_fifo, s->air_len);
+        }
+        s->air_pending = true;
+        s->packet_ready = false;
+        if (s->gdo0_pin >= 0) {
+            esp32_gpio_force_line(s->gdo0_pin, 0);
+        }
+        break;
+    case 0x36: /* SIDLE */
+        s->marc_state = 0x01;
+        break;
+    case 0x3a: /* SFRX */
+        s->rx_len = 0;
+        s->rx_pos = 0;
+        s->packet_ready = false;
+        if (s->rx_timer) {
+            timer_del(s->rx_timer);
+        }
+        if (s->gdo0_pin >= 0) {
+            esp32_gpio_force_line(s->gdo0_pin, 0);
+        }
+        break;
+    case 0x3b: /* SFTX */
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        break;
+    case 0x39: /* SPWD */
+        s->marc_state = 0x00;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t cc1101_read_reg(M5Cc1101SpiState *s, uint8_t reg)
+{
+    uint8_t state;
+
+    switch (reg & 0x3f) {
+    case 0x30:
+        return 0x00;
+    case 0x31:
+        return 0x14;
+    case 0x33:
+        return 0x80;
+    case 0x34:
+        return 0x80;
+    case 0x35:
+        state = s->marc_state;
+        if (state == 0x13 && s->tx_poll_count) {
+            if (--s->tx_poll_count == 0) {
+                s->marc_state = 0x01;
+            }
+        }
+        return s->marc_state;
+    case 0x3a:
+        return s->marc_state == 0x13 ? s->tx_len : 0;
+    case 0x3b:
+        return s->rx_len > s->rx_pos ? s->rx_len - s->rx_pos : 0;
+    default:
+        return s->regs[reg & 0x3f];
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t cc1101_transfer(void *opaque, uint8_t tx)
+{
+    M5Cc1101SpiState *s = opaque;
+    uint8_t value = 0;
+
+    if (getenv("M5SW_CC1101_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[cc1101-spi] phase=%u tx=%02x\n", s->phase, tx);
+    }
+
+    if (s->phase == CC_IDLE) {
+        if (tx >= 0x30 && tx <= 0x3d && !(tx & 0x80)) {
+            cc1101_strobe(s, tx);
+        } else if (tx & 0x80) {
+            s->reg = tx & 0x3f;
+            s->phase = s->reg == 0x3f ? CC_FIFO_READ : CC_REG_READ;
+        } else {
+            s->reg = tx & 0x3f;
+            s->phase = s->reg == 0x3f ? CC_FIFO_WRITE : CC_REG_WRITE;
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+
+    switch (s->phase) {
+    case CC_REG_WRITE:
+        s->regs[s->reg] = tx;
+        s->phase = CC_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case CC_REG_READ:
+        value = cc1101_read_reg(s, s->reg++);
+        break;
+    case CC_FIFO_WRITE:
+        if (s->tx_len < sizeof(s->tx_fifo)) {
+            s->tx_fifo[s->tx_len++] = tx;
+        }
+        break;
+    case CC_FIFO_READ:
+        value = s->rx_pos < s->rx_len ? s->rx_fifo[s->rx_pos++] : 0;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+    return value;
+}
+
+static void cc1101_set_cs(void *opaque, int level)
+{
+    M5Cc1101SpiState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (level) {
+        s->phase = CC_IDLE;
+        s->reg = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static const M5SpiSlaveOps cc1101_ops = {
+    .set_cs = cc1101_set_cs,
+    .transfer = cc1101_transfer,
+};
+
+void m5_cc1101_spi_attach(DeviceState *spi, int cs_pin, int gdo0_pin)
+{
+    M5Cc1101SpiState *s = g_new0(M5Cc1101SpiState, 1);
+
+    s->gdo0_pin = gdo0_pin;
+    s->rx_timer = timer_new_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME, cc1101_rx_timer, s);
+    cc1101_reset(s);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_spi_slave(spi, &cc1101_ops, s, cs_pin);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(gdo0_pin, 0);
+    esp32_gpio_force_line(gdo0_pin, 0);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_epd_panel.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_epd_panel.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..542ffc7
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_epd_panel.c
@@ -0,0 +1,299 @@
+/*
+ * Generic M5Stack e-paper display sink.
+ *
+ * Supports the two controller families used by the ESP32-S3 Paper devices:
+ *  - SSD1677 4-gray: RAM command 0x24/0x26 carry 1-bit LSB/MSB planes;
+ *  - ED2208 Spectra 6: command 0x10 carries packed 4-bit color indices.
+ *
+ * The model exposes the post-refresh optical image through a QEMU console.
+ * It intentionally ignores waveform timing and renders the requested target
+ * image when the controller is activated.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-core.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "ui/console.h"
+#include "ui/input.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+struct M5EpdPanelState {
+    DeviceState parent_obj;
+    QemuConsole *con;
+    uint8_t *lsb;
+    uint8_t *msb;
+    uint8_t *packed;
+    uint32_t native_w, native_h;
+    uint32_t out_w, out_h;
+    uint32_t plane_size;
+    uint32_t packed_size;
+    bool color;
+    bool rotate;
+    bool dirty;
+    bool touch;
+    uint32_t touch_int_pin;
+    uint32_t busy_pin;
+    bool busy_ready_level;
+    uint32_t key_a_pin, key_b_pin, key_c_pin;
+    int mouse_x, mouse_y;
+    bool mouse_btn;
+    uint8_t cmd;
+    uint32_t data_index;
+};
+
+static uint32_t epd_pixel(M5EpdPanelState *s, uint32_t x, uint32_t y)
+{
+    uint32_t idx;
+    uint8_t v;
+
+    if (s->color) {
+        idx = y * (s->native_w / 2) + x / 2;
+        if (idx >= s->packed_size) {
+            return 0xffffffffu;
+        }
+        v = s->packed[idx];
+        v = (x & 1) ? (v & 0x0f) : (v >> 4);
+        switch (v) {
+        case 0x0: return 0xff000000u; /* black */
+        case 0x1: return 0xffffffffu; /* white */
+        case 0x2: return 0xfffff338u; /* yellow */
+        case 0x3: return 0xffbf0000u; /* red */
+        case 0x5: return 0xff6440ffu; /* blue */
+        case 0x6: return 0xff438a1cu; /* green */
+        default:  return 0xffffffffu;
+        }
+    }
+
+    idx = y * ((s->native_w + 7) >> 3) + (x >> 3);
+    if (idx >= s->plane_size) {
+        return 0xffffffffu;
+    }
+    uint8_t mask = 0x80u >> (x & 7);
+    /* M5GFX sends the SSD1677 planes inverted on the wire. */
+    uint8_t l = !(s->lsb[idx] & mask);
+    uint8_t m = !(s->msb[idx] & mask);
+    switch ((m << 1) | l) {
+    case 0: return 0xff000000u;
+    case 1: return 0xff505050u;
+    case 2: return 0xffb0b0b0u;
+    default: return 0xffffffffu;
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_epd_update(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5EpdPanelState *s = opaque;
+    DisplaySurface *surf = qemu_console_surface(s->con);
+
+    if (!surf || surface_bits_per_pixel(surf) != 32 ||
+        surface_width(surf) != s->out_w || surface_height(surf) != s->out_h) {
+        surf = qemu_create_displaysurface(s->out_w, s->out_h);
+        dpy_gfx_replace_surface(s->con, surf);
+        s->dirty = true;
+    }
+    if (!s->dirty) {
+        return;
+    }
+    s->dirty = false;
+    uint32_t *dst = (uint32_t *)surface_data(surf);
+    int stride = surface_stride(surf) / 4;
+    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < s->out_h; y++) {
+        uint32_t *d = dst + y * stride;
+        for (uint32_t x = 0; x < s->out_w; x++) {
+            uint32_t sx = x, sy = y;
+            if (s->rotate) {
+                sx = y;
+                sy = s->native_h - 1 - x;
+            }
+            d[x] = epd_pixel(s, sx, sy);
+        }
+    }
+    dpy_gfx_update(s->con, 0, 0, s->out_w, s->out_h);
+}
+
+static void m5_epd_invalidate(void *opaque)
+{
+    ((M5EpdPanelState *)opaque)->dirty = true;
+}
+
+static const GraphicHwOps m5_epd_ops = {
+    .invalidate = m5_epd_invalidate,
+    .gfx_update = m5_epd_update,
+};
+
+void m5_epd_panel_write(M5EpdPanelState *s, const uint8_t *buf, uint32_t len,
+                        bool command_phase)
+{
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
+        uint8_t b = buf[i];
+        if (command_phase) {
+            s->cmd = b;
+            if (b == 0x10 || b == 0x24 || b == 0x26) {
+                s->data_index = 0;
+            }
+            if (b == 0x12 || b == 0x20) {
+                s->dirty = true;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        if (s->color && s->cmd == 0x10) {
+            if (s->data_index < s->packed_size) {
+                s->packed[s->data_index++] = b;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        if (!s->color && s->cmd == 0x24) {
+            if (s->data_index < s->plane_size) {
+                s->lsb[s->data_index++] = b;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+        if (!s->color && s->cmd == 0x26) {
+            if (s->data_index < s->plane_size) {
+                s->msb[s->data_index++] = b;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_epd_input(DeviceState *dev, QemuConsole *src, InputEvent *evt)
+{
+    M5EpdPanelState *s = M5_EPD_PANEL(dev);
+    switch (evt->type) {
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_KEY: {
+        InputKeyEvent *key = evt->u.key.data;
+        int q = qemu_input_key_value_to_qcode(key->key);
+        int down = key->down;
+        if (getenv("M5SW_EPD_INPUT_TRACE")) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[m5epd-input] qcode=%d down=%d\n", q, down);
+        }
+        uint32_t pin = UINT32_MAX;
+        if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_A || q == Q_KEY_CODE_1 || q == Q_KEY_CODE_LEFT) {
+            pin = s->key_a_pin;
+        } else if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_B || q == Q_KEY_CODE_2 || q == Q_KEY_CODE_RIGHT) {
+            pin = s->key_b_pin;
+        } else if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_C || q == Q_KEY_CODE_3) {
+            pin = s->key_c_pin;
+        } else if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_P) {
+            m5sw_pwr_button(down);
+            break;
+        }
+        if (pin != UINT32_MAX) {
+            /*
+             * Buttons are external mechanical pulls.  Force the line so an
+             * output latch left enabled by the guest cannot mask a press.
+             */
+            esp32_gpio_force_line(pin, !down);
+            if (getenv("M5SW_EPD_INPUT_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[m5epd-input] pin=%u level=%d line=%d\n",
+                        pin, !down, esp32_gpio_get_line(pin));
+            }
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_ABS: {
+        InputMoveEvent *m = evt->u.abs.data;
+        if (m->axis == INPUT_AXIS_X) {
+            s->mouse_x = qemu_input_scale_axis(m->value, INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MIN,
+                                               INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MAX, 0, s->out_w - 1);
+        } else if (m->axis == INPUT_AXIS_Y) {
+            s->mouse_y = qemu_input_scale_axis(m->value, INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MIN,
+                                               INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MAX, 0, s->out_h - 1);
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_BTN: {
+        InputBtnEvent *b = evt->u.btn.data;
+        if (b->button == INPUT_BUTTON_LEFT) {
+            s->mouse_btn = b->down;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_epd_input_sync(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5EpdPanelState *s = M5_EPD_PANEL(dev);
+    if (s->touch) {
+        m5sw_ft_touch_set_pin(s->mouse_btn, s->mouse_x, s->mouse_y,
+                              s->touch_int_pin);
+    }
+}
+
+static const QemuInputHandler m5_epd_input_handler = {
+    .name = "M5Stack e-paper touch/keys",
+    .mask = INPUT_EVENT_MASK_KEY | INPUT_EVENT_MASK_BTN | INPUT_EVENT_MASK_ABS,
+    .event = m5_epd_input,
+    .sync = m5_epd_input_sync,
+};
+
+static void m5_epd_realize(DeviceState *dev, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5EpdPanelState *s = M5_EPD_PANEL(dev);
+    s->plane_size = ((s->native_w + 7) >> 3) * s->native_h;
+    s->packed_size = s->native_w * s->native_h / 2;
+    s->lsb = g_new0(uint8_t, s->plane_size);
+    s->msb = g_new0(uint8_t, s->plane_size);
+    s->packed = g_new0(uint8_t, s->packed_size);
+    memset(s->packed, 0xff, s->packed_size);
+    s->dirty = true;
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(s->busy_pin, s->busy_ready_level);
+    esp32_gpio_set_open_drain(s->busy_pin, true);
+    esp32_gpio_force_line(s->busy_pin, s->busy_ready_level);
+    if (s->key_a_pin != UINT32_MAX) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(s->key_a_pin, 1);
+    }
+    if (s->key_b_pin != UINT32_MAX) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(s->key_b_pin, 1);
+    }
+    if (s->key_c_pin != UINT32_MAX) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(s->key_c_pin, 1);
+    }
+    s->con = graphic_console_init(dev, 0, &m5_epd_ops, s);
+    qemu_console_resize(s->con, s->out_w, s->out_h);
+    QemuInputHandlerState *hs = qemu_input_handler_register(dev, &m5_epd_input_handler);
+    qemu_input_handler_activate(hs);
+}
+
+static Property m5_epd_props[] = {
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("native-width", M5EpdPanelState, native_w, 400),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("native-height", M5EpdPanelState, native_h, 600),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("out-width", M5EpdPanelState, out_w, 400),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("out-height", M5EpdPanelState, out_h, 600),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("color", M5EpdPanelState, color, true),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("rotate", M5EpdPanelState, rotate, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("touch", M5EpdPanelState, touch, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("touch-int-pin", M5EpdPanelState, touch_int_pin, 4),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("busy-pin", M5EpdPanelState, busy_pin, 18),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("busy-ready-level", M5EpdPanelState, busy_ready_level, true),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("key-a-pin", M5EpdPanelState, key_a_pin, 10),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("key-b-pin", M5EpdPanelState, key_b_pin, 9),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("key-c-pin", M5EpdPanelState, key_c_pin, 1),
+    DEFINE_PROP_END_OF_LIST(),
+};
+
+static void m5_epd_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    dc->realize = m5_epd_realize;
+    device_class_set_props(dc, m5_epd_props);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo m5_epd_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_EPD_PANEL,
+    .parent = TYPE_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5EpdPanelState),
+    .class_init = m5_epd_class_init,
+};
+
+static void m5_epd_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&m5_epd_info);
+}
+type_init(m5_epd_register)
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_i2s.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_i2s.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..b3105de
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_i2s.c

+/*
+ * ESP32-S3 I2S register front-end.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/irq.h"
+#include "hw/sysbus.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define R_INT_RAW  0x0c
+#define R_INT_ST   0x10
+#define R_INT_ENA  0x14
+#define R_INT_CLR  0x18
+#define R_RX_CONF  0x20
+#define R_TX_CONF  0x24
+#define RX_UPDATE  (1u << 8)
+#define TX_UPDATE  (1u << 8)
+#define RX_START   (1u << 2)
+#define TX_START   (1u << 2)
+
+struct M5I2SState {
+    SysBusDevice parent_obj;
+    MemoryRegion iomem;
+    qemu_irq irq;
+    uint32_t regs[0x1000 / 4];
+    uint32_t int_ena;
+    uint32_t int_raw;
+    ESPGdmaState *gdma;
+    uint32_t port;
+    FILE *pcm_file;
+    uint64_t pcm_bytes;
+    uint64_t pcm_limit;
+    uint32_t pcm_writes;
+    bool pcm_started;
+    QEMUTimer *tx_timer;
+    int64_t capture_until_ms;
+    int64_t dma_due_ms;
+    uint32_t dma_events;
+    bool dma_irq;
+    bool tx_started;
+    bool dma_active;
+    uint32_t dma_channel;
+    QEMUTimer *rx_timer;
+    uint8_t *rx_pcm;
+    uint32_t rx_pcm_size;
+    uint32_t rx_pcm_pos;
+    uint32_t rx_dma_channel;
+    int64_t rx_dma_due_ms;
+    bool rx_started;
+    bool rx_dma_active;
+    bool trace;
+};
+
+static void i2s_update_irq(M5I2SState *s)
+{
+    qemu_set_irq(s->irq, !!(s->int_raw & s->int_ena));
+}
+
+static uint64_t i2s_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    M5I2SState *s = opaque;
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_INT_RAW:
+        return s->int_raw;
+    case R_INT_ST:
+        return s->int_raw & s->int_ena;
+    case R_INT_ENA:
+        return s->int_ena;
+    default:
+        return addr < sizeof(s->regs) ? s->regs[addr / 4] : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static void i2s_capture_tx(M5I2SState *s)
+{
+    uint32_t channel;
+    uint32_t bytes;
+    GdmaPeripheral periph = s->port ? GDMA_I2S1 : GDMA_I2S0;
+
+    if (!s->gdma
+        || !esp_gdma_get_channel_periph(s->gdma, periph,
+                                         ESP_GDMA_OUT_IDX, &channel)) {
+        return;
+    }
+    s->dma_channel = channel;
+    s->dma_active = true;
+    bytes = esp_gdma_get_out_transfer_size(s->gdma, channel, 1 << 20);
+    if (!bytes) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[i2s%d] dma start bytes=%u\n", s->port, bytes);
+    }
+}
+
+static bool i2s_store_pcm(M5I2SState *s, const uint8_t *buffer, uint32_t bytes);
+
+static void i2s_complete_dma(M5I2SState *s, uint32_t channel)
+{
+    const uint32_t snapshot_size = 4096;
+    g_autofree uint8_t *buffer = g_malloc0(snapshot_size);
+    uint32_t bytes;
+
+    if (!s->gdma) {
+        return;
+    }
+    bytes = esp_gdma_complete_out_descriptor(
+        s->gdma, channel, buffer, snapshot_size);
+    if (!bytes) {
+        if (s->trace) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[i2s%d] dma complete empty chan=%u\n",
+                    s->port, channel);
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[i2s%d] dma read chan=%u bytes=%u head=",
+                s->port, channel, bytes);
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MIN(bytes, 16); i++) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "%02x", buffer[i]);
+        }
+        fprintf(stderr, "\n");
+    }
+    i2s_store_pcm(s, buffer, bytes);
+    s->dma_events++;
+}
+
+static bool i2s_store_pcm(M5I2SState *s, const uint8_t *buffer, uint32_t bytes)
+{
+    uint32_t write_size;
+
+    if (!bytes || s->pcm_bytes >= s->pcm_limit) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (!s->pcm_started) {
+        bool any_nonzero = false;
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < bytes; i++) {
+            if (buffer[i]) {
+                any_nonzero = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        if (!any_nonzero) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        const char *path = getenv("M5SW_I2S_PCM_FILE");
+        if (!path) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        s->pcm_file = fopen(path, "wb");
+        if (!s->pcm_file) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        s->pcm_started = true;
+    }
+    write_size = MIN(bytes, s->pcm_limit - s->pcm_bytes);
+    if (write_size) {
+        fwrite(buffer, 1, write_size, s->pcm_file);
+        fflush(s->pcm_file);
+        s->pcm_bytes += write_size;
+        s->pcm_writes++;
+    }
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr,
+                "[i2s%d] pcm bytes=%u total=%" PRIu64
+                " writes=%u\n",
+                s->port, write_size, s->pcm_bytes, s->pcm_writes);
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+static void i2s_tx_timer(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5I2SState *s = opaque;
+    const uint32_t snapshot_size = 1 << 20;
+    g_autofree uint8_t *buffer = NULL;
+    uint32_t channel;
+    uint32_t bytes;
+    GdmaPeripheral periph = s->port ? GDMA_I2S1 : GDMA_I2S0;
+
+    if (qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) >= s->capture_until_ms
+        || s->pcm_bytes >= s->pcm_limit) {
+        return;
+    }
+    timer_mod(s->tx_timer, qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 1);
+    if (s->dma_irq && s->dma_active && s->dma_events < 128
+        && qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) >= s->dma_due_ms) {
+        if (s->trace) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[i2s%d] dma completion due chan=%u\n",
+                    s->port, s->dma_channel);
+        }
+        i2s_complete_dma(s, s->dma_channel);
+        s->dma_active = false;
+        s->dma_due_ms = qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 20;
+    }
+    if (!s->gdma
+        || !esp_gdma_get_channel_periph(s->gdma, periph,
+                                         ESP_GDMA_OUT_IDX, &channel)) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (!s->dma_active) {
+        s->dma_channel = channel;
+        s->dma_active = true;
+        s->dma_due_ms = qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 20;
+    }
+    if (s->dma_irq) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    buffer = g_malloc(snapshot_size);
+    bytes = esp_gdma_snapshot_out_channel(s->gdma, channel, buffer,
+                                          snapshot_size);
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[i2s%d] timer snapshot bytes=%u writes=%u head=",
+                s->port, bytes, s->pcm_writes);
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < MIN(bytes, 16); i++) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "%02x", buffer[i]);
+        }
+        fprintf(stderr, "\n");
+    }
+    if (!bytes) {
+        return;
+    }
+    i2s_store_pcm(s, buffer, bytes);
+}
+
+static void i2s_rx_timer(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5I2SState *s = opaque;
+    uint8_t buffer[4096];
+    uint32_t available;
+    uint32_t chunk;
+    uint32_t sent;
+
+    if (!s->rx_started || !s->rx_dma_active || !s->gdma
+        || !s->rx_pcm || !s->rx_pcm_size) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) < s->rx_dma_due_ms) {
+        timer_mod(s->rx_timer,
+                  qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 2);
+        return;
+    }
+    available = s->rx_pcm_size - s->rx_pcm_pos;
+    if (!available) {
+        s->rx_pcm_pos = 0;
+        available = s->rx_pcm_size;
+    }
+    chunk = MIN(available, sizeof(buffer));
+    memcpy(buffer, s->rx_pcm + s->rx_pcm_pos, chunk);
+    s->rx_pcm_pos = (s->rx_pcm_pos + chunk) % s->rx_pcm_size;
+    sent = esp_gdma_complete_in_descriptor(
+        s->gdma, s->rx_dma_channel, buffer, chunk);
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[i2s%d] rx dma inject chan=%u bytes=%u\n",
+                s->port, s->rx_dma_channel, sent);
+    }
+    s->rx_dma_due_ms = qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 20;
+    timer_mod(s->rx_timer,
+              qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 2);
+}
+
+static void i2s_rx_start(M5I2SState *s)
+{
+    uint32_t channel;
+    const char *path = getenv("M5SW_I2S_RX_PCM_FILE");
+    GdmaPeripheral periph = s->port ? GDMA_I2S1 : GDMA_I2S0;
+
+    if (!s->gdma
+        || !esp_gdma_get_channel_periph(s->gdma, periph,
+                                         ESP_GDMA_IN_IDX, &channel)) {
+        return;
+    }
+    s->rx_dma_channel = channel;
+    s->rx_dma_active = true;
+    s->rx_pcm_pos = 0;
+    s->rx_dma_due_ms = qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 20;
+    if (path) {
+        g_autofree char *contents = NULL;
+        gsize length = 0;
+        if (g_file_get_contents(path, &contents, &length, NULL) && length) {
+            g_free(s->rx_pcm);
+            s->rx_pcm = (uint8_t *)contents;
+            contents = NULL;
+            s->rx_pcm_size = length;
+        }
+    }
+    timer_mod(s->rx_timer,
+              qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 2);
+}
+
+static void i2s_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t value,
+                      unsigned size)
+{
+    M5I2SState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t v = value;
+
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[i2s] wr %03x <- %08x\n",
+                (unsigned)addr, v);
+    }
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_INT_ENA:
+        s->int_ena = v;
+        i2s_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    case R_INT_CLR:
+        s->int_raw &= ~v;
+        i2s_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    case R_RX_CONF:
+        if ((v & RX_START) && !s->rx_started) {
+            i2s_rx_start(s);
+        }
+        s->rx_started = !!(v & RX_START);
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = v & ~RX_UPDATE;
+        break;
+    case R_TX_CONF:
+        if ((v & TX_START) && !s->tx_started) {
+            i2s_capture_tx(s);
+            if (!s->pcm_started) {
+                s->pcm_writes = 0;
+                s->pcm_bytes = 0;
+            }
+            s->dma_events = 0;
+            s->capture_until_ms =
+                qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 2000;
+            s->dma_due_ms = qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 500;
+            timer_mod(s->tx_timer,
+                      qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 1);
+        }
+        s->tx_started = !!(v & TX_START);
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = v & ~TX_UPDATE;
+        break;
+    default:
+        if (addr < sizeof(s->regs)) {
+            s->regs[addr / 4] = v;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps i2s_ops = {
+    .read = i2s_read,
+    .write = i2s_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 1,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+static void i2s_reset(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5I2SState *s = M5_I2S(dev);
+    memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+    s->int_ena = 0;
+    s->int_raw = 0;
+    s->tx_started = false;
+    s->dma_active = false;
+    s->dma_channel = 0;
+    s->rx_started = false;
+    s->rx_dma_active = false;
+    s->rx_dma_channel = 0;
+    s->pcm_started = false;
+    if (s->rx_timer) {
+        timer_del(s->rx_timer);
+    }
+    if (s->tx_timer) {
+        timer_del(s->tx_timer);
+    }
+}
+
+static void i2s_init(Object *obj)
+{
+    M5I2SState *s = M5_I2S(obj);
+    SysBusDevice *sbd = SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj);
+    memory_region_init_io(&s->iomem, obj, &i2s_ops, s, TYPE_M5_I2S, 0x1000);
+    sysbus_init_mmio(sbd, &s->iomem);
+    sysbus_init_irq(sbd, &s->irq);
+    s->trace = getenv("M5SW_I2S_TRACE") != NULL;
+    s->dma_irq = getenv("M5SW_I2S_DMA_IRQ") != NULL;
+    s->pcm_limit = 4 << 20;
+    s->tx_timer = timer_new_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME, i2s_tx_timer, s);
+    s->rx_timer = timer_new_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME, i2s_rx_timer, s);
+}
+
+void m5_i2s_attach(DeviceState *dev, ESPGdmaState *gdma, uint32_t port)
+{
+    M5I2SState *s = M5_I2S(dev);
+    s->gdma = gdma;
+    s->port = port;
+}
+
+static void i2s_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    device_class_set_legacy_reset(dc, i2s_reset);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo i2s_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_I2S,
+    .parent = TYPE_SYS_BUS_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5I2SState),
+    .instance_init = i2s_init,
+    .class_init = i2s_class_init,
+};
+
+static void i2s_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&i2s_info);
+}
+type_init(i2s_register)
+
+void m5_i2s_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                       ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    static const struct {
+        hwaddr base;
+        int irq_source;
+    } ports[] = {
+        { 0x6000f000, ETS_I2S0_INTR_SOURCE },
+        { 0x6002d000, ETS_I2S1_INTR_SOURCE },
+    };
+
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(ports); i++) {
+        DeviceState *dev = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_I2S);
+        m5_i2s_attach(dev, gdma, i);
+        sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), &error_fatal);
+        memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+            sys_mem, ports[i].base,
+            sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 0), 1);
+        sysbus_connect_irq(
+            SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 0,
+            qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ports[i].irq_source));
+    }
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_i2s_esp32.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_i2s_esp32.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..6988db5
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_i2s_esp32.c
@@ -0,0 +1,554 @@
+/*
+ * ESP32 classic I2S controller model.
+ *
+ * The original ESP32 uses the I2S block's own linked-list DMA engine rather
+ * than the GDMA block found on ESP32-S3.  Core2 uses I2S0 for both the NS4168
+ * speaker path and the SPM1423 PDM microphone path.  This model consumes the
+ * real IDF DMA descriptors and provides host PCM capture/injection at the
+ * controller boundary.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "exec/address-spaces.h"
+#include "hw/irq.h"
+#include "hw/misc/esp32_reg.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "hw/sysbus.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define R_CONF          0x008
+#define R_INT_RAW       0x00c
+#define R_INT_ST        0x010
+#define R_INT_ENA       0x014
+#define R_INT_CLR       0x018
+#define R_OUT_LINK      0x030
+#define R_IN_LINK       0x034
+#define R_OUT_EOF_DESC  0x038
+#define R_IN_EOF_DESC   0x03c
+#define R_STATE         0x0bc
+
+#define CONF_TX_RESET       BIT(0)
+#define CONF_RX_RESET       BIT(1)
+#define CONF_TX_FIFO_RESET  BIT(2)
+#define CONF_RX_FIFO_RESET  BIT(3)
+#define CONF_TX_START       BIT(4)
+#define CONF_RX_START       BIT(5)
+
+#define LINK_STOP           BIT(28)
+#define LINK_START          BIT(29)
+#define LINK_RESTART        BIT(30)
+#define LINK_ADDR_MASK      0x000fffff
+
+#define INT_RX_EOF          BIT(9)
+#define INT_TX_EOF          BIT(12)
+#define INT_RX_DSCR_ERR     BIT(13)
+#define INT_TX_DSCR_ERR     BIT(14)
+
+#define I2S_DESC_SIZE       12
+#define I2S_DESC_MAX_BYTES  4092
+#define I2S_PCM_MAX_BYTES   (4 << 20)
+#define I2S_DMA_PERIOD_MS   10
+
+typedef struct M5Esp32I2SState {
+    SysBusDevice parent_obj;
+    MemoryRegion iomem;
+    qemu_irq irq;
+    uint32_t port;
+    uint32_t regs[0x1000 / 4];
+    uint32_t int_ena;
+    uint32_t int_raw;
+    QEMUTimer *tx_timer;
+    QEMUTimer *rx_timer;
+    uint32_t tx_desc;
+    uint32_t tx_first_desc;
+    uint32_t rx_desc;
+    uint32_t rx_first_desc;
+    bool tx_running;
+    bool rx_running;
+    bool trace;
+    FILE *pcm_file;
+    uint64_t pcm_bytes;
+    uint32_t pcm_writes;
+    bool pcm_started;
+    uint8_t *rx_pcm;
+    uint32_t rx_pcm_size;
+    uint32_t rx_pcm_pos;
+} M5Esp32I2SState;
+
+typedef struct M5Esp32I2SDesc {
+    uint32_t dw0;
+    uint32_t buffer;
+    uint32_t next;
+} M5Esp32I2SDesc;
+
+static void i2s_update_irq(M5Esp32I2SState *s)
+{
+    qemu_set_irq(s->irq, !!(s->int_raw & s->int_ena));
+}
+
+static bool i2s_store_pcm(M5Esp32I2SState *s, const uint8_t *buffer,
+                          uint32_t bytes)
+{
+    uint32_t write_size;
+
+    if (!bytes || s->pcm_bytes >= I2S_PCM_MAX_BYTES) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (!s->pcm_started) {
+        bool any_nonzero = false;
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < bytes; i++) {
+            if (buffer[i]) {
+                any_nonzero = true;
+                break;
+            }
+        }
+        if (!any_nonzero) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        const char *path = getenv("M5SW_I2S_PCM_FILE");
+        if (!path) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        s->pcm_file = fopen(path, "wb");
+        if (!s->pcm_file) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        s->pcm_started = true;
+    }
+    write_size = MIN(bytes, I2S_PCM_MAX_BYTES - s->pcm_bytes);
+    fwrite(buffer, 1, write_size, s->pcm_file);
+    fflush(s->pcm_file);
+    s->pcm_bytes += write_size;
+    s->pcm_writes++;
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr,
+                "[esp32-i2s%d] pcm bytes=%u total=%" PRIu64
+                " writes=%u\n",
+                s->port, write_size, s->pcm_bytes, s->pcm_writes);
+    }
+    return true;
+}
+
+static uint32_t i2s_desc_decode_addr(uint32_t link_reg)
+{
+    /*
+     * The legacy I2S link register stores the low 20 bits of an internal-RAM
+     * address.  IDF places I2S descriptors in ESP32 DRAM at 0x3ffxxxxx.
+     */
+    return 0x3ff00000u | (link_reg & LINK_ADDR_MASK);
+}
+
+static bool i2s_read_desc(uint32_t addr, M5Esp32I2SDesc *desc)
+{
+    uint8_t raw[I2S_DESC_SIZE];
+
+    if (!addr || address_space_read(&address_space_memory, addr,
+                                    MEMTXATTRS_UNSPECIFIED, raw,
+                                    sizeof(raw)) != MEMTX_OK) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    desc->dw0 = ldl_le_p(raw);
+    desc->buffer = ldl_le_p(raw + 4);
+    desc->next = ldl_le_p(raw + 8);
+    return true;
+}
+
+static bool i2s_write_desc_length(uint32_t addr, uint32_t length)
+{
+    uint8_t raw[4];
+    uint32_t dw0;
+
+    if (address_space_read(&address_space_memory, addr,
+                           MEMTXATTRS_UNSPECIFIED, raw, sizeof(raw))
+        != MEMTX_OK) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    dw0 = ldl_le_p(raw);
+    dw0 = (dw0 & ~0x00fff000u) | ((length & 0xfff) << 12);
+    stl_le_p(raw, dw0);
+    return address_space_write(&address_space_memory, addr,
+                               MEMTXATTRS_UNSPECIFIED, raw,
+                               sizeof(raw)) == MEMTX_OK;
+}
+
+static uint32_t i2s_desc_bytes(const M5Esp32I2SDesc *desc)
+{
+    uint32_t bytes = desc->dw0 & 0xfff;
+
+    return bytes ? MIN(bytes, I2S_DESC_MAX_BYTES) : 4096;
+}
+
+static uint32_t i2s_next_desc(const M5Esp32I2SDesc *desc, uint32_t current,
+                              uint32_t first)
+{
+    if (desc->next && desc->next != current) {
+        return desc->next;
+    }
+    return (desc->dw0 & BIT(30)) ? first : 0;
+}
+
+static void i2s_tx_timer(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5Esp32I2SState *s = opaque;
+    M5Esp32I2SDesc desc;
+    g_autofree uint8_t *buffer = NULL;
+    uint32_t bytes;
+
+    if (!s->tx_running || !s->tx_desc) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (!i2s_read_desc(s->tx_desc, &desc)) {
+        s->int_raw |= INT_TX_DSCR_ERR;
+        i2s_update_irq(s);
+        s->tx_running = false;
+        return;
+    }
+    bytes = i2s_desc_bytes(&desc);
+    buffer = g_malloc0(bytes);
+    if (bytes && address_space_read(&address_space_memory, desc.buffer,
+                                    MEMTXATTRS_UNSPECIFIED, buffer,
+                                    bytes) != MEMTX_OK) {
+        s->int_raw |= INT_TX_DSCR_ERR;
+        i2s_update_irq(s);
+        s->tx_running = false;
+        return;
+    }
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr,
+                "[esp32-i2s%d] tx desc=%08x buffer=%08x bytes=%u next=%08x\n",
+                s->port, s->tx_desc, desc.buffer, bytes, desc.next);
+    }
+    i2s_store_pcm(s, buffer, bytes);
+    s->regs[R_OUT_EOF_DESC / 4] = s->tx_desc;
+    s->int_raw |= INT_TX_EOF;
+    i2s_update_irq(s);
+    s->tx_desc = i2s_next_desc(&desc, s->tx_desc, s->tx_first_desc);
+    if (!s->tx_desc) {
+        s->tx_running = false;
+        return;
+    }
+    timer_mod(s->tx_timer,
+              qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + I2S_DMA_PERIOD_MS);
+}
+
+static void i2s_load_rx_pcm(M5Esp32I2SState *s)
+{
+    const char *path = getenv("M5SW_I2S_RX_PCM_FILE");
+    g_autofree char *contents = NULL;
+    gsize length = 0;
+
+    g_free(s->rx_pcm);
+    s->rx_pcm = NULL;
+    s->rx_pcm_size = 0;
+    s->rx_pcm_pos = 0;
+    if (!path) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (g_file_get_contents(path, &contents, &length, NULL) && length) {
+        s->rx_pcm = (uint8_t *)contents;
+        contents = NULL;
+        s->rx_pcm_size = length;
+    }
+}
+
+static void i2s_rx_timer(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5Esp32I2SState *s = opaque;
+    M5Esp32I2SDesc desc;
+    g_autofree uint8_t *buffer = NULL;
+    uint32_t bytes;
+    uint32_t available;
+    uint32_t first;
+
+    if (!s->rx_running || !s->rx_desc) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (!i2s_read_desc(s->rx_desc, &desc)) {
+        s->int_raw |= INT_RX_DSCR_ERR;
+        i2s_update_irq(s);
+        s->rx_running = false;
+        return;
+    }
+    bytes = i2s_desc_bytes(&desc);
+    buffer = g_malloc0(bytes);
+    if (s->rx_pcm_size && bytes) {
+        available = s->rx_pcm_size - s->rx_pcm_pos;
+        if (!available) {
+            s->rx_pcm_pos = 0;
+            available = s->rx_pcm_size;
+        }
+        first = MIN(bytes, available);
+        memcpy(buffer, s->rx_pcm + s->rx_pcm_pos, first);
+        if (first < bytes) {
+            memcpy(buffer + first, s->rx_pcm,
+                   MIN(bytes - first, s->rx_pcm_size));
+        }
+        s->rx_pcm_pos = (s->rx_pcm_pos + first) % s->rx_pcm_size;
+    }
+    if (bytes && address_space_write(&address_space_memory, desc.buffer,
+                                     MEMTXATTRS_UNSPECIFIED, buffer,
+                                     bytes) != MEMTX_OK) {
+        s->int_raw |= INT_RX_DSCR_ERR;
+        i2s_update_irq(s);
+        s->rx_running = false;
+        return;
+    }
+    i2s_write_desc_length(s->rx_desc, bytes);
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr,
+                "[esp32-i2s%d] rx desc=%08x buffer=%08x bytes=%u next=%08x\n",
+                s->port, s->rx_desc, desc.buffer, bytes, desc.next);
+    }
+    s->regs[R_IN_EOF_DESC / 4] = s->rx_desc;
+    s->int_raw |= INT_RX_EOF;
+    i2s_update_irq(s);
+    s->rx_desc = i2s_next_desc(&desc, s->rx_desc, s->rx_first_desc);
+    if (!s->rx_desc) {
+        s->rx_running = false;
+        return;
+    }
+    timer_mod(s->rx_timer,
+              qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + I2S_DMA_PERIOD_MS);
+}
+
+static uint64_t i2s_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    M5Esp32I2SState *s = opaque;
+
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_INT_RAW:
+        return s->int_raw;
+    case R_INT_ST:
+        return s->int_raw & s->int_ena;
+    case R_INT_ENA:
+        return s->int_ena;
+    case R_STATE:
+        return (!s->tx_running ? BIT(0) : 0) |
+               (!s->rx_running ? BIT(2) : 0);
+    default:
+        return addr < sizeof(s->regs) ? s->regs[addr / 4] : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static void i2s_write_link(M5Esp32I2SState *s, hwaddr addr, uint32_t value,
+                           bool tx)
+{
+    uint32_t first;
+
+    s->regs[addr / 4] = value;
+    first = i2s_desc_decode_addr(value);
+    if (tx) {
+        s->tx_first_desc = first;
+        if (value & (LINK_START | LINK_RESTART)) {
+            s->tx_desc = first;
+            s->tx_running = true;
+            if (s->regs[R_CONF / 4] & CONF_TX_START) {
+                timer_mod(s->tx_timer,
+                          qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL)
+                          + I2S_DMA_PERIOD_MS);
+            }
+        }
+        if (value & LINK_STOP) {
+            s->tx_running = false;
+            timer_del(s->tx_timer);
+        }
+    } else {
+        s->rx_first_desc = first;
+        if (value & (LINK_START | LINK_RESTART)) {
+            s->rx_desc = first;
+            s->rx_running = true;
+            i2s_load_rx_pcm(s);
+            if (s->regs[R_CONF / 4] & CONF_RX_START) {
+                timer_mod(s->rx_timer,
+                          qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL)
+                          + I2S_DMA_PERIOD_MS);
+            }
+        }
+        if (value & LINK_STOP) {
+            s->rx_running = false;
+            timer_del(s->rx_timer);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void i2s_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t value,
+                      unsigned size)
+{
+    M5Esp32I2SState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t v = value;
+    bool old_tx_start;
+    bool old_rx_start;
+
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[esp32-i2s%d] wr %03x <- %08x\n",
+                s->port, (unsigned)addr, v);
+    }
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_INT_ENA:
+        s->int_ena = v;
+        i2s_update_irq(s);
+        return;
+    case R_INT_CLR:
+        s->int_raw &= ~v;
+        i2s_update_irq(s);
+        return;
+    case R_CONF:
+        old_tx_start = !!(s->regs[addr / 4] & CONF_TX_START);
+        old_rx_start = !!(s->regs[addr / 4] & CONF_RX_START);
+        if (v & CONF_TX_RESET) {
+            s->tx_running = false;
+            timer_del(s->tx_timer);
+            s->tx_desc = 0;
+        }
+        if (v & CONF_RX_RESET) {
+            s->rx_running = false;
+            timer_del(s->rx_timer);
+            s->rx_desc = 0;
+        }
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = v & ~(CONF_TX_RESET | CONF_RX_RESET |
+                                  CONF_TX_FIFO_RESET | CONF_RX_FIFO_RESET);
+        if ((v & CONF_TX_START) && !old_tx_start && s->tx_first_desc) {
+            s->tx_desc = s->tx_first_desc;
+            s->tx_running = true;
+            if (!s->pcm_started) {
+                s->pcm_writes = 0;
+                s->pcm_bytes = 0;
+            }
+            timer_mod(s->tx_timer,
+                      qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL)
+                      + I2S_DMA_PERIOD_MS);
+        }
+        if ((v & CONF_RX_START) && !old_rx_start && s->rx_first_desc) {
+            s->rx_desc = s->rx_first_desc;
+            s->rx_running = true;
+            i2s_load_rx_pcm(s);
+            timer_mod(s->rx_timer,
+                      qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL)
+                      + I2S_DMA_PERIOD_MS);
+        }
+        if (!(v & CONF_TX_START)) {
+            s->tx_running = false;
+            timer_del(s->tx_timer);
+        }
+        if (!(v & CONF_RX_START)) {
+            s->rx_running = false;
+            timer_del(s->rx_timer);
+        }
+        return;
+    case R_OUT_LINK:
+        i2s_write_link(s, addr, v, true);
+        return;
+    case R_IN_LINK:
+        i2s_write_link(s, addr, v, false);
+        return;
+    default:
+        if (addr < sizeof(s->regs)) {
+            s->regs[addr / 4] = v;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps i2s_ops = {
+    .read = i2s_read,
+    .write = i2s_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 4,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+static void i2s_reset(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5Esp32I2SState *s = M5_ESP32_I2S(dev);
+
+    timer_del(s->tx_timer);
+    timer_del(s->rx_timer);
+    memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+    s->int_ena = 0;
+    s->int_raw = 0;
+    s->tx_desc = 0;
+    s->tx_first_desc = 0;
+    s->rx_desc = 0;
+    s->rx_first_desc = 0;
+    s->tx_running = false;
+    s->rx_running = false;
+    s->pcm_started = false;
+    qemu_set_irq(s->irq, 0);
+}
+
+static void i2s_init(Object *obj)
+{
+    M5Esp32I2SState *s = M5_ESP32_I2S(obj);
+
+    memory_region_init_io(&s->iomem, obj, &i2s_ops, s,
+                          TYPE_M5_ESP32_I2S, 0x1000);
+    sysbus_init_mmio(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj), &s->iomem);
+    sysbus_init_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj), &s->irq);
+    s->tx_timer = timer_new_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL, i2s_tx_timer, s);
+    s->rx_timer = timer_new_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL, i2s_rx_timer, s);
+    s->trace = getenv("M5SW_I2S_TRACE") != NULL;
+}
+
+static void i2s_finalize(Object *obj)
+{
+    M5Esp32I2SState *s = M5_ESP32_I2S(obj);
+
+    timer_free(s->tx_timer);
+    timer_free(s->rx_timer);
+    if (s->pcm_file) {
+        fclose(s->pcm_file);
+    }
+    g_free(s->rx_pcm);
+}
+
+static void i2s_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+
+    device_class_set_legacy_reset(dc, i2s_reset);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo i2s_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_ESP32_I2S,
+    .parent = TYPE_SYS_BUS_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5Esp32I2SState),
+    .instance_init = i2s_init,
+    .instance_finalize = i2s_finalize,
+    .class_init = i2s_class_init,
+};
+
+static void i2s_register_types(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&i2s_info);
+}
+
+type_init(i2s_register_types)
+
+void m5_esp32_i2s_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, BusState *periph_bus,
+                             DeviceState *intmatrix)
+{
+    static const struct {
+        hwaddr base;
+        int irq;
+    } ports[] = {
+        { DR_REG_I2S_BASE, ETS_I2S0_INTR_SOURCE },
+        { DR_REG_I2S1_BASE, ETS_I2S1_INTR_SOURCE },
+    };
+
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(ports); i++) {
+        DeviceState *dev = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_ESP32_I2S);
+        M5Esp32I2SState *s = M5_ESP32_I2S(dev);
+        char name[16];
+
+        s->port = i;
+        snprintf(name, sizeof(name), "esp32.i2s%u", i);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), name, OBJECT(dev));
+        qdev_realize(dev, periph_bus, &error_fatal);
+        memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+            sys_mem, ports[i].base,
+            sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 0), 0);
+        sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 0,
+                           qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ports[i].irq));
+    }
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_lcd_cam.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_lcd_cam.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..0a34bcd
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_lcd_cam.c
@@ -0,0 +1,322 @@
+/*
+ * Minimal ESP32-S3 LCD_CAM camera front-end.
+ *
+ * The camera sensor is configured through SCCB by the guest.  This model
+ * supplies deterministic RGB565 frames through the real LCD_CAM interrupt
+ * and GDMA RX descriptor path used by esp32-camera.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/irq.h"
+#include "hw/sysbus.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define R_CAM_CTRL   0x04
+#define R_CAM_CTRL1  0x08
+#define R_CAM_RGB    0x0c
+#define R_INT_ENA    0x64
+#define R_INT_RAW    0x68
+#define R_INT_ST     0x6c
+#define R_INT_CLR    0x70
+#define CAM_UPDATE   (1u << 4)
+#define CAM_START    (1u << 29)
+#define CAM_RESET    (1u << 30)
+#define CAM_AFIFO_RST (1u << 31)
+#define CAM_VSYNC_INT (1u << 2)
+
+struct M5LcdCamState {
+    SysBusDevice parent_obj;
+    MemoryRegion iomem;
+    qemu_irq irq;
+    ESPGdmaState *gdma;
+    uint32_t regs[0x100 / 4];
+    uint32_t int_ena;
+    uint32_t int_raw;
+    uint32_t width;
+    uint32_t height;
+    uint32_t frame_seed;
+    bool frame_auto_increment;
+    uint32_t frame_offset;
+    uint32_t frame_bytes;
+    uint32_t chunk_len;
+    uint32_t chunks_left;
+    uint8_t *frame_data;
+    uint32_t frame_data_size;
+    bool started;
+    bool dma_armed;
+    QEMUTimer *capture_timer;
+};
+
+static void lcd_cam_update_irq(M5LcdCamState *s)
+{
+    qemu_set_irq(s->irq, !!(s->int_raw & s->int_ena));
+}
+
+static uint16_t camera_pattern_pixel(M5LcdCamState *s, uint32_t pixel)
+{
+    uint32_t area = MAX(1u, s->width * s->height);
+    uint32_t p = pixel % area;
+    uint32_t x = p % MAX(1u, s->width);
+    uint32_t y = p / MAX(1u, s->width);
+    uint8_t r, g, b;
+
+    uint32_t band = (y * 4 / MAX(1u, s->height) + s->frame_seed) & 3;
+    if (band == 0) {
+        r = 235; g = 48; b = 48;
+    } else if (band == 1) {
+        r = 235; g = 190; b = 35;
+    } else if (band == 2) {
+        r = 40; g = 190; b = 80;
+    } else {
+        r = 35; g = 105; b = 235;
+    }
+    if ((x % 40) < 2 || (y % 40) < 2) {
+        r = g = b = 245;
+    }
+    return ((r >> 3) << 11) | ((g >> 2) << 5) | (b >> 3);
+}
+
+static void lcd_cam_fill_frame(M5LcdCamState *s, uint8_t *data, uint32_t len)
+{
+    uint32_t frame_bytes = MAX(2u, s->width * s->height * 2);
+    if (s->frame_data && s->frame_data_size >= frame_bytes) {
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
+            data[i] = s->frame_data[(s->frame_offset + i) % frame_bytes];
+        }
+        s->frame_offset = (s->frame_offset + len) % frame_bytes;
+        return;
+    }
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i += 2) {
+        uint32_t offset = (s->frame_offset + i) % frame_bytes;
+        uint16_t pixel = camera_pattern_pixel(s, offset / 2);
+        data[i] = pixel & 0xff;
+        if (i + 1 < len) {
+            data[i + 1] = pixel >> 8;
+        }
+    }
+    s->frame_offset = (s->frame_offset + len) % frame_bytes;
+}
+
+static void lcd_cam_capture(M5LcdCamState *s)
+{
+    uint32_t channel;
+    uint32_t len = s->chunk_len;
+    g_autofree uint8_t *data = g_malloc0(len);
+
+    if (!(s->int_ena & CAM_VSYNC_INT)) {
+        timer_mod(s->capture_timer,
+                  qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + 10);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    lcd_cam_fill_frame(s, data, len);
+    if (s->gdma) {
+        bool found = esp_gdma_get_channel_periph(
+            s->gdma, GDMA_LCDCAM, ESP_GDMA_IN_IDX, &channel);
+        if (!found) {
+            if (getenv("M5SW_CAMERA_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[lcd-cam] waiting for camera GDMA channel\n");
+            }
+            /*
+             * The esp32-camera driver only programs the IN_LINK after its
+             * first VSYNC event.  A real sensor keeps producing VSYNC while
+             * the DMA channel is idle, so do the same instead of waiting for
+             * a DMA transfer that cannot start yet.
+             */
+            s->int_raw |= CAM_VSYNC_INT;
+            lcd_cam_update_irq(s);
+            timer_mod(s->capture_timer,
+                      qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + 10);
+            return;
+        }
+        s->dma_armed = false;
+        if (s->chunks_left) {
+            esp_gdma_complete_in_descriptor(s->gdma, channel, data, len);
+            esp_gdma_set_chan_interrupt(s->gdma, ESP_GDMA_IN_IDX, channel,
+                                        R_GDMA_INTERRUPT_IN_SUC_EOF_MASK);
+            s->chunks_left--;
+            timer_mod(s->capture_timer,
+                      qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + 1);
+        } else {
+            if (s->frame_auto_increment) {
+                s->frame_seed++;
+            }
+            s->int_raw |= CAM_VSYNC_INT;
+            lcd_cam_update_irq(s);
+            if (getenv("M5SW_CAMERA_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[lcd-cam] frame complete bytes=%u\n",
+                        s->frame_bytes);
+            }
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void lcd_cam_gdma_started(void *opaque, GdmaPeripheral periph, int dir)
+{
+    M5LcdCamState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (getenv("M5SW_CAMERA_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr,
+                "[lcd-cam] gdma-start periph=%u dir=%d started=%d\n",
+                periph, dir, s->started);
+    }
+    if (periph == GDMA_LCDCAM && dir == ESP_GDMA_IN_IDX) {
+        s->dma_armed = true;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint64_t lcd_cam_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    M5LcdCamState *s = opaque;
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_INT_ENA:
+        return s->int_ena;
+    case R_INT_RAW:
+        return s->int_raw;
+    case R_INT_ST:
+        return s->int_raw & s->int_ena;
+    default:
+        return addr < sizeof(s->regs) ? s->regs[addr / 4] : 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static void lcd_cam_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t value,
+                          unsigned size)
+{
+    M5LcdCamState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t v = value;
+
+    switch (addr) {
+    case R_CAM_CTRL:
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = v & ~CAM_UPDATE;
+        break;
+    case R_CAM_CTRL1: {
+        bool start = !!(v & CAM_START);
+        bool rising = start && !s->started;
+        if (getenv("M5SW_CAMERA_TRACE")) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[lcd-cam] ctrl1=%08x start=%d rising=%d armed=%d\n",
+                    (unsigned)v, start, rising, s->dma_armed);
+        }
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = v & ~(CAM_RESET | CAM_AFIFO_RST);
+        s->started = start;
+        if (rising) {
+            s->frame_bytes = MAX(2u, s->width * s->height * 2);
+            s->chunk_len = (v & 0xffff) + 1;
+            s->chunks_left = (s->frame_bytes + s->chunk_len - 1)
+                           / s->chunk_len;
+            s->frame_offset = 0;
+            lcd_cam_capture(s);
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case R_INT_ENA:
+        s->int_ena = v;
+        lcd_cam_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    case R_INT_CLR:
+        s->int_raw &= ~v;
+        lcd_cam_update_irq(s);
+        break;
+    default:
+        if (addr < sizeof(s->regs)) {
+            s->regs[addr / 4] = v;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps lcd_cam_ops = {
+    .read = lcd_cam_read,
+    .write = lcd_cam_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 1,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+static void lcd_cam_reset(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5LcdCamState *s = M5_LCD_CAM(dev);
+    memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+    s->int_ena = 0;
+    s->int_raw = 0;
+    s->frame_offset = 0;
+    s->frame_bytes = 0;
+    s->chunk_len = 0;
+    s->chunks_left = 0;
+    s->started = false;
+    s->dma_armed = false;
+}
+
+static bool frame_auto_increment_get(Object *obj, Error **errp)
+{
+    return M5_LCD_CAM(obj)->frame_auto_increment;
+}
+
+static void frame_auto_increment_set(Object *obj, bool value, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5_LCD_CAM(obj)->frame_auto_increment = value;
+}
+
+static void lcd_cam_init(Object *obj)
+{
+    M5LcdCamState *s = M5_LCD_CAM(obj);
+    SysBusDevice *sbd = SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj);
+    memory_region_init_io(&s->iomem, obj, &lcd_cam_ops, s,
