+                          TYPE_M5_LCD_CAM, 0x1000);
+    sysbus_init_mmio(sbd, &s->iomem);
+    sysbus_init_irq(sbd, &s->irq);
+    object_property_add_uint32_ptr(
+        obj, "frame-seed", &s->frame_seed, OBJ_PROP_FLAG_READWRITE);
+    object_property_add_bool(
+        obj, "auto-increment", frame_auto_increment_get,
+        frame_auto_increment_set);
+    s->capture_timer = timer_new_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL,
+                                    (QEMUTimerCB *)lcd_cam_capture, s);
+}
+
+static void lcd_cam_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    device_class_set_legacy_reset(dc, lcd_cam_reset);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo lcd_cam_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_LCD_CAM,
+    .parent = TYPE_SYS_BUS_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5LcdCamState),
+    .instance_init = lcd_cam_init,
+    .class_init = lcd_cam_class_init,
+};
+
+static void lcd_cam_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&lcd_cam_info);
+}
+type_init(lcd_cam_register)
+
+void m5_lcd_cam_attach(DeviceState *dev, ESPGdmaState *gdma,
+                       uint32_t width, uint32_t height)
+{
+    M5LcdCamState *s = M5_LCD_CAM(dev);
+    object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), "m5-lcd-cam", OBJECT(dev));
+    s->gdma = gdma;
+    s->width = width;
+    s->height = height;
+    esp_gdma_set_start_callback(gdma, GDMA_LCDCAM, ESP_GDMA_IN_IDX,
+                                lcd_cam_gdma_started, s);
+    const char *frame_path = getenv("M5SW_CAMERA_FRAME");
+    if (frame_path) {
+        gsize size = 0;
+        if (!g_file_get_contents(frame_path, (gchar **)&s->frame_data,
+                                 &size, NULL)) {
+            s->frame_data = NULL;
+            size = 0;
+        }
+        s->frame_data_size = MIN(size, UINT32_MAX);
+    }
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_max3421e.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_max3421e.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..21e8e93
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_max3421e.c
@@ -0,0 +1,535 @@
+/*
+ * MAX3421E USB host controller model for the CoreS3 M-Bus.
+ *
+ * UIFlow2 exposes USB HID through a MAX3421E connected to the shared
+ * CoreS3 M-Bus SPI.  The model implements the register/FIFO interface,
+ * USB Chapter 9 enumeration and one interrupt-IN HID mouse endpoint.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "qapi/visitor.h"
+#include "qom/object.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define TYPE_M5_MAX3421E "m5.max3421e"
+OBJECT_DECLARE_SIMPLE_TYPE(M5Max3421eState, M5_MAX3421E)
+
+enum {
+    R_RCVFIFO = 1,
+    R_SNDFIFO = 2,
+    R_SUDFIFO = 4,
+    R_RCVBC = 6,
+    R_SNDBC = 7,
+    R_USBIRQ = 13,
+    R_USBIEN = 14,
+    R_USBCTL = 15,
+    R_CPUCTL = 16,
+    R_PINCTL = 17,
+    R_REVISION = 18,
+    R_IOPINS1 = 20,
+    R_IOPINS2 = 21,
+    R_GPINIRQ = 22,
+    R_GPINIEN = 23,
+    R_GPINPOL = 24,
+    R_HIRQ = 25,
+    R_HIEN = 26,
+    R_MODE = 27,
+    R_PERADDR = 28,
+    R_HCTL = 29,
+    R_HXFR = 30,
+    R_HRSL = 31,
+};
+
+#define USBIRQ_OSCOKIRQ  BIT(0)
+#define HIRQ_BUSEVENTIRQ BIT(0)
+#define HIRQ_RWUIRQ      BIT(1)
+#define HIRQ_RCVDAVIRQ   BIT(2)
+#define HIRQ_SNDBAVIRQ   BIT(3)
+#define HIRQ_SUSDNIRQ    BIT(4)
+#define HIRQ_CONDETIRQ   BIT(5)
+#define HIRQ_FRAMEIRQ    BIT(6)
+#define HIRQ_HXFRDNIRQ   BIT(7)
+
+#define USBCTL_CHIPRES   BIT(5)
+#define HCTL_BUSRST      BIT(0)
+#define HCTL_FRMRST      BIT(1)
+#define HCTL_SAMPLEBUS   BIT(2)
+
+#define TOK_SETUP  0x10
+#define TOK_IN     0x00
+#define TOK_OUT    0x20
+#define TOK_INHS   0x80
+#define TOK_OUTHS  0xA0
+
+#define HRSL_JSTATUS 0x80
+#define HRSL_KSTATUS 0x40
+#define HRSL_NAK     0x04
+
+#define MAX3421E_FIFO_SIZE 64
+
+typedef struct M5Max3421eState {
+    DeviceState parent_obj;
+    M5SpiSlaveOps ops;
+    int irq_pin;
+    int cs_pin;
+    uint8_t regs[32];
+    uint8_t sudfifo[8];
+    uint8_t sndfifo[MAX3421E_FIFO_SIZE];
+    uint8_t rcvfifo[MAX3421E_FIFO_SIZE];
+    uint8_t setup[8];
+    uint8_t control_data[128];
+    uint32_t control_len;
+    uint32_t control_pos;
+    uint32_t control_remaining;
+    uint32_t snd_pos;
+    uint32_t sud_pos;
+    uint32_t rcv_pos;
+    uint32_t rcv_len;
+    uint32_t snd_len;
+    uint32_t token_value;
+    uint32_t current_reg;
+    bool command_byte;
+    bool write_byte;
+    bool control_active;
+    bool control_direction_in;
+    bool control_status;
+    bool configured;
+    bool connected;
+    int64_t mouse_x;
+    int64_t mouse_y;
+    int64_t mouse_buttons;
+    int64_t enumerated;
+    int64_t last_report;
+} M5Max3421eState;
+
+static const uint8_t max3421e_device_descriptor[18] = {
+    18, 0x01, 0x00, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 8,
+    0x3a, 0x30, 0x01, 0x10, 0x00, 0x01,
+    0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
+};
+
+static const uint8_t max3421e_config_descriptor[] = {
+    9, 0x02, 34, 0x00, 1, 1, 0, 0x80, 50,
+    9, 0x04, 0, 0, 1, 0x03, 0x01, 0x02, 0,
+    9, 0x21, 0x11, 0x01, 0x00, 1, 0x22, 52, 0,
+    7, 0x05, 0x81, 0x03, 8, 0x00, 10,
+};
+
+static const uint8_t max3421e_mouse_report[] = {
+    0x05, 0x01, 0x09, 0x02, 0xA1, 0x01, 0x09, 0x01, 0xA1, 0x00,
+    0x05, 0x09, 0x19, 0x01, 0x29, 0x03, 0x15, 0x00, 0x25, 0x01,
+    0x95, 0x03, 0x75, 0x01, 0x81, 0x02, 0x95, 0x01, 0x75, 0x05,
+    0x81, 0x01, 0x05, 0x01, 0x09, 0x30, 0x09, 0x31, 0x15, 0x81,
+    0x25, 0x7F, 0x75, 0x08, 0x95, 0x02, 0x81, 0x06, 0xC0, 0xC0,
+};
+
+static void max3421e_update_irq(M5Max3421eState *s)
+{
+    if (s->irq_pin >= 0) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(
+            s->irq_pin, (s->regs[R_HIRQ] & s->regs[R_HIEN]) ? 0 : 1);
+    }
+}
+
+static void max3421e_set_cs(void *opaque, int level)
+{
+    M5Max3421eState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (!level) {
+        s->command_byte = true;
+        s->current_reg = 0;
+        s->rcv_pos = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t max3421e_read_reg(M5Max3421eState *s, uint32_t reg)
+{
+    if (reg == R_RCVFIFO) {
+        if (s->rcv_pos >= s->rcv_len) {
+            return 0;
+        }
+        return s->rcvfifo[s->rcv_pos++];
+    }
+    if (reg == R_SNDFIFO || reg == R_SUDFIFO) {
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (reg == R_RCVBC) {
+        return s->rcv_len;
+    }
+    if (reg == R_SNDBC) {
+        return s->snd_len;
+    }
+    if (reg == R_HRSL) {
+        return s->connected ? HRSL_JSTATUS : 0;
+    }
+    if (reg == R_USBIRQ) {
+        return s->regs[R_USBIRQ] | USBIRQ_OSCOKIRQ;
+    }
+    return reg < ARRAY_SIZE(s->regs) ? s->regs[reg] : 0;
+}
+
+static void max3421e_prepare_control(M5Max3421eState *s)
+{
+    const uint8_t bm_request_type = s->setup[0];
+    const uint8_t request = s->setup[1];
+    const uint8_t value_hi = s->setup[3];
+    const uint16_t length = s->setup[6] | ((uint16_t)s->setup[7] << 8);
+
+    s->control_len = 0;
+    s->control_pos = 0;
+    s->control_remaining = 0;
+    s->control_direction_in = !!(bm_request_type & 0x80);
+    s->control_status = false;
+    s->control_active = true;
+
+    if (request == 0x06) {
+        if (value_hi == 0x01) {
+            s->control_len = sizeof(max3421e_device_descriptor);
+            memcpy(s->control_data, max3421e_device_descriptor,
+                   s->control_len);
+        } else if (value_hi == 0x02) {
+            s->control_len = sizeof(max3421e_config_descriptor);
+            memcpy(s->control_data, max3421e_config_descriptor,
+                   s->control_len);
+        } else if (value_hi == 0x22) {
+            s->control_len = sizeof(max3421e_mouse_report);
+            memcpy(s->control_data, max3421e_mouse_report,
+                   s->control_len);
+        }
+    } else if (request == 0x00) {
+        s->control_len = 2;
+        memset(s->control_data, 0, s->control_len);
+    } else if (request == 0x08) {
+        s->control_len = 1;
+        s->control_data[0] = s->configured ? 1 : 0;
+    }
+
+    if (s->control_len > length) {
+        s->control_len = length;
+    }
+    s->control_remaining = s->control_len;
+    if (!s->control_remaining) {
+        s->control_status = true;
+    }
+}
+
+static void max3421e_fill_control(M5Max3421eState *s)
+{
+    uint32_t chunk;
+
+    if (!s->control_active || !s->control_direction_in ||
+        s->control_status || !s->control_remaining) {
+        s->rcv_len = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    chunk = MIN(s->control_remaining, 8);
+    memcpy(s->rcvfifo, s->control_data + s->control_pos, chunk);
+    s->rcv_len = chunk;
+    s->control_pos += chunk;
+    s->control_remaining -= chunk;
+    if (!s->control_remaining) {
+        s->control_status = true;
+    }
+}
+
+static void max3421e_fill_hid_report(M5Max3421eState *s)
+{
+    if (!s->connected || !s->configured ||
+        (!s->mouse_buttons && !s->mouse_x && !s->mouse_y)) {
+        s->rcv_len = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    s->rcvfifo[0] = s->mouse_buttons & 0xff;
+    s->rcvfifo[1] = s->mouse_x & 0xff;
+    s->rcvfifo[2] = s->mouse_y & 0xff;
+    s->rcvfifo[3] = 0;
+    s->rcv_len = 4;
+    s->last_report = 1;
+}
+
+static void max3421e_process_token(M5Max3421eState *s, uint32_t value)
+{
+    const uint32_t token = value & 0xf0;
+    const uint32_t ep = value & 0x0f;
+
+    s->token_value = value;
+    if (getenv("M5SW_MAX3421E_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[max3421e] token=%02x ep=%u setup=%02x/%02x/"
+                "%02x%02x len=%u\n",
+                token, ep, s->setup[0], s->setup[1],
+                s->setup[3], s->setup[2],
+                s->setup[6] | (s->setup[7] << 8));
+    }
+    s->regs[R_HIRQ] |= HIRQ_HXFRDNIRQ;
+    s->regs[R_HRSL] = s->connected ? HRSL_JSTATUS : 0;
+
+    if (token == TOK_SETUP) {
+        memcpy(s->setup, s->sudfifo, sizeof(s->setup));
+        s->sud_pos = 0;
+        max3421e_prepare_control(s);
+    } else if (token == TOK_IN || token == TOK_INHS) {
+        if (s->control_active && s->control_direction_in &&
+            !s->control_status) {
+            max3421e_fill_control(s);
+        } else if (s->control_active && s->control_status &&
+                   !s->control_direction_in) {
+            s->control_active = false;
+            if (s->setup[1] == 0x09 && s->setup[2] == 1) {
+                s->configured = true;
+                s->enumerated = 1;
+            }
+            s->rcv_len = 0;
+        } else if (!s->control_active && ep == 1) {
+            max3421e_fill_hid_report(s);
+        } else {
+            s->rcv_len = 0;
+        }
+        if (s->rcv_len) {
+            s->regs[R_HIRQ] |= HIRQ_RCVDAVIRQ;
+        } else {
+            s->regs[R_HIRQ] &= ~HIRQ_RCVDAVIRQ;
+        }
+    } else if (token == TOK_OUT || token == TOK_OUTHS) {
+        if (s->control_active && s->control_status) {
+            s->control_active = false;
+            if (s->setup[1] == 0x09 && s->setup[2] == 1) {
+                s->configured = true;
+            }
+        }
+        s->snd_pos = 0;
+    }
+    max3421e_update_irq(s);
+}
+
+static void max3421e_write_reg(M5Max3421eState *s, uint32_t reg, uint8_t value)
+{
+    switch (reg) {
+    case R_RCVFIFO:
+        break;
+    case R_SNDFIFO:
+        if (s->snd_pos < sizeof(s->sndfifo)) {
+            s->sndfifo[s->snd_pos++] = value;
+        }
+        break;
+    case R_SUDFIFO:
+        if (s->sud_pos >= sizeof(s->sudfifo)) {
+            s->sud_pos = 0;
+        }
+        s->sudfifo[s->sud_pos++] = value;
+        break;
+    case R_SNDBC:
+        s->snd_len = value;
+        break;
+    case R_RCVBC:
+        break;
+    case R_USBIRQ:
+        s->regs[R_USBIRQ] &= ~value;
+        break;
+    case R_USBIEN:
+    case R_CPUCTL:
+    case R_PINCTL:
+    case R_IOPINS1:
+    case R_IOPINS2:
+    case R_GPINIRQ:
+    case R_GPINIEN:
+    case R_GPINPOL:
+    case R_PERADDR:
+        s->regs[reg] = value;
+        break;
+    case R_USBCTL:
+        if (value & USBCTL_CHIPRES) {
+            s->regs[R_USBIRQ] = USBIRQ_OSCOKIRQ;
+            s->regs[R_HIRQ] = HIRQ_CONDETIRQ;
+            s->configured = false;
+            s->control_active = false;
+        }
+        s->regs[reg] = value & ~USBCTL_CHIPRES;
+        break;
+    case R_HIRQ:
+        s->regs[R_HIRQ] &= ~value;
+        break;
+    case R_HIEN:
+        s->regs[R_HIEN] = value;
+        break;
+    case R_MODE:
+        s->regs[R_MODE] = value;
+        if ((value & 0x09) == 0x09) {
+            s->regs[R_HIRQ] |= HIRQ_FRAMEIRQ;
+        }
+        break;
+    case R_HCTL:
+        if (value & HCTL_SAMPLEBUS) {
+            s->regs[R_HRSL] = s->connected ? HRSL_JSTATUS : 0;
+        }
+        if (value & HCTL_BUSRST) {
+            s->regs[R_HIRQ] |= HIRQ_BUSEVENTIRQ;
+        }
+        /* SAMPLEBUS is visible to the driver as a one-shot request bit. */
+        s->regs[R_HCTL] = value & ~HCTL_BUSRST;
+        break;
+    case R_HXFR:
+        max3421e_process_token(s, value);
+        s->regs[R_HXFR] = 0;
+        break;
+    default:
+        if (reg < ARRAY_SIZE(s->regs)) {
+            s->regs[reg] = value;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    max3421e_update_irq(s);
+}
+
+static uint8_t max3421e_transfer(void *opaque, uint8_t tx)
+{
+    M5Max3421eState *s = opaque;
+    uint8_t ret;
+
+    if (s->command_byte) {
+        const uint32_t command = tx;
+        s->current_reg = (command >> 3) & 0x1f;
+        s->write_byte = !!(command & 0x02);
+        s->command_byte = false;
+        if (s->current_reg == R_RCVFIFO) {
+            s->rcv_pos = 0;
+        }
+        if (getenv("M5SW_MAX3421E_TRACE")) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[max3421e] cmd=%02x reg=%u %s\n", command,
+                    s->current_reg, s->write_byte ? "wr" : "rd");
+        }
+        return 0;
+    }
+    if (s->write_byte) {
+        if (getenv("M5SW_MAX3421E_TRACE")) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[max3421e] wr reg=%u val=%02x\n",
+                    s->current_reg, tx);
+        }
+        max3421e_write_reg(s, s->current_reg, tx);
+        return 0;
+    }
+    ret = max3421e_read_reg(s, s->current_reg);
+    if (getenv("M5SW_MAX3421E_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[max3421e] rd reg=%u val=%02x\n",
+                s->current_reg, ret);
+    }
+    return ret;
+}
+
+static void max3421e_reset(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5Max3421eState *s = M5_MAX3421E(dev);
+
+    memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+    memset(s->sudfifo, 0, sizeof(s->sudfifo));
+    memset(s->sndfifo, 0, sizeof(s->sndfifo));
+    memset(s->rcvfifo, 0, sizeof(s->rcvfifo));
+    memset(s->setup, 0, sizeof(s->setup));
+    memset(s->control_data, 0, sizeof(s->control_data));
+    s->regs[R_USBIRQ] = USBIRQ_OSCOKIRQ;
+    s->regs[R_REVISION] = 0x13;
+    s->regs[R_HIRQ] = HIRQ_CONDETIRQ;
+    s->regs[R_HRSL] = HRSL_JSTATUS;
+    s->regs[R_IOPINS2] = 0xf0;
+    s->control_len = 0;
+    s->control_pos = 0;
+    s->control_remaining = 0;
+    s->snd_pos = 0;
+    s->sud_pos = 0;
+    s->rcv_pos = 0;
+    s->rcv_len = 0;
+    s->snd_len = 0;
+    s->token_value = 0;
+    s->current_reg = 0;
+    s->command_byte = true;
+    s->write_byte = false;
+    s->control_active = false;
+    s->control_direction_in = false;
+    s->control_status = false;
+    s->configured = false;
+    s->enumerated = 0;
+    s->last_report = 0;
+    max3421e_update_irq(s);
+}
+
+static void max3421e_prop_get_i64(Object *obj, Visitor *v, const char *name,
+                                  void *opaque, Error **errp)
+{
+    visit_type_int64(v, name, (int64_t *)opaque, errp);
+}
+
+static void max3421e_prop_set_i64(Object *obj, Visitor *v, const char *name,
+                                  void *opaque, Error **errp)
+{
+    int64_t value;
+
+    if (visit_type_int64(v, name, &value, errp)) {
+        *(int64_t *)opaque = value;
+    }
+}
+
+static void max3421e_init(Object *obj)
+{
+    M5Max3421eState *s = M5_MAX3421E(obj);
+
+    s->irq_pin = -1;
+    s->cs_pin = -1;
+    s->connected = true;
+    object_property_add(obj, "mouse-x", "int", max3421e_prop_get_i64,
+                        max3421e_prop_set_i64, NULL, &s->mouse_x);
+    object_property_add(obj, "mouse-y", "int", max3421e_prop_get_i64,
+                        max3421e_prop_set_i64, NULL, &s->mouse_y);
+    object_property_add(obj, "mouse-buttons", "int",
+                        max3421e_prop_get_i64, max3421e_prop_set_i64,
+                        NULL, &s->mouse_buttons);
+    object_property_add(obj, "enumerated", "int",
+                        max3421e_prop_get_i64, NULL, NULL, &s->enumerated);
+    object_property_add(obj, "last-report", "int",
+                        max3421e_prop_get_i64, NULL, NULL, &s->last_report);
+}
+
+static void max3421e_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+
+    device_class_set_legacy_reset(dc, max3421e_reset);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo max3421e_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_MAX3421E,
+    .parent = TYPE_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5Max3421eState),
+    .instance_init = max3421e_init,
+    .class_init = max3421e_class_init,
+};
+
+static M5SpiSlaveOps max3421e_ops = {
+    .set_cs = max3421e_set_cs,
+    .transfer = max3421e_transfer,
+};
+
+void m5_max3421e_attach(DeviceState *spi, int cs_pin, int irq_pin)
+{
+    DeviceState *dev = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_MAX3421E);
+    M5Max3421eState *s;
+
+    object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), "max3421e-usb",
+                              OBJECT(dev));
+    qdev_realize(dev, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    s = M5_MAX3421E(dev);
+    s->irq_pin = irq_pin;
+    s->cs_pin = cs_pin;
+    s->ops = max3421e_ops;
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_spi_slave(spi, &s->ops, s, cs_pin);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(irq_pin, 1);
+    esp32_gpio_force_line(irq_pin, 1);
+}
+
+static void max3421e_register_types(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&max3421e_info);
+}
+
+type_init(max3421e_register_types)
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_rmt.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_rmt.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2fd3c85
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_rmt.c
@@ -0,0 +1,284 @@
+/*
+ * ESP32-S3 RMT APB and hardware-memory model.
+ *
+ * The UIFlow2 IR stack uses the new RMT driver in non-DMA mode: the encoder
+ * writes rmt_symbol_word_t entries into RMTMEM, starts the channel through
+ * CHnCONF0, and waits for the TX_END interrupt. This model implements that
+ * data path and records transmitted symbols for host-side assertions.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "hw/irq.h"
+#include "hw/misc/esp32s3_reg.h"
+#include "hw/sysbus.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define RMT_MEM_OFFSET      0x800
+#define RMT_MEM_SIZE        0x800
+#define RMT_TX_CHANNELS     4
+#define RMT_SYMBOLS_PER_CH  48
+
+#define RMT_CH0CONF0        0x20
+#define RMT_CH4CONF0        0x30
+#define RMT_CH4STATUS       0x60
+#define RMT_INT_RAW         0x70
+#define RMT_INT_ST          0x74
+#define RMT_INT_ENA         0x78
+#define RMT_INT_CLR         0x7c
+
+struct M5RmtState {
+    SysBusDevice parent_obj;
+    MemoryRegion mmio;
+    MemoryRegion mem;
+    qemu_irq irq;
+    uint32_t regs[RMT_MEM_OFFSET / 4];
+    uint32_t symbols[RMT_MEM_SIZE / 4];
+    uint32_t int_ena;
+    uint32_t int_raw;
+    QEMUTimer *tx_timer;
+    QEMUTimer *rx_timer;
+    FILE *tx_file;
+    uint32_t pending_mask;
+    uint32_t rx_pending_mask;
+    bool rx_injected[4];
+    bool trace;
+};
+
+static void rmt_update_irq(M5RmtState *s)
+{
+    qemu_set_irq(s->irq, !!(s->int_raw & s->int_ena));
+}
+
+static void rmt_capture_tx(M5RmtState *s, uint32_t channel)
+{
+    uint32_t conf = s->regs[(RMT_CH0CONF0 + channel * 4) / 4];
+    uint32_t blocks = MAX((conf >> 16) & 0xf, 1);
+    uint32_t count = 0;
+    uint32_t limit = MIN(blocks * RMT_SYMBOLS_PER_CH,
+                         RMT_SYMBOLS_PER_CH);
+    uint32_t base = channel * RMT_SYMBOLS_PER_CH;
+
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < limit; i++) {
+        uint32_t symbol = s->symbols[base + i];
+        if ((symbol & 0x7fff) == 0
+            && ((symbol >> 16) & 0x7fff) == 0) {
+            break;
+        }
+        count++;
+    }
+    if (!s->tx_file) {
+        const char *path = getenv("M5SW_RMT_TX_FILE");
+        if (path) {
+            s->tx_file = fopen(path, "wb");
+        }
+    }
+    if (s->tx_file && count) {
+        fwrite(&s->symbols[base], sizeof(uint32_t), count, s->tx_file);
+        fflush(s->tx_file);
+    }
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[rmt] tx channel=%u symbols=%u blocks=%u\n",
+                channel, count, blocks);
+    }
+
+    s->int_raw |= 1u << channel;
+    rmt_update_irq(s);
+}
+
+static void rmt_tx_timer(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5RmtState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t pending = s->pending_mask;
+    s->pending_mask = 0;
+
+    for (uint32_t channel = 0; channel < RMT_TX_CHANNELS; channel++) {
+        if (pending & (1u << channel)) {
+            rmt_capture_tx(s, channel);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void rmt_rx_timer(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5RmtState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t pending = s->rx_pending_mask;
+    s->rx_pending_mask = 0;
+
+    for (uint32_t channel = 0; channel < 4; channel++) {
+        if (pending & (1u << channel)) {
+            s->int_raw |= 1u << (16 + channel);
+        }
+    }
+    rmt_update_irq(s);
+}
+
+static void rmt_inject_rx(M5RmtState *s, uint32_t channel)
+{
+    const char *path = getenv("M5SW_RMT_RX_FILE");
+    g_autofree uint8_t *data = NULL;
+    gsize size = 0;
+    uint32_t count;
+
+    if (!path || s->rx_injected[channel]) {
+        return;
+    }
+    if (!g_file_get_contents(path, (gchar **)&data, &size, NULL)) {
+        return;
+    }
+    count = MIN(size / sizeof(uint32_t), RMT_SYMBOLS_PER_CH);
+    memcpy(&s->symbols[(4 + channel) * RMT_SYMBOLS_PER_CH],
+           data, count * sizeof(uint32_t));
+    /* mem_waddr_ex is an absolute RMTMEM word address; each channel
+     * occupies 48 words on ESP32-S3 and starts at (channel + 4) * 48. */
+    s->regs[(RMT_CH4STATUS + channel * 4) / 4] =
+        (channel + 4) * 48 + count;
+    s->rx_injected[channel] = true;
+    s->rx_pending_mask |= 1u << channel;
+    timer_mod(s->rx_timer,
+              qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 20);
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[rmt] rx inject channel=%u symbols=%u\n",
+                channel, count);
+    }
+}
+
+static uint64_t rmt_read(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, unsigned size)
+{
+    M5RmtState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (addr == RMT_INT_RAW) {
+        return s->int_raw;
+    }
+    if (addr == RMT_INT_ST) {
+        return s->int_raw & s->int_ena;
+    }
+    if (addr == RMT_INT_ENA) {
+        return s->int_ena;
+    }
+    return addr < RMT_MEM_OFFSET ? s->regs[addr / 4] : 0;
+}
+
+static void rmt_write(void *opaque, hwaddr addr, uint64_t value,
+                      unsigned size)
+{
+    M5RmtState *s = opaque;
+    uint32_t v = value;
+
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[rmt] wr %03x <- %08x\n", (unsigned)addr, v);
+    }
+    if (addr == RMT_INT_ENA) {
+        s->int_ena = v;
+        rmt_update_irq(s);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (addr == RMT_INT_CLR) {
+        s->int_raw &= ~v;
+        rmt_update_irq(s);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (addr >= RMT_CH0CONF0 && addr < RMT_CH0CONF0 + RMT_TX_CHANNELS * 4) {
+        uint32_t channel = (addr - RMT_CH0CONF0) / 4;
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = v & ~1u;
+        if (v & 1u) {
+            s->pending_mask |= 1u << channel;
+            timer_mod(s->tx_timer,
+                      qemu_clock_get_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME) + 2);
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+    if (addr >= RMT_CH4CONF0 && addr < RMT_CH4CONF0 + 4 * 8) {
+        uint32_t offset = addr - RMT_CH4CONF0;
+        uint32_t channel = offset / 8;
+        if ((offset % 8) == 4) {
+            bool was_enabled = s->regs[addr / 4] & 1u;
+            s->regs[addr / 4] = v;
+            if (!was_enabled && (v & 1u)) {
+                rmt_inject_rx(s, channel);
+            }
+            return;
+        }
+    }
+    if (addr < RMT_MEM_OFFSET) {
+        s->regs[addr / 4] = v;
+    }
+}
+
+static const MemoryRegionOps rmt_ops = {
+    .read = rmt_read,
+    .write = rmt_write,
+    .endianness = DEVICE_LITTLE_ENDIAN,
+    .valid.min_access_size = 4,
+    .valid.max_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.min_access_size = 4,
+    .impl.max_access_size = 4,
+};
+
+static void rmt_reset(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5RmtState *s = M5_RMT(dev);
+    memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+    memset(s->symbols, 0, sizeof(s->symbols));
+    s->int_ena = 0;
+    s->int_raw = 0;
+    s->pending_mask = 0;
+    s->rx_pending_mask = 0;
+    memset(s->rx_injected, 0, sizeof(s->rx_injected));
+    timer_del(s->tx_timer);
+    timer_del(s->rx_timer);
+}
+
+static void rmt_init(Object *obj)
+{
+    M5RmtState *s = M5_RMT(obj);
+    SysBusDevice *sbd = SYS_BUS_DEVICE(obj);
+
+    memory_region_init_io(&s->mmio, obj, &rmt_ops, s, TYPE_M5_RMT,
+                          RMT_MEM_OFFSET);
+    memory_region_init_ram_ptr(&s->mem, obj, TYPE_M5_RMT ".mem",
+                               RMT_MEM_SIZE, s->symbols);
+    sysbus_init_mmio(sbd, &s->mmio);
+    sysbus_init_mmio(sbd, &s->mem);
+    sysbus_init_irq(sbd, &s->irq);
+    s->trace = getenv("M5SW_RMT_TRACE") != NULL;
+    s->tx_timer = timer_new_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME, rmt_tx_timer, s);
+    s->rx_timer = timer_new_ms(QEMU_CLOCK_REALTIME, rmt_rx_timer, s);
+}
+
+static void rmt_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    device_class_set_legacy_reset(dc, rmt_reset);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo rmt_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_RMT,
+    .parent = TYPE_SYS_BUS_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5RmtState),
+    .instance_init = rmt_init,
+    .class_init = rmt_class_init,
+};
+
+static void rmt_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&rmt_info);
+}
+type_init(rmt_register)
+
+void esp32s3_rmt_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix)
+{
+    DeviceState *dev = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_RMT);
+    object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), "rmt0", OBJECT(dev));
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, DR_REG_RMT_BASE,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 0), 1);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, DR_REG_RMT_BASE + RMT_MEM_OFFSET,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 1), 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_RMT_INTR_SOURCE));
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_scs_servo.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_scs_servo.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..dc40359
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_scs_servo.c
@@ -0,0 +1,205 @@
+/*
+ * Minimal SCS serial servo bus peer for StackChan.
+ *
+ * The guest UART bytes are parsed as SCSCL packets. Servo ID 1/2 expose the
+ * EEPROM/SRAM fields used by the UIFlow2 driver, including position, torque,
+ * voltage, current and PWM-mode angle limits.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/char/esp32_uart.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define SCS_MAX_PACKET 256
+#define SCS_MEM_SIZE 71
+#define SCS_ID_X 1
+#define SCS_ID_Y 2
+
+typedef struct M5ScsServoState {
+    ESP32UARTState *uart;
+    uint8_t packet[SCS_MAX_PACKET];
+    uint16_t packet_len;
+    uint8_t mem[3][SCS_MEM_SIZE];
+} M5ScsServoState;
+
+static void scs_put_be16(uint8_t *mem, int addr, uint16_t value)
+{
+    mem[addr] = value >> 8;
+    mem[addr + 1] = value;
+}
+
+static uint16_t scs_get_be16(const uint8_t *mem, int addr)
+{
+    return ((uint16_t)mem[addr] << 8) | mem[addr + 1];
+}
+
+static void scs_init_mem(M5ScsServoState *s)
+{
+    for (int id = SCS_ID_X; id <= SCS_ID_Y; id++) {
+        uint8_t *m = s->mem[id];
+        memset(m, 0, SCS_MEM_SIZE);
+        m[3] = 0x01;                         /* version low */
+        m[4] = 0x01;                         /* version high */
+        m[5] = id;
+        m[6] = 1;                            /* baud-rate selector */
+        scs_put_be16(m, 9, 0);               /* min angle limit */
+        scs_put_be16(m, 11, 1000);           /* max angle limit */
+        m[40] = 0;                           /* torque disabled */
+        scs_put_be16(m, 42, id == SCS_ID_X ? 450 : 125);
+        scs_put_be16(m, 56, id == SCS_ID_X ? 450 : 125);
+        m[62] = 120;                         /* 12.0 V */
+        m[63] = 35;                          /* 35 C */
+        m[66] = 0;
+        scs_put_be16(m, 69, 0);
+    }
+}
+
+static void scs_send(M5ScsServoState *s, uint8_t id, uint8_t error,
+                     const uint8_t *data, uint8_t data_len)
+{
+    uint8_t out[SCS_MAX_PACKET];
+    uint8_t len = data_len + 2;
+    uint8_t sum = 0;
+    int pos = 0;
+
+    out[pos++] = 0xff;
+    out[pos++] = 0xff;
+    out[pos++] = id;
+    out[pos++] = len;
+    out[pos++] = error;
+    sum = id + len + error;
+    for (int i = 0; i < data_len; i++) {
+        out[pos++] = data[i];
+        sum += data[i];
+    }
+    out[pos++] = ~sum;
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SCS_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[scs] response id=%u err=%u len=%u\n",
+                id, error, data_len);
+    }
+    esp32_uart_inject_rx(s->uart, out, pos);
+}
+
+static uint8_t *scs_mem_for(M5ScsServoState *s, uint8_t id)
+{
+    if (id != SCS_ID_X && id != SCS_ID_Y) {
+        return NULL;
+    }
+    return s->mem[id];
+}
+
+static void scs_handle_packet(M5ScsServoState *s)
+{
+    uint8_t id = s->packet[2];
+    uint8_t len = s->packet[3];
+    uint8_t inst = s->packet[4];
+    uint8_t addr = s->packet[5];
+    uint8_t data_len;
+    uint8_t *mem = scs_mem_for(s, id);
+
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SCS_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[scs] packet id=%u inst=%02x len=%u addr=%u\n",
+                id, inst, len, addr);
+    }
+
+    if (inst == 0x01) { /* PING */
+        if (id != 0xfe) {
+            scs_send(s, id, 0, NULL, 0);
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+    if (inst == 0x02) { /* READ */
+        uint8_t response[SCS_MEM_SIZE];
+        data_len = s->packet[6];
+        if (!mem || data_len > sizeof(response) || addr + data_len > SCS_MEM_SIZE) {
+            return;
+        }
+        memcpy(response, mem + addr, data_len);
+        scs_send(s, id, 0, response, data_len);
+        return;
+    }
+    if (inst == 0x03 || inst == 0x04) { /* WRITE / REG_WRITE */
+        data_len = len - 3;
+        if (mem && data_len && addr + data_len <= SCS_MEM_SIZE) {
+            memcpy(mem + addr, s->packet + 6, data_len);
+            if (addr <= 43 && addr + data_len > 42) {
+                scs_put_be16(mem, 56, scs_get_be16(mem, 42));
+            }
+            if (id != 0xfe) {
+                scs_send(s, id, 0, NULL, 0);
+            }
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+    if (inst == 0x05) { /* REG_ACTION */
+        return;
+    }
+    if (inst == 0x83) { /* SYNC_WRITE */
+        data_len = s->packet[6];
+        int count = (len - 4) / (data_len + 1);
+        int pos = 7;
+        for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
+            uint8_t sid = s->packet[pos++];
+            uint8_t *sm = scs_mem_for(s, sid);
+            if (sm && data_len && addr + data_len <= SCS_MEM_SIZE) {
+                memcpy(sm + addr, s->packet + pos, data_len);
+                if (addr <= 43 && addr + data_len > 42) {
+                    scs_put_be16(sm, 56, scs_get_be16(sm, 42));
+                }
+            }
+            pos += data_len;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+static void scs_uart_byte(void *opaque, uint8_t byte)
+{
+    M5ScsServoState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (s->packet_len < sizeof(s->packet)) {
+        s->packet[s->packet_len++] = byte;
+    } else {
+        s->packet_len = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (s->packet_len == 1 && s->packet[0] != 0xff) {
+        s->packet_len = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    if (s->packet_len == 2 && s->packet[1] != 0xff) {
+        s->packet_len = s->packet[1] == 0xff ? 1 : 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    if (s->packet_len < 4) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    uint8_t payload_len = s->packet[3];
+    uint16_t total = payload_len + 4;
+    if (payload_len < 2 || total > sizeof(s->packet)) {
+        s->packet_len = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+    if (s->packet_len < total) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    uint8_t sum = 0;
+    for (int i = 2; i < total - 1; i++) {
+        sum += s->packet[i];
+    }
+    if ((uint8_t)~sum == s->packet[total - 1]) {
+        scs_handle_packet(s);
+    }
+    s->packet_len = 0;
+}
+
+void m5_scs_servo_attach(DeviceState *uart)
+{
+    M5ScsServoState *s = g_new0(M5ScsServoState, 1);
+    s->uart = ESP32_UART(uart);
+    scs_init_mem(s);
+    esp32_uart_set_tx_byte_callback(s->uart, scs_uart_byte, s);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_spi_panel.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_spi_panel.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..dd0bf03
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_spi_panel.c
@@ -0,0 +1,707 @@
+/*
+ * Generic SPI display panel for M5Stack ESP32-S3 boards.
+ *
+ * The StopWatch uses a QSPI CO5300 panel.  StickS3/AtomS3R/CoreS3 use
+ * ordinary SPI panels (ST7789/GC9107/ILI9342) with the command/data
+ * distinction carried by the GP-SPI command and MOSI phases.
+ *
+ * This model implements the common MIPI-DCS command subset used by
+ * M5GFX: CASET, RASET, RAMWR, MADCTL, COLMOD, sleep/display on/off and
+ * inversion.  It keeps a controller-sized RGB565 framebuffer and exposes
+ * the visible window (panel width/height with controller offsets) through
+ * a QEMU console.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "qemu/main-loop.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-core.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "ui/console.h"
+#include "ui/input.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+static unsigned m5_panel_trace_count;
+
+static void m5_panel_update(void *opaque);
+
+struct M5SpiPanelState {
+    DeviceState parent_obj;
+    QemuConsole *con;
+    QEMUTimer *refresh_timer;
+    QEMUBH *refresh_bh;
+    uint16_t *mem;
+    uint32_t mem_w, mem_h;
+    uint32_t vis_w, vis_h;
+    uint32_t off_x, off_y;
+    bool physical_invert;
+    bool controller_invert;
+    bool dirty;
+    bool disp_on;
+    bool sleep;
+    uint8_t madctl;
+    uint8_t colmod;
+    uint8_t cmd;
+    uint8_t params[4];
+    unsigned pidx;
+    uint16_t xs, xe, ys, ye, cx, cy;
+    bool pixel_mode;
+    uint8_t pix_hi;
+    bool pix_half;
+    uint32_t key_a_pin, key_b_pin;
+    uint32_t read_id;
+    uint32_t glass_rotation;
+    bool apply_madctl;
+    bool touch;
+    uint32_t touch_int_pin;
+    bool immediate_update;
+    bool keyboard;
+    bool plc_keys;
+    bool infer_command;
+    uint32_t infer_params_left;
+    uint32_t pending_pixel_bytes;
+    int mouse_x, mouse_y;
+    bool mouse_btn;
+    char *dump_path;
+};
+
+static bool m5_panel_is_dcs_command(uint8_t cmd)
+{
+    switch (cmd) {
+    case 0x01:
+    case 0x10:
+    case 0x11:
+    case 0x13:
+    case 0x20:
+    case 0x21:
+    case 0x28:
+    case 0x29:
+    case 0x2A:
+    case 0x2B:
+    case 0x2C:
+    case 0x36:
+    case 0x3A:
+    case 0xB1:
+    case 0xB2:
+    case 0xB3:
+    case 0xB4:
+    case 0xB6:
+    case 0xC0:
+    case 0xC1:
+    case 0xC2:
+    case 0xC3:
+    case 0xC4:
+    case 0xC5:
+    case 0xE0:
+    case 0xE1:
+    case 0xFC:
+        return true;
+    default:
+        return false;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint32_t m5_panel_dcs_param_count(uint8_t cmd)
+{
+    switch (cmd) {
+    case 0x2A:
+    case 0x2B:
+        return 4;
+    case 0xB1:
+    case 0xB2:
+    case 0xC0:
+        return 3;
+    case 0xB3:
+        return 6;
+    case 0xB4:
+    case 0xC1:
+    case 0xC5:
+        return 1;
+    case 0xB6:
+    case 0xC2:
+    case 0xC3:
+    case 0xC4:
+    case 0xFC:
+        return 2;
+    case 0xE0:
+    case 0xE1:
+        return 16;
+    case 0x36:
+    case 0x3A:
+        return 1;
+    default:
+        return 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_reset_window(M5SpiPanelState *s)
+{
+    s->cx = s->xs;
+    s->cy = s->ys;
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_put_pixel(M5SpiPanelState *s, uint16_t px)
+{
+    static unsigned pixel_trace_count;
+    int x = s->cx, y = s->cy;
+    if (s->apply_madctl) {
+        if (s->madctl & 0x20) { int t = x; x = y; y = t; }
+        if (s->madctl & 0x40) { x = s->mem_w - 1 - x; }
+        if (s->madctl & 0x80) { y = s->mem_h - 1 - y; }
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_PIXEL_TRACE") &&
+        (px == 0xf800 || px == 0x001f || px == 0x07e0)
+        && pixel_trace_count++ < 500) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-pixel] (%d,%d)=%04x window=(%u,%u)-(%u,%u)\n",
+                x, y, px, s->xs, s->ys, s->xe, s->ye);
+    }
+    if (x >= 0 && x < (int)s->mem_w && y >= 0 && y < (int)s->mem_h) {
+        s->mem[y * s->mem_w + x] = px;
+    }
+    if (++s->cx > s->xe) {
+        s->cx = s->xs;
+        if (++s->cy > s->ye) {
+            s->cy = s->ys;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void m5_spi_panel_write(M5SpiPanelState *s, const uint8_t *buf, uint32_t len,
+                        bool command_phase)
+{
+    static unsigned stream_trace_count;
+    static unsigned stream_trace_group;
+    static unsigned dc_trace_count;
+    if (!command_phase && s->infer_command && len == 1
+        && s->infer_params_left == 0 && s->pending_pixel_bytes == 0
+        && m5_panel_is_dcs_command(buf[0])) {
+        command_phase = true;
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_DC_TRACE") && len && dc_trace_count++ < 1000) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-dc] phase=%d len=%u first=%02x\n",
+                command_phase, len, buf[0]);
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_TRACE") && m5_panel_trace_count < 10000) {
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len && m5_panel_trace_count < 10000; i++) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel] %c %02x\n",
+                    command_phase ? 'C' : 'D', buf[i]);
+            m5_panel_trace_count++;
+        }
+    }
+    if (!command_phase && s->cmd == 0x2c && getenv("M5SW_PANEL_STREAM_TRACE")
+        && stream_trace_count < 6000) {
+        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len && stream_trace_count < 6000; i++) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-stream] %02x\n", buf[i]);
+            stream_trace_count++;
+        }
+    }
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
+        uint8_t b = buf[i];
+        if (command_phase) {
+            s->infer_params_left = m5_panel_dcs_param_count(b);
+            s->pending_pixel_bytes = 0;
+            if (b == 0x2c) {
+                stream_trace_count = 0;
+                if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_STREAM_TRACE")) {
+                    fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-stream-group] %u\n",
+                            stream_trace_group++);
+                }
+            }
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_WINDOW_TRACE") && (b == 0x2A || b == 0x2B)) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-window] cmd=%02x\n", b);
+            }
+            s->cmd = b;
+            s->pidx = 0;
+            s->pixel_mode = false;
+            switch (b) {
+            case 0x28: s->disp_on = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x29: s->disp_on = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x10: s->sleep = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x11: s->sleep = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x20: s->controller_invert = false; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x21: s->controller_invert = true; s->dirty = true; break;
+            case 0x01:
+                s->disp_on = false;
+                s->sleep = true;
+                s->madctl = 0;
+                s->controller_invert = false;
+                break;
+            default:
+                break;
+            }
+            continue;
+        }
+
+        switch (s->cmd) {
+        case 0x2A:
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_WINDOW_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-window] 2a[%u]=%02x\n", s->pidx, b);
+            }
+            if (s->pidx < 4) {
+                s->params[s->pidx++] = b;
+            }
+            if (s->pidx == 4) {
+                s->xs = (s->params[0] << 8) | s->params[1];
+                s->xe = (s->params[2] << 8) | s->params[3];
+            }
+            break;
+        case 0x2B:
+            if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_WINDOW_TRACE")) {
+                fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-window] 2b[%u]=%02x\n", s->pidx, b);
+            }
+            if (s->pidx < 4) {
+                s->params[s->pidx++] = b;
+            }
+            if (s->pidx == 4) {
+                s->ys = (s->params[0] << 8) | s->params[1];
+                s->ye = (s->params[2] << 8) | s->params[3];
+            }
+            break;
+        case 0x2C:
+        case 0x3C:
+            if (!s->pixel_mode) {
+                m5_panel_reset_window(s);
+                s->pixel_mode = true;
+                s->pix_half = false;
+                    s->pending_pixel_bytes =
+                        (uint32_t)(s->xe - s->xs + 1) *
+                        (uint32_t)(s->ye - s->ys + 1) * 2;
+            }
+            if (!s->pix_half) {
+                s->pix_hi = b;
+                s->pix_half = true;
+            } else {
+                m5_panel_put_pixel(s, (s->pix_hi << 8) | b);
+                s->pix_half = false;
+                s->dirty = true;
+            }
+            break;
+        case 0x36:
+            s->madctl = b;
+            s->dirty = true;
+            break;
+        case 0x3A:
+            s->colmod = b;
+            break;
+        default:
+            break;
+        }
+        if (s->infer_params_left) {
+            s->infer_params_left--;
+        }
+        if (s->pending_pixel_bytes) {
+            s->pending_pixel_bytes--;
+        }
+    }
+    if (s->immediate_update && s->dirty) {
+        qemu_bh_schedule(s->refresh_bh);
+        qemu_clock_notify(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL);
+    }
+}
+
+uint32_t m5_spi_panel_read_id(M5SpiPanelState *s)
+{
+    return s->read_id;
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_update(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = opaque;
+    DisplaySurface *surf = qemu_console_surface(s->con);
+    if (!surf || surface_bits_per_pixel(surf) != 32 ||
+        surface_width(surf) != s->vis_w || surface_height(surf) != s->vis_h) {
+        surf = qemu_create_displaysurface(s->vis_w, s->vis_h);
+        dpy_gfx_replace_surface(s->con, surf);
+        s->dirty = true;
+    }
+    if (!s->dirty) {
+        return;
+    }
+    s->dirty = false;
+    uint32_t *dst = (uint32_t *)surface_data(surf);
+    int stride = surface_stride(surf) / 4;
+    bool on = s->disp_on && !s->sleep;
+    bool invert = s->physical_invert ^ s->controller_invert;
+    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < s->vis_h; y++) {
+        uint32_t *d = dst + y * stride;
+        for (uint32_t x = 0; x < s->vis_w; x++) {
+            uint32_t mx = x + s->off_x;
+            uint32_t my = y + s->off_y;
+            switch (s->glass_rotation & 3) {
+            case 1:
+                mx = s->off_x + y;
+                my = s->off_y + s->vis_w - 1 - x;
+                break;
+            case 2:
+                mx = s->off_x + s->vis_w - 1 - x;
+                my = s->off_y + s->vis_h - 1 - y;
+                break;
+            case 3:
+                mx = s->off_x + s->vis_h - 1 - y;
+                my = s->off_y + x;
+                break;
+            default:
+                break;
+            }
+            uint16_t p = s->mem[my * s->mem_w + mx];
+            if (invert) {
+                p = ~p;
+            }
+            uint32_t r = ((p >> 11) & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+            uint32_t g = ((p >> 5) & 0x3f) * 255 / 63;
+            uint32_t b = (p & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+            if (!on) {
+                r = g = b = 0;
+            }
+            if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_REFRESH_TRACE") && x == 20 && y == 20) {
+                fprintf(stderr,
+                        "[m5panel-pixel-render] mx=%u my=%u p=%04x on=%d inv=%d\n",
+                        mx, my, p, on, invert);
+            }
+            d[x] = 0xff000000u | (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
+        }
+    }
+    if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_REFRESH_TRACE")) {
+        int stride_dbg = surface_stride(surf) / 4;
+        uint32_t *dbg = (uint32_t *)surface_data(surf);
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-render] surface=%p sample=%08x size=%ux%u\n",
+                (void *)surf, dbg[20 * stride_dbg + 20],
+                surface_width(surf), surface_height(surf));
+    }
+    dpy_gfx_update(s->con, 0, 0, s->vis_w, s->vis_h);
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_invalidate(void *opaque)
+{
+    ((M5SpiPanelState *)opaque)->dirty = true;
+}
+
+static const GraphicHwOps m5_panel_ops = {
+    .invalidate = m5_panel_invalidate,
+    .gfx_update = m5_panel_update,
+};
+
+static uint32_t m5_panel_pixel_rgb(M5SpiPanelState *s, uint32_t x, uint32_t y)
+{
+    uint32_t mx = x + s->off_x;
+    uint32_t my = y + s->off_y;
+
+    switch (s->glass_rotation & 3) {
+    case 1:
+        mx = s->off_x + y;
+        my = s->off_y + s->vis_w - 1 - x;
+        break;
+    case 2:
+        mx = s->off_x + s->vis_w - 1 - x;
+        my = s->off_y + s->vis_h - 1 - y;
+        break;
+    case 3:
+        mx = s->off_x + s->vis_h - 1 - y;
+        my = s->off_y + x;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+
+    uint16_t p = s->mem[my * s->mem_w + mx];
+    bool on = s->disp_on && !s->sleep;
+    bool invert = s->physical_invert ^ s->controller_invert;
+    if (invert) {
+        p = ~p;
+    }
+    uint32_t r = ((p >> 11) & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+    uint32_t g = ((p >> 5) & 0x3f) * 255 / 63;
+    uint32_t b = (p & 0x1f) * 255 / 31;
+    if (!on) {
+        r = g = b = 0;
+    }
+    return (r << 16) | (g << 8) | b;
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_dump_ppm(M5SpiPanelState *s)
+{
+    if (!s->dump_path) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    FILE *f = fopen(s->dump_path, "wb");
+    if (!f) {
+        return;
+    }
+    fprintf(f, "P6\n%u %u\n255\n", s->vis_w, s->vis_h);
+    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < s->vis_h; y++) {
+        for (uint32_t x = 0; x < s->vis_w; x++) {
+            uint32_t rgb = m5_panel_pixel_rgb(s, x, y);
+            uint8_t bytes[3] = { rgb >> 16, rgb >> 8, rgb };
+            fwrite(bytes, sizeof(bytes), 1, f);
+        }
+    }
+    fclose(f);
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_refresh_tick(void *opaque)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = opaque;
+    int64_t now = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL);
+    if (getenv("M5SW_PANEL_REFRESH_TRACE") && s->dirty) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-refresh] dirty sample=%04x\n",
+                s->mem[20 * s->mem_w + 20]);
+    }
+    m5_panel_update(s);
+    timer_mod(s->refresh_timer, now + 16666667);
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_refresh_bh(void *opaque)
+{
+    m5_panel_update(opaque);
+}
+
+static char *m5_panel_get_dump_path(Object *obj, Error **errp)
+{
+    return g_strdup(((M5SpiPanelState *)obj)->dump_path);
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_set_dump_path(Object *obj, const char *value, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = (M5SpiPanelState *)obj;
+    g_free(s->dump_path);
+    s->dump_path = g_strdup(value);
+}
+
+static bool m5_panel_get_refresh_now(Object *obj, Error **errp)
+{
+    return false;
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_set_refresh_now(Object *obj, bool value, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = (M5SpiPanelState *)obj;
+    if (value) {
+        s->dirty = true;
+        m5_panel_update(s);
+        m5_panel_dump_ppm(s);
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t tca8418_event(int row, int col, bool down)
+{
+    int raw_row = col / 2;
+    int raw_col = row + ((col % 2) ? 4 : 0);
+    int value = raw_row * 10 + raw_col + 1;
+
+    return value | (down ? 0x80 : 0);
+}
+
+static bool tca8418_key_pos(int qcode, int *row, int *col)
+{
+    if (qcode >= Q_KEY_CODE_A && qcode <= Q_KEY_CODE_Z) {
+        int index = qcode - Q_KEY_CODE_A;
+        *row = index < 10 ? 2 : 3;
+        *col = index < 10 ? 2 + index : 3 + index - 10;
+        return true;
+    }
+    switch (qcode) {
+    case Q_KEY_CODE_RET:
+        *row = 2;
+        *col = 13;
+        return true;
+    case Q_KEY_CODE_SPC:
+        *row = 3;
+        *col = 13;
+        return true;
+    case Q_KEY_CODE_TAB:
+        *row = 1;
+        *col = 0;
+        return true;
+    case Q_KEY_CODE_BACKSPACE:
+        *row = 0;
+        *col = 13;
+        return true;
+    default:
+        return false;
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_keyboard_event(int qcode, bool down)
+{
+    int row, col;
+
+    if (qcode == Q_KEY_CODE_LEFT || qcode == Q_KEY_CODE_RIGHT ||
+        qcode == Q_KEY_CODE_UP || qcode == Q_KEY_CODE_DOWN) {
+        int fn_raw = tca8418_event(2, 0, down);
+        int direction_raw;
+
+        if (qcode == Q_KEY_CODE_LEFT) {
+            direction_raw = tca8418_event(3, 10, down);
+        } else if (qcode == Q_KEY_CODE_DOWN) {
+            direction_raw = tca8418_event(3, 11, down);
+        } else if (qcode == Q_KEY_CODE_RIGHT) {
+            direction_raw = tca8418_event(3, 12, down);
+        } else {
+            direction_raw = tca8418_event(2, 11, down);
+        }
+        if (down) {
+            m5sw_keyboard_event(fn_raw);
+            m5sw_keyboard_event(direction_raw);
+        } else {
+            m5sw_keyboard_event(direction_raw);
+            m5sw_keyboard_event(fn_raw);
+        }
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (tca8418_key_pos(qcode, &row, &col)) {
+        m5sw_keyboard_event(tca8418_event(row, col, down));
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_input(DeviceState *dev, QemuConsole *src, InputEvent *evt)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = M5_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+    if (getenv("M5SW_INPUT_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-input] type=%d\n", evt->type);
+    }
+    switch (evt->type) {
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_KEY: {
+        InputKeyEvent *key = evt->u.key.data;
+        int q = qemu_input_key_value_to_qcode(key->key);
+        int down = key->down;
+        if (getenv("M5SW_INPUT_TRACE")) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[m5panel-input] qcode=%d down=%d\n", q, down);
+        }
+        if (s->keyboard) {
+            m5_panel_keyboard_event(q, down);
+            break;
+        }
+        if (s->plc_keys) {
+            if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_A) {
+                m5sw_plc_key_set(0, down);
+            } else if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_B) {
+                m5sw_plc_key_set(1, down);
+            } else if (q == Q_KEY_CODE_C) {
+                m5sw_plc_key_set(2, down);
+            }
+            break;
+        }
+        switch (q) {
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_A: case Q_KEY_CODE_LEFT: case Q_KEY_CODE_1:
+            esp32_gpio_set_input(s->key_a_pin, !down);
+            break;
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_B: case Q_KEY_CODE_RIGHT: case Q_KEY_CODE_2:
+            esp32_gpio_set_input(s->key_b_pin, !down);
+            break;
+        case Q_KEY_CODE_P: case Q_KEY_CODE_3:
+            m5sw_pwr_button(down);
+            break;
+        default:
+            break;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_ABS: {
+        InputMoveEvent *m = evt->u.abs.data;
+        int v = qemu_input_scale_axis(m->value, INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MIN,
+                                      INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MAX, 0, s->vis_w - 1);
+        if (m->axis == INPUT_AXIS_X) {
+            s->mouse_x = v;
+        } else if (m->axis == INPUT_AXIS_Y) {
+            s->mouse_y = qemu_input_scale_axis(m->value, INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MIN,
+                                               INPUT_EVENT_ABS_MAX, 0, s->vis_h - 1);
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    case INPUT_EVENT_KIND_BTN: {
+        InputBtnEvent *b = evt->u.btn.data;
+        if (b->button == INPUT_BUTTON_LEFT) {
+            s->mouse_btn = b->down;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static void m5_panel_input_sync(DeviceState *dev)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = M5_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+    if (s->touch) {
+        m5sw_ft_touch_set_pin(s->mouse_btn, s->mouse_x, s->mouse_y,
+                              s->touch_int_pin);
+    }
+}
+
+static const QemuInputHandler m5_panel_input_handler = {
+    .name = "M5Stack SPI panel keys",
+    .mask = INPUT_EVENT_MASK_KEY | INPUT_EVENT_MASK_BTN | INPUT_EVENT_MASK_ABS,
+    .event = m5_panel_input,
+    .sync = m5_panel_input_sync,
+};
+
+static void m5_panel_realize(DeviceState *dev, Error **errp)
+{
+    M5SpiPanelState *s = M5_SPI_PANEL(dev);
+    s->mem = g_new0(uint16_t, s->mem_w * s->mem_h);
+    s->con = graphic_console_init(dev, 0, &m5_panel_ops, s);
+    qemu_console_resize(s->con, s->vis_w, s->vis_h);
+    s->dirty = true;
+    s->sleep = true;
+    s->xe = s->mem_w - 1;
+    s->ye = s->mem_h - 1;
+    m5_panel_reset_window(s);
+    s->refresh_timer = timer_new_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL,
+                                    m5_panel_refresh_tick, s);
+    s->refresh_bh = qemu_bh_new(m5_panel_refresh_bh, s);
+    timer_mod(s->refresh_timer,
+              qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL) + 16666667);
+    QemuInputHandlerState *hs = qemu_input_handler_register(dev, &m5_panel_input_handler);
+    qemu_input_handler_activate(hs);
+    object_property_add_str(OBJECT(s), "dump-path",
+                            m5_panel_get_dump_path, m5_panel_set_dump_path);
+    object_property_add_bool(OBJECT(s), "refresh-now",
+                             m5_panel_get_refresh_now, m5_panel_set_refresh_now);
+}
+
+static Property m5_panel_props[] = {
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("mem-width", M5SpiPanelState, mem_w, 240),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("mem-height", M5SpiPanelState, mem_h, 320),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("vis-width", M5SpiPanelState, vis_w, 135),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("vis-height", M5SpiPanelState, vis_h, 240),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("offset-x", M5SpiPanelState, off_x, 52),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("offset-y", M5SpiPanelState, off_y, 40),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("key-a-pin", M5SpiPanelState, key_a_pin, 10),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("key-b-pin", M5SpiPanelState, key_b_pin, 9),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("read-id", M5SpiPanelState, read_id, 0x00007683),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("glass-rotation", M5SpiPanelState, glass_rotation, 0),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("apply-madctl", M5SpiPanelState, apply_madctl, true),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("touch", M5SpiPanelState, touch, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_UINT32("touch-int-pin", M5SpiPanelState, touch_int_pin, 21),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("immediate-update", M5SpiPanelState, immediate_update, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("keyboard", M5SpiPanelState, keyboard, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("plc-keys", M5SpiPanelState, plc_keys, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("infer-command", M5SpiPanelState, infer_command, false),
+    DEFINE_PROP_BOOL("invert", M5SpiPanelState, physical_invert, true),
+    DEFINE_PROP_END_OF_LIST(),
+};
+
+static void m5_panel_class_init(ObjectClass *klass, void *data)
+{
+    DeviceClass *dc = DEVICE_CLASS(klass);
+    dc->realize = m5_panel_realize;
+    device_class_set_props(dc, m5_panel_props);
+}
+
+static const TypeInfo m5_panel_info = {
+    .name = TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL,
+    .parent = TYPE_DEVICE,
+    .instance_size = sizeof(M5SpiPanelState),
+    .class_init = m5_panel_class_init,
+};
+
+static void m5_panel_register(void)
+{
+    type_register_static(&m5_panel_info);
+}
+type_init(m5_panel_register)
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_spi_sd.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_spi_sd.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..15e4844

+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_spi_sd.c
