+/*
+ * SPI-mode SD card protocol model backed by a raw sector image.
+ *
+ * The model implements the command/data frames used by ESP-IDF's SDSPI host:
+ * CMD0/8/16/17/24/55/58/59, ACMD41 and single-block read/write.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define SD_BLOCK_SIZE 512
+#define SD_RESP_SIZE  1024
+
+typedef enum {
+    SD_IDLE,
+    SD_COMMAND,
+    SD_RESPONSE,
+    SD_WAIT_DATA,
+    SD_WRITE_DATA,
+    SD_WRITE_CRC,
+} SdSpiMode;
+
+typedef struct M5SpiSdState {
+    FILE *image;
+    uint64_t image_size;
+    SdSpiMode mode;
+    uint8_t command[6];
+    uint8_t command_pos;
+    uint8_t response[SD_RESP_SIZE];
+    uint32_t response_pos;
+    uint32_t response_len;
+    uint32_t sector;
+    uint8_t block[SD_BLOCK_SIZE];
+    uint32_t block_pos;
+    uint8_t cid[16];
+    uint8_t csd[16];
+    uint8_t crc_pos;
+    SdSpiMode next_mode;
+    bool app_cmd;
+    bool trace;
+} M5SpiSdState;
+
+static uint8_t sd_crc7(const uint8_t *data, uint32_t size)
+{
+    uint8_t crc = 0;
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < size; i++) {
+        for (int bit = 7; bit >= 0; bit--) {
+            crc <<= 1;
+            if ((crc >> 7) ^ ((data[i] >> bit) & 1)) {
+                crc ^= 0x89;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    return crc;
+}
+
+static uint16_t sd_crc16(const uint8_t *data, uint32_t size)
+{
+    uint16_t crc = 0;
+    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < size; i++) {
+        crc ^= (uint16_t)data[i] << 8;
+        for (int bit = 0; bit < 8; bit++) {
+            crc = (crc & 0x8000) ? (crc << 1) ^ 0x1021 : crc << 1;
+        }
+    }
+    return (crc >> 8) | (crc << 8);
+}
+
+static void sd_set_identity(M5SpiSdState *s, uint64_t size)
+{
+    uint32_t csize;
+    uint32_t sectsize = 63;
+    uint32_t wpsize = 255;
+
+    memset(s->cid, 0, sizeof(s->cid));
+    s->cid[0] = 0xaa;
+    s->cid[1] = 'X';
+    s->cid[2] = 'Y';
+    memcpy(&s->cid[3], "QEMU!", 5);
+    s->cid[8] = 0x01;
+    s->cid[9] = 0xde;
+    s->cid[10] = 0xad;
+    s->cid[11] = 0xbe;
+    s->cid[12] = 0xef;
+    s->cid[13] = 0x00;
+    s->cid[14] = 0x62;
+    s->cid[15] = (sd_crc7(s->cid, 15) << 1) | 1;
+
+    if (!size) {
+        size = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
+    }
+    csize = (size >> 18) - 1;
+    memset(s->csd, 0, sizeof(s->csd));
+    s->csd[0] = 0x00;
+    s->csd[1] = 0x26;
+    s->csd[3] = 0x32;
+    s->csd[4] = 0x5f;
+    s->csd[5] = 0x50 | 9;
+    s->csd[6] = 0xe0 | ((csize >> 10) & 0x03);
+    s->csd[7] = (csize >> 2) & 0xff;
+    s->csd[8] = 0x3f | ((csize << 6) & 0xc0);
+    s->csd[9] = 0xff;
+    s->csd[10] = 0x40 | 0x80 | (sectsize >> 1);
+    s->csd[11] = 0x80 | wpsize;
+    s->csd[12] = 0x92;
+    s->csd[13] = 0x60;
+    s->csd[15] = (sd_crc7(s->csd, 15) << 1) | 1;
+}
+
+static uint8_t sd_spi_transfer(void *opaque, uint8_t tx);
+
+static const M5SpiSlaveOps sd_spi_ops = {
+    .set_cs = NULL,
+    .transfer = sd_spi_transfer,
+};
+
+static void sd_set_response_lead(M5SpiSdState *s, const uint8_t *data,
+                                 uint32_t size, uint32_t lead)
+{
+    size = MIN(size, sizeof(s->response) - lead);
+    memset(s->response, 0xff, lead);
+    s->response_len = size + lead;
+    if (size) {
+        memcpy(&s->response[lead], data, size);
+    }
+    s->response_pos = 0;
+    s->next_mode = SD_IDLE;
+    s->mode = size ? SD_RESPONSE : SD_IDLE;
+}
+
+static void sd_set_response(M5SpiSdState *s, const uint8_t *data,
+                            uint32_t size)
+{
+    sd_set_response_lead(s, data, size, 1);
+}
+
+static void sd_set_read_response(M5SpiSdState *s, const uint8_t *data,
+                                 uint32_t size)
+{
+    sd_set_response_lead(s, data, size, 2);
+}
+
+static void sd_set_response_next(M5SpiSdState *s, const uint8_t *data,
+                                 uint32_t size, SdSpiMode next)
+{
+    sd_set_response(s, data, size);
+    s->next_mode = next;
+}
+
+static void sd_r1(M5SpiSdState *s, uint8_t value)
+{
+    sd_set_response(s, &value, 1);
+}
+
+static uint32_t sd_arg(const uint8_t *cmd)
+{
+    return ((uint32_t)cmd[1] << 24) | ((uint32_t)cmd[2] << 16) |
+           ((uint32_t)cmd[3] << 8) | cmd[4];
+}
+
+static void sd_execute(M5SpiSdState *s)
+{
+    uint8_t cmd = s->command[0] & 0x3f;
+    uint32_t arg = sd_arg(s->command);
+    bool app_cmd = s->app_cmd;
+    uint8_t r7[5] = { 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01, 0xaa };
+    uint8_t r3[5] = { 0x00, 0x00, 0xff, 0x80, 0x00 };
+    uint8_t read_resp[SD_BLOCK_SIZE + 4];
+
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[sd-spi] cmd=%u arg=%08x app=%d\n",
+                cmd, arg, s->app_cmd);
+    }
+    if (cmd != 55) {
+        s->app_cmd = false;
+    }
+    switch (cmd) {
+    case 0: /* GO_IDLE_STATE */
+        sd_r1(s, 0x01);
+        break;
+    case 1: /* SEND_OP_COND */
+        sd_r1(s, 0x00);
+        break;
+    case 8: /* SEND_IF_COND */
+        sd_set_response(s, r7, sizeof(r7));
+        break;
+    case 9:   /* SEND_CSD */
+    case 10:  /* SEND_CID */
+    {
+        uint8_t data[20];
+        const uint8_t *identity = cmd == 9 ? s->csd : s->cid;
+        uint16_t crc = sd_crc16(identity, 16);
+        data[0] = 0x00;
+        data[1] = 0xfe;
+        memcpy(&data[2], identity, 16);
+        data[18] = crc & 0xff;
+        data[19] = crc >> 8;
+        sd_set_read_response(s, data, sizeof(data));
+        break;
+    }
+    case 12: /* STOP_TRANSMISSION */
+    case 16: /* SET_BLOCKLEN */
+    case 59: /* CRC_ON_OFF */
+        sd_r1(s, 0x00);
+        break;
+    case 13: /* SEND_STATUS */
+        if (app_cmd) {
+            uint8_t status[68];
+            uint16_t crc;
+            memset(status, 0, sizeof(status));
+            status[0] = 0x00;
+            status[1] = 0xfe;
+            crc = sd_crc16(&status[2], 64);
+            status[66] = crc & 0xff;
+            status[67] = crc >> 8;
+            sd_set_read_response(s, status, sizeof(status));
+        } else {
+            uint8_t status[2] = { 0x00, 0x00 }; /* R1 + card status */
+            sd_set_response(s, status, sizeof(status));
+        }
+        break;
+    case 17: /* READ_SINGLE_BLOCK */
+        s->sector = arg / SD_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        memset(read_resp, 0xff, sizeof(read_resp));
+        read_resp[0] = 0x00;
+        read_resp[1] = 0xfe;
+        memset(s->block, 0, sizeof(s->block));
+        if (s->image) {
+            fseeko(s->image, (off_t)s->sector * SD_BLOCK_SIZE, SEEK_SET);
+            fread(s->block, 1, sizeof(s->block), s->image);
+        }
+        memcpy(&read_resp[2], s->block, sizeof(s->block));
+        {
+            uint16_t crc = sd_crc16(s->block, sizeof(s->block));
+            read_resp[SD_BLOCK_SIZE + 2] = crc & 0xff;
+            read_resp[SD_BLOCK_SIZE + 3] = crc >> 8;
+        }
+        sd_set_read_response(s, read_resp, sizeof(read_resp));
+        break;
+    case 24: /* WRITE_SINGLE_BLOCK */
+        s->sector = arg / SD_BLOCK_SIZE;
+        s->block_pos = 0;
+        s->crc_pos = 0;
+        {
+            uint8_t r1 = 0x00;
+            sd_set_response_next(s, &r1, 1, SD_WAIT_DATA);
+        }
+        break;
+    case 41: /* ACMD41 */
+        sd_r1(s, 0x00);
+        break;
+    case 51: /* ACMD51: SEND_SCR */
+        if (app_cmd) {
+            uint8_t scr[12] = {
+                0x00, 0xfe,             /* R1 + data token */
+                0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, /* SCR structure/version/data status */
+                0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, /* capability bits: 1-bit SPI */
+                0x00, 0x00,
+                0x00, 0x00,
+            };
+            uint16_t crc = sd_crc16(&scr[2], 8);
+            scr[10] = crc & 0xff;
+            scr[11] = crc >> 8;
+            sd_set_read_response(s, scr, sizeof(scr));
+        } else {
+            sd_r1(s, 0x05);
+        }
+        break;
+    case 55: /* APP_CMD */
+        s->app_cmd = true;
+        sd_r1(s, 0x01);
+        break;
+    case 58: /* READ_OCR */
+        sd_set_response(s, r3, sizeof(r3));
+        break;
+    default:
+        if (s->trace) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "[sd-spi] unsupported cmd=%u\n", cmd);
+        }
+        sd_r1(s, 0x05);
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t sd_spi_transfer(void *opaque, uint8_t tx)
+{
+    M5SpiSdState *s = opaque;
+    uint8_t out = 0xff;
+    uint8_t result;
+
+    switch (s->mode) {
+    case SD_RESPONSE:
+        out = s->response[s->response_pos++];
+        if (s->response_pos >= s->response_len) {
+            s->mode = s->next_mode;
+            s->next_mode = SD_IDLE;
+            s->response_len = 0;
+            s->response_pos = 0;
+        }
+        break;
+    case SD_COMMAND:
+        s->command[s->command_pos++] = tx;
+        if (s->command_pos == sizeof(s->command)) {
+            s->command_pos = 0;
+            sd_execute(s);
+        }
+        break;
+    case SD_WAIT_DATA:
+        if (tx == 0xfe) {
+            out = 0xff;
+            s->mode = SD_WRITE_DATA;
+            s->block_pos = 0;
+        }
+        break;
+    case SD_WRITE_DATA:
+        s->block[s->block_pos++] = tx;
+        if (s->block_pos == SD_BLOCK_SIZE) {
+            s->mode = SD_WRITE_CRC;
+            s->crc_pos = 0;
+        }
+        break;
+    case SD_WRITE_CRC:
+        if (++s->crc_pos >= 2) {
+            if (s->image) {
+                fseeko(s->image, (off_t)s->sector * SD_BLOCK_SIZE, SEEK_SET);
+                fwrite(s->block, 1, sizeof(s->block), s->image);
+                fflush(s->image);
+            }
+            result = 0x05;
+            s->response[0] = result;
+            s->response_pos = 0;
+            s->response_len = 1;
+            s->next_mode = SD_IDLE;
+            s->mode = SD_RESPONSE;
+        }
+        break;
+    case SD_IDLE:
+    default:
+        if (tx == 0xff) {
+            out = 0xff;
+        } else if ((tx & 0xc0) == 0x40) {
+            s->command[0] = tx;
+            s->command_pos = 1;
+            s->mode = SD_COMMAND;
+        }
+        break;
+    }
+    if (s->trace) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[sd-spi] tx=%02x rx=%02x mode=%d\n",
+                tx, out, s->mode);
+    }
+    return out;
+}
+
+void m5_spi_sd_attach(DeviceState *spi, int cs_pin)
+{
+    M5SpiSdState *s = g_new0(M5SpiSdState, 1);
+    const char *path = getenv("M5SW_SPI_SD_IMAGE");
+
+    s->trace = getenv("M5SW_SPI_SD_TRACE") != NULL;
+    if (path) {
+        s->image = fopen(path, "r+b");
+        if (s->image) {
+            fseeko(s->image, 0, SEEK_END);
+            s->image_size = ftello(s->image);
+            fseeko(s->image, 0, SEEK_SET);
+        }
+    }
+    sd_set_identity(s, s->image_size);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_spi_slave(spi, &sd_spi_ops, s, cs_pin);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_st25r3916_spi.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_st25r3916_spi.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2645eb2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_st25r3916_spi.c
@@ -0,0 +1,258 @@
+/*
+ * ST25R3916 NFC reader SPI transport model.
+ *
+ * UIFlow2 uses command/register writes, register reads, Space-B accesses,
+ * FIFO load/read and ISO14443A command responses over this transport. This
+ * model keeps the same deterministic NTAG behavior as the I2C model while
+ * allowing ST25R3916 to share an SPI bus with SD and CC1101 chip selects.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/gpio/esp32_gpio.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define ST25_FIFO_SIZE 512
+#define ST25_TAG_SIZE  180
+
+enum {
+    ST25_IDLE,
+    ST25_REG_WRITE,
+    ST25_REG_READ,
+    ST25_SPACE_ADDR,
+    ST25_SPACE_WRITE,
+    ST25_SPACE_READ,
+    ST25_FIFO_WRITE,
+    ST25_FIFO_READ,
+};
+
+typedef struct M5St25r3916SpiState {
+    uint8_t regs[64];
+    uint8_t space_b[64];
+    uint8_t tx[64];
+    uint32_t tx_len;
+    uint8_t rx[ST25_FIFO_SIZE];
+    uint32_t rx_len;
+    uint32_t rx_pos;
+    uint8_t tag[ST25_TAG_SIZE];
+    uint8_t phase;
+    uint8_t reg;
+    uint8_t space_addr;
+    bool field_on;
+    int irq_pin;
+} M5St25r3916SpiState;
+
+static void st25_set_fifo(M5St25r3916SpiState *s,
+                          const uint8_t *data, uint32_t size)
+{
+    size = MIN(size, sizeof(s->rx));
+    if (size) {
+        memcpy(s->rx, data, size);
+    }
+    s->rx_len = size;
+    s->rx_pos = 0;
+    if (s->irq_pin >= 0) {
+        esp32_gpio_force_line(s->irq_pin, size != 0);
+    }
+}
+
+static void st25_set_fifo_byte(M5St25r3916SpiState *s, uint8_t value)
+{
+    st25_set_fifo(s, &value, 1);
+}
+
+static void st25_init_tag(M5St25r3916SpiState *s)
+{
+    static const uint8_t uid[4] = { 0xde, 0xad, 0xbe, 0xef };
+    static const uint8_t page4[4] = { 'M', '5', 'N', '1' };
+
+    memcpy(s->tag, uid, sizeof(uid));
+    s->tag[4] = uid[0] ^ uid[1] ^ uid[2] ^ uid[3];
+    s->tag[5] = 0x48;
+    s->tag[12] = 0xe1;
+    s->tag[13] = 0x10;
+    s->tag[14] = 0x12;
+    memcpy(&s->tag[16], page4, sizeof(page4));
+}
+
+static void st25_execute_command(M5St25r3916SpiState *s, uint8_t command)
+{
+    static const uint8_t atqa[2] = { 0x44, 0x00 };
+    static const uint8_t version[10] = {
+        0x00, 0x04, 0x04, 0x02, 0x02, 0x01, 0x0b, 0x03, 0x00, 0x00,
+    };
+    uint8_t response[18] = { 0 };
+
+    switch (command) {
+    case 0xc1: /* SET_DEFAULT */
+        memset(s->regs, 0, sizeof(s->regs));
+        memset(s->space_b, 0, sizeof(s->space_b));
+        s->regs[0x3f] = 0x2a;
+        st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        break;
+    case 0xc6: /* TRANSMIT_REQA */
+    case 0xc7: /* TRANSMIT_WUPA */
+        st25_set_fifo(s, atqa, sizeof(atqa));
+        break;
+    case 0xc5: /* TRANSMIT_WITHOUT_CRC */
+        if (s->tx_len >= 1 && s->tx[0] == 0x93) {
+            response[0] = s->tag[0];
+            response[1] = s->tag[1];
+            response[2] = s->tag[2];
+            response[3] = s->tag[3];
+            response[4] = s->tag[4];
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, 5);
+        }
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        break;
+    case 0xc4: /* TRANSMIT_WITH_CRC */
+        if (s->tx_len >= 2 && s->tx[0] == 0x93) {
+            response[0] = 0x00;
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, 1);
+        } else if (s->tx_len >= 1 && s->tx[0] == 0x60) {
+            st25_set_fifo(s, version, sizeof(version));
+        } else if (s->tx_len >= 2 && s->tx[0] == 0x30) {
+            uint32_t base = (s->tx[1] & ~3u) * 4;
+            uint32_t available = sizeof(s->tag) - MIN(base, sizeof(s->tag));
+            uint32_t copy = MIN(available, 16);
+            memcpy(response, &s->tag[base], copy);
+            st25_set_fifo(s, response, copy + 2);
+        } else if (s->tx_len >= 6 && s->tx[0] == 0xa2) {
+            uint32_t offset = (s->tx[1] & 0x3f) * 4;
+            if (offset + 4 <= sizeof(s->tag)) {
+                memcpy(&s->tag[offset], &s->tx[2], 4);
+            }
+            st25_set_fifo_byte(s, 0x0a);
+        } else if (s->tx_len >= 1 && s->tx[0] == 0x50) {
+            st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        }
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        break;
+    case 0xc8: /* NFC_INITIAL_FIELD_ON */
+        s->field_on = true;
+        s->regs[0x1a] |= 0x80;
+        break;
+    case 0xc2: /* STOP_ALL_ACTIVITIES */
+        s->field_on = false;
+        break;
+    case 0xdb: /* CLEAR_FIFO */
+        st25_set_fifo(s, NULL, 0);
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t st25_read_reg(M5St25r3916SpiState *s, uint8_t reg)
+{
+    uint32_t count;
+
+    switch (reg & 0x3f) {
+    case 0x1e:
+        count = MIN(s->rx_len, 0x3ff);
+        return count >> 8;
+    case 0x1f:
+        count = MIN(s->rx_len, 0x3ff);
+        return (count & 0x3f) << 6;
+    case 0x1a:
+        return s->regs[0x1a] |
+               ((s->field_on || (s->regs[0x02] & 0x80)) ? 0x80 : 0);
+    case 0x31:
+        return 0x10;
+    case 0x3f:
+        return 0x2a;
+    default:
+        return s->regs[reg & 0x3f];
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t st25_transfer(void *opaque, uint8_t tx)
+{
+    M5St25r3916SpiState *s = opaque;
+    uint8_t value = 0;
+
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SPI_XFER_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[st25-spi] phase=%u tx=%02x\n", s->phase, tx);
+    }
+    if (s->phase == ST25_IDLE) {
+        if (tx <= 0x3f) {
+            s->reg = tx & 0x3f;
+            s->phase = ST25_REG_WRITE;
+        } else if (tx <= 0x7f) {
+            s->reg = tx & 0x3f;
+            s->phase = ST25_REG_READ;
+        } else if (tx == 0xfb) {
+            s->phase = ST25_SPACE_ADDR;
+        } else if (tx == 0x80) {
+            s->tx_len = 0;
+            s->phase = ST25_FIFO_WRITE;
+        } else if (tx == 0x9f) {
+            s->rx_pos = 0;
+            s->phase = ST25_FIFO_READ;
+        } else {
+            st25_execute_command(s, tx);
+        }
+        return value;
+    }
+
+    switch (s->phase) {
+    case ST25_REG_WRITE:
+        s->regs[s->reg] = tx;
+        s->phase = ST25_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case ST25_REG_READ:
+        value = st25_read_reg(s, s->reg++);
+        break;
+    case ST25_SPACE_ADDR:
+        s->space_addr = tx & 0x3f;
+        s->phase = (tx & 0x40) ? ST25_SPACE_READ : ST25_SPACE_WRITE;
+        break;
+    case ST25_SPACE_WRITE:
+        s->space_b[s->space_addr] = tx;
+        s->phase = ST25_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case ST25_SPACE_READ:
+        value = s->space_b[s->space_addr++ & 0x3f];
+        break;
+    case ST25_FIFO_WRITE:
+        if (s->tx_len < sizeof(s->tx)) {
+            s->tx[s->tx_len++] = tx;
+        }
+        break;
+    case ST25_FIFO_READ:
+        value = s->rx_pos < s->rx_len ? s->rx[s->rx_pos++] : 0;
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+    return value;
+}
+
+static void st25_set_cs(void *opaque, int level)
+{
+    M5St25r3916SpiState *s = opaque;
+
+    if (level) {
+        s->phase = ST25_IDLE;
+        s->reg = 0;
+        s->space_addr = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static const M5SpiSlaveOps st25_ops = {
+    .set_cs = st25_set_cs,
+    .transfer = st25_transfer,
+};
+
+void m5_st25r3916_spi_attach(DeviceState *spi, int cs_pin, int irq_pin)
+{
+    M5St25r3916SpiState *s = g_new0(M5St25r3916SpiState, 1);
+
+    s->irq_pin = irq_pin;
+    s->regs[0x3f] = 0x2a;
+    st25_init_tag(s);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_spi_slave(spi, &st25_ops, s, cs_pin);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(irq_pin, 0);
+    esp32_gpio_force_line(irq_pin, 0);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_sx1262.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_sx1262.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..eafab65
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_sx1262.c
@@ -0,0 +1,247 @@
+/*
+ * Minimal Semtech SX1262 SPI modem model for the UIFlow2 LoRa driver.
+ *
+ * This implements the command/status/register/FIFO protocol and a local TX
+ * loopback packet path. RF modulation and multi-device propagation are not
+ * modeled; packets sent by this modem become the next received packet.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+#define SX1262_MAX_PACKET 255
+
+typedef enum {
+    SX_IDLE,
+    SX_ARGS,
+    SX_RESPONSE,
+    SX_WRITE_BUFFER,
+    SX_READ_BUFFER,
+} Sx1262Mode;
+
+typedef struct M5Sx1262State {
+    const M5SpiSlaveOps *ops;
+    Sx1262Mode mode;
+    uint8_t opcode;
+    uint8_t args[16];
+    uint8_t arg_count;
+    uint8_t expected_args;
+    uint8_t response[264];
+    uint16_t response_len;
+    uint16_t response_pos;
+    uint8_t tx_packet[SX1262_MAX_PACKET];
+    uint8_t rx_packet[SX1262_MAX_PACKET];
+    uint16_t tx_len;
+    uint16_t rx_len;
+    uint16_t rx_pos;
+    uint16_t irq_status;
+    uint16_t registers[16];
+} M5Sx1262State;
+
+static uint8_t sx1262_status(void)
+{
+    return 0x20; /* STDBY_RC, command status OK */
+}
+
+static void sx1262_set_response(M5Sx1262State *s,
+                                const uint8_t *data, uint16_t size)
+{
+    memcpy(s->response, data, MIN(size, sizeof(s->response)));
+    s->response_len = MIN(size, sizeof(s->response));
+    s->response_pos = 0;
+    s->mode = SX_RESPONSE;
+}
+
+static void sx1262_command(M5Sx1262State *s)
+{
+    uint8_t response[4] = { sx1262_status(), 0, 0, 0 };
+
+    switch (s->opcode) {
+    case 0x02: /* CLR_IRQ_STATUS */
+        s->irq_status &= ~(((uint16_t)s->args[0] << 8) | s->args[1]);
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case 0x07: /* CLR_ERRORS */
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case 0x08: /* CFG_DIO_IRQ */
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case 0x0d: { /* WRITE_REGISTER */
+        uint16_t addr = ((uint16_t)s->args[0] << 8) | s->args[1];
+        s->registers[(addr >> 4) & 0xf] = s->args[2];
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    }
+    case 0x0e: /* WRITE_BUFFER */
+        s->tx_len = 0;
+        s->mode = SX_WRITE_BUFFER;
+        break;
+    case 0x12: /* GET_IRQ_STATUS */
+        response[1] = s->irq_status >> 8;
+        response[2] = s->irq_status;
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 3);
+        break;
+    case 0x13: /* GET_RX_BUFFER_STATUS */
+        response[1] = s->rx_len;
+        response[2] = 0;
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 3);
+        break;
+    case 0x14: /* GET_PACKET_STATUS */
+        response[1] = 0xb0; /* RSSI */
+        response[2] = 0x08; /* SNR */
+        response[3] = 0xb0;
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 4);
+        break;
+    case 0x17: /* GET_ERROR */
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 3);
+        break;
+    case 0x1d: { /* READ_REGISTER */
+        uint16_t addr = ((uint16_t)s->args[0] << 8) | s->args[1];
+        response[0] = s->registers[(addr >> 4) & 0xf];
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 1);
+        break;
+    }
+    case 0x1e: /* READ_BUFFER */
+        s->rx_pos = 0;
+        response[0] = sx1262_status();
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 1);
+        s->mode = SX_READ_BUFFER;
+        break;
+    case 0x80: /* SET_STANDBY */
+    case 0x84: /* SET_SLEEP */
+    case 0x82: /* SET_RX */
+    case 0x83: /* SET_TX */
+    case 0x86: /* SET_RF_FREQUENCY */
+    case 0x89: /* CALIBRATE */
+    case 0x8a: /* SET_PACKET_TYPE */
+    case 0x8b: /* SET_MODULATION_PARAMS */
+    case 0x8c: /* SET_PACKET_PARAMS */
+    case 0x8e: /* SET_TX_PARAMS */
+    case 0x8f: /* SET_BUFFER_BASE_ADDRESS */
+    case 0x95: /* SET_PA_CONFIG */
+    case 0x97: /* SET_DIO3_AS_TCXO_CTRL */
+    case 0x98: /* CALIBRATE_IMAGE */
+    case 0x9d: /* SET_DIO2_AS_RF_SWITCH_CTRL */
+        if (s->opcode == 0x83) {
+            memcpy(s->rx_packet, s->tx_packet, s->tx_len);
+            s->rx_len = s->tx_len;
+            s->irq_status = 1; /* TX_DONE */
+        } else if (s->opcode == 0x82 && s->rx_len) {
+            s->irq_status = 2; /* RX_DONE */
+        }
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    case 0xc0: /* GET_STATUS */
+        response[0] = sx1262_status();
+        sx1262_set_response(s, response, 1);
+        break;
+    default:
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        break;
+    }
+}
+
+static uint8_t sx1262_transfer(void *opaque, uint8_t tx)
+{
+    M5Sx1262State *s = opaque;
+    if (getenv("M5SW_SX1262_TRACE")) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[sx1262] mode=%d tx=%02x\n", s->mode, tx);
+    }
+
+    switch (s->mode) {
+    case SX_IDLE:
+        s->opcode = tx;
+        s->arg_count = 0;
+        s->expected_args = 0;
+        s->response_pos = 0;
+        s->response_len = 0;
+        switch (tx) {
+        case 0x02: s->expected_args = 2; break;
+        case 0x07: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x08: s->expected_args = 8; break;
+        case 0x0d: s->expected_args = 3; break;
+        case 0x0e: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x1d: s->expected_args = 3; break;
+        case 0x1e: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x80: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x82: s->expected_args = 3; break;
+        case 0x83: s->expected_args = 3; break;
+        case 0x84: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x86: s->expected_args = 4; break;
+        case 0x89: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x8a: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        case 0x8b: s->expected_args = 4; break;
+        case 0x8c: s->expected_args = 6; break;
+        case 0x8e: s->expected_args = 2; break;
+        case 0x8f: s->expected_args = 2; break;
+        case 0x95: s->expected_args = 4; break;
+        case 0x97: s->expected_args = 4; break;
+        case 0x98: s->expected_args = 2; break;
+        case 0x9d: s->expected_args = 1; break;
+        default: break;
+        }
+        if (s->expected_args == 0) {
+            sx1262_command(s);
+        } else {
+            s->mode = SX_ARGS;
+        }
+        return sx1262_status();
+    case SX_ARGS:
+        if (s->arg_count < sizeof(s->args)) {
+            s->args[s->arg_count] = tx;
+        }
+        s->arg_count++;
+        if (s->arg_count >= s->expected_args) {
+            sx1262_command(s);
+        }
+        return sx1262_status();
+    case SX_RESPONSE:
+        if (s->response_pos < s->response_len) {
+            return s->response[s->response_pos++];
+        }
+        return sx1262_status();
+    case SX_WRITE_BUFFER:
+        if (s->tx_len < SX1262_MAX_PACKET) {
+            s->tx_packet[s->tx_len++] = tx;
+        }
+        return sx1262_status();
+    case SX_READ_BUFFER:
+        if (!s->response_pos) {
+            s->response_pos++;
+            return sx1262_status();
+        }
+        if (s->rx_pos < s->rx_len) {
+            return s->rx_packet[s->rx_pos++];
+        }
+        return 0;
+    default:
+        return sx1262_status();
+    }
+}
+
+static void sx1262_set_cs(void *opaque, int level)
+{
+    M5Sx1262State *s = opaque;
+    if (level) {
+        s->mode = SX_IDLE;
+        s->response_pos = 0;
+        s->response_len = 0;
+    }
+}
+
+static const M5SpiSlaveOps sx1262_ops = {
+    .set_cs = sx1262_set_cs,
+    .transfer = sx1262_transfer,
+};
+
+void m5_sx1262_attach(DeviceState *spi, int cs_pin)
+{
+    M5Sx1262State *s = g_new0(M5Sx1262State, 1);
+    s->ops = &sx1262_ops;
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_spi_slave(spi, &sx1262_ops, s, cs_pin);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(21, 0); /* SX_BUSY: command processor ready */
+    esp32_gpio_force_line(21, 0);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(5, 0);  /* DIO1 idle */
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_uart_peer.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_uart_peer.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..74d5b4c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5_uart_peer.c
@@ -0,0 +1,31 @@
+/*
+ * Byte-level UART peer used by RS485 and other half-duplex board buses.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/char/esp32_uart.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+typedef struct M5UartPeer {
+    ESP32UARTState *uart;
+    bool echo;
+} M5UartPeer;
+
+static void m5_uart_peer_tx(void *opaque, uint8_t byte)
+{
+    M5UartPeer *peer = opaque;
+
+    if (peer->echo) {
+        esp32_uart_inject_rx(peer->uart, &byte, 1);
+    }
+}
+
+void m5_uart_echo_attach(DeviceState *uart, const char *enable_env)
+{
+    M5UartPeer *peer = g_new0(M5UartPeer, 1);
+
+    peer->uart = ESP32_UART(uart);
+    peer->echo = enable_env && getenv(enable_env) != NULL;
+    esp32_uart_set_tx_byte_callback(peer->uart, m5_uart_peer_tx, peer);
+}

+/*
+ * M5Stack AtomS3R board glue: ESP32-S3-PICO-1-N8R8 + ST7735S
+ * 128x128 SPI panel, BMI270 IMU and one GPIO button.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5atoms3r_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                          ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "lp5562", 0x30 },
+        { "es8311", 0x18 },
+        { "pi4ioe", 0x43 },
+        { "bmm150", 0x10 },
+        { "bmi270", 0x68 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init(bus);
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+    memory_region_init_alias(spi2_alias, NULL, "m5atoms3r.spi2-alias",
+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi2_alias, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 132);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 132);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 128);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 128);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 2);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 1);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 41);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 41);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x00007683);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "glass-rotation", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "infer-command", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 42);
+}

diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5core2.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5core2.c
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..46f0c48
--- /dev/null
+++ b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5core2.c
@@ -0,0 +1,82 @@
+/*
+ * M5Stack Core2 board glue: ESP32 LX6 + ILI9342 320x240 SPI panel,
+ * AXP2101/AXP192 PMIC and FT5x06-compatible touch.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5core2_board_init(Esp32SocState *s)
+{
+    I2CBus *bus0 = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(DEVICE(&s->i2c[0]), "i2c");
+    const char *pmic_override = getenv("M5SW_CORE2_PMIC");
+    bool legacy_axp192 = pmic_override && !strcmp(pmic_override, "axp192");
+
+    /*
+     * Core2 wires AXP, RTC, touch and IMU to one physical GPIO21/22 bus.
+     * ESP32 has two controller register blocks, either of which firmware may
+     * route to those pins. Point both controllers at the same board bus and
+     * attach each slave once.
+     */
+    esp32_i2c_set_bus(DEVICE(&s->i2c[0]), bus0);
+    esp32_i2c_set_bus(DEVICE(&s->i2c[1]), bus0);
+
+    DeviceState *axp = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+    qdev_prop_set_string(axp, "model", legacy_axp192 ? "axp192" : "axp2101");
+    qdev_prop_set_uint8(axp, "address", 0x34);
+    object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(),
+                              legacy_axp192 ? "axp192" : "axp2101",
+                              OBJECT(axp));
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(axp, (BusState *)bus0, &error_fatal);
+
+    if (!legacy_axp192) {
+        for (uint8_t address = 0x40; address <= 0x41; address++) {
+            DeviceState *ina = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+            char *path = g_strdup_printf("ina3221-%02x", address);
+
+            qdev_prop_set_string(ina, "model", "ina3221");
+            qdev_prop_set_uint8(ina, "address", address);
+            object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), path, OBJECT(ina));
+            qdev_realize_and_unref(ina, (BusState *)bus0, &error_fatal);
+            g_free(path);
+        }
+    }
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; const char *path; } devs[] = {
+        { "ft5x06", 0x38, "core2-ft5x06" },
+        { "mpu6886", 0x68, "mpu6886" },
+        { "pcf8563", 0x51, "pcf8563" },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].path, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus0, &error_fatal);
+    }
+
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init_pins(bus0, 21, 22);
+    esp32_gpio_set_input(39, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "glass-rotation", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x000000e3);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "touch", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "touch-int-pin", 39);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "immediate-update", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), "m5-panel", OBJECT(panel));
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32_spi_attach_m5_panel(DEVICE(&s->spi[3]), panel, 15);
+}
diff --git a/hw/m5stopwatch/m5cores3.c b/hw/m5stopwatch/m5cores3.c

+/*
+ * M5Stack CoreS3 board glue: ESP32-S3 + ILI9342 320x240 SPI panel,
+ * AXP2101 PMIC, AW9523 GPIO expander, GC0308 camera and
+ * FT5x06-compatible touch.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5cores3_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                         ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "axp2101", 0x34 },
+        { "aw9523", 0x58 },
+        { "bmm150", 0x10 },
+        { "gc0308", 0x21 },
+        { "ltr553", 0x23 },
+        { "aw88298", 0x36 },
+        { "ft5x06", 0x38 },
+        { "pcf8563", 0x51 },
+        { "es7210", 0x40 },
+        { "bmi270", 0x69 },
+        { "rx8130", 0x32 },
+        { "es8311", 0x18 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init_pins(bus, 12, 11);
+
+    DeviceState *cam = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_LCD_CAM);
+    m5_lcd_cam_attach(cam, gdma, 320, 240);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, 0x60041000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), 0), 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(
+        SYS_BUS_DEVICE(cam), 0,
+        qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_LCD_CAM_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+    memory_region_init_alias(spi2_alias, NULL, "m5cores3.spi2-alias",
+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi2_alias, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 0);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x000000e3);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "touch", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 35);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_panel_cs_pin(spi, 3);
+    /*
+     * Display and M-Bus share the ESP32-S3 SPI2 register window.  M-Bus
+     * provides the external SD card on CS4 and the MAX3421E USB host on
+     * CS0 with IRQ G14; the display has no readback path and is gated off
+     * whenever an M-Bus slave owns the transaction.
+     */
+    m5_spi_sd_attach(spi, 4);
+    m5_max3421e_attach(spi, 0, 14);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_spi_slave_dma(spi, 4, GDMA_SPI2);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_spi_slave_dma(spi, 0, GDMA_SPI2);

+/*
+ * M5Stack PaperColor board glue: ESP32-S3R8, ED2208 Spectra 6
+ * 400x600 e-paper, M5PM1 and three GPIO keys.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5papercolor_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                             ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "m5pm1", 0x6E },
+        { "rx8130", 0x32 },
+        { "es8311", 0x18 },
+        { "es7210", 0x40 },
+        { "sht40", 0x44 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init_pins(bus, 3, 2);
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi_mr, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_EPD_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "native-width", 400);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "native-height", 600);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "out-width", 400);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "out-height", 600);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "color", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "busy-pin", 11);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 10);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 9);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-c-pin", 1);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach_epd(spi, gdma, M5_EPD_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI2);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 43);
+
+    DeviceState *sd_spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(sd_spi), &error_fatal);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(
+        sys_mem, 0x60025000,
+        sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(sd_spi), 0), 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(sd_spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(sd_spi, gdma, NULL, NULL, GDMA_SPI3);
+    m5_spi_sd_attach(sd_spi, 47);
+}

+/*
+ * M5Stack StamPLC board glue: ESP32-S3FN8 + ST7789 240x135,
+ * PI4IOE5V6408 industrial I/O expander and three front-panel keys.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "m5stopwatch.h"
+
+void m5stamplc_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                          ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "ina226", 0x40 },
+        { "pi4ioe", 0x43 },
+        { "pi4ioe", 0x44 },
+        { "lm75", 0x48 },
+        { "aw9523", 0x59 },
+        { "rx8130", 0x32 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        g_autofree char *child_name =
+            g_strdup_printf("%s-%02x", devs[i].model, devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), child_name, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+    memory_region_init_alias(spi2_alias, NULL, "m5stamplc.spi2-alias",
+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi2_alias, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 135);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 40);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 53);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x00000081);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "apply-madctl", false);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "plc-keys", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 6);
+
+    /* PWR-485 is UART2: TX/BOOT G0, RX G39, direction G46. */
+    m5_uart_echo_attach(m5soc_get_uart_device(2), "M5SW_RS485_ECHO");
+}

+/*
+ * M5Stack StickS3 board glue: ESP32-S3 + ST7789 135x240 SPI panel,
+ * M5PM1 PMIC, BMI270 IMU and two GPIO buttons.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later

+#include "qemu/osdep.h"
+#include "hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.h"
+#include "hw/dma/esp_gdma.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"

+void m5sticks3_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                          ESPGdmaState *gdma)

+    m5stopwatch_sens_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_regi2c_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_fe_init(sys_mem);
+    m5stopwatch_phy_init(sys_mem);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c0 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c0_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60013000, i2c0_mr, 1);
+
+    DeviceState *i2c1 = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_I2C);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *i2c1_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60027000, i2c1_mr, 1);
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c0), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT0_INTR_SOURCE));
+    sysbus_connect_irq(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(i2c1), 0,
+                       qdev_get_gpio_in(intmatrix, ETS_I2C_EXT1_INTR_SOURCE));
+
+    I2CBus *bus = (I2CBus *)qdev_get_child_bus(i2c0, "i2c");
+    esp32s3_i2c_set_bus(i2c1, bus);
+
+    static const struct { const char *model; uint8_t addr; } devs[] = {
+        { "m5pm1", 0x6E },
+        { "bmi270", 0x68 },
+        { "es8311", 0x18 },
+    };
+    for (int i = 0; i < ARRAY_SIZE(devs); i++) {
+        DeviceState *d = qdev_new(TYPE_M5SW_I2C_DEV);
+        qdev_prop_set_string(d, "model", devs[i].model);
+        qdev_prop_set_uint8(d, "address", devs[i].addr);
+        object_property_add_child(qdev_get_machine(), devs[i].model, OBJECT(d));
+        qdev_realize_and_unref(d, (BusState *)bus, &error_fatal);
+    }
+    m5stopwatch_soft_i2c_init(bus);
+
+    DeviceState *spi = qdev_new(TYPE_ESP32S3_GPSPI);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), &error_fatal);
+    MemoryRegion *spi_mr = sysbus_mmio_get_region(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(spi), 0);
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60025000, spi_mr, 1);
+    MemoryRegion *spi2_alias = g_new(MemoryRegion, 1);
+    memory_region_init_alias(spi2_alias, NULL, "m5sticks3.spi2-alias",
+                             spi_mr, 0, memory_region_size(spi_mr));
+    memory_region_add_subregion_overlap(sys_mem, 0x60024000, spi2_alias, 1);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_connect_irqs(spi, intmatrix, ETS_SPI2_INTR_SOURCE,
+                                ETS_SPI3_INTR_SOURCE);
+
+    DeviceState *panel = qdev_new(TYPE_M5_SPI_PANEL);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-width", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "mem-height", 320);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-width", 135);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "vis-height", 240);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-x", 52);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "offset-y", 40);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "read-id", 0x00000081);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-a-pin", 11);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint32(panel, "key-b-pin", 12);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "infer-command", true);
+    qdev_prop_set_bit(panel, "invert", true);
+    qdev_realize_and_unref(panel, NULL, &error_fatal);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_attach(spi, gdma, NULL, M5_SPI_PANEL(panel), GDMA_SPI3);
+    esp32s3_gpspi_set_dc_pin(spi, 45);
+}
+
+void m5_board_init(MemoryRegion *sys_mem, DeviceState *intmatrix,
+                   ESPGdmaState *gdma)
+{
+    m5_i2s_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+    switch (m5_board) {
+    case M5_BOARD_STOPWATCH:
+        m5stopwatch_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_STICKS3:
+        m5sticks3_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_ATOMS3R:
+        m5atoms3r_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_CORES3:
+        m5cores3_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_CARDPUTER_ADV:
+        m5cardputeradv_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_STAMPLC:
+        m5stamplc_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_STACKCHAN:
+        m5stackchan_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_PAPERMONO:
+        m5papermono_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    case M5_BOARD_PAPERCOLOR:
+        m5papercolor_board_init(sys_mem, intmatrix, gdma);
+        break;
+    default:
+        break;
+    }
+}
