 {
     ESP32S3ClockState *s = ESP32S3_CLOCK(opaque);
 
+    if (getenv("M5SW_CLK_TRACE")
+        && (addr == A_SYSTEM_PERIP_CLK_EN0
+            || addr == A_SYSTEM_PERIP_CLK_EN1
+            || addr == A_SYSTEM_PERIP_RST_EN0
+            || addr == A_SYSTEM_PERIP_RST_EN1
+            || addr == A_SYSTEM_BT_LPCK_DIV_INT
+            || addr == A_SYSTEM_BT_LPCK_DIV_FRAC)) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[clk] wr %03" HWADDR_PRIx " <- %08" PRIx64 "\n",
+                addr, value);
+    }
     switch(addr) {
         case A_SYSTEM_CORE_1_CONTROL_1_REG:
                 s->app_cpu_addr = (uint32_t)value;
             break;
+        case A_SYSTEM_PERIP_CLK_EN0:
+            s->perip_clk_en0 = (uint32_t)value;
+            if (s->perip_clk_en1 & BIT(9)) {
+                s->perip_clk_en0 |= BIT(9);
+            }
+            break;
+        case A_SYSTEM_PERIP_CLK_EN1:
+            s->perip_clk_en1 = (uint32_t)value;
+            /*
+             * ESP-IDF v5.5.4's uart_ll_is_enabled() checks UART2 through the
+             * EN0 register even though the real gate is EN1 bit 9.  Mirror the
+             * gate so the unmodified driver can validate the port.
+             */
+            if (s->perip_clk_en1 & BIT(9)) {
+                s->perip_clk_en0 |= BIT(9);
+            } else {
+                s->perip_clk_en0 &= ~BIT(9);
+            }
+            break;
+        case A_SYSTEM_PERIP_RST_EN0:
+            s->perip_rst_en0 = (uint32_t)value;
+            if (s->perip_rst_en1 & BIT(9)) {
+                s->perip_rst_en0 |= BIT(9);
+            } else {
+                s->perip_rst_en0 &= ~BIT(9);
+            }
+            break;
+        case A_SYSTEM_PERIP_RST_EN1:
+            s->perip_rst_en1 = (uint32_t)value;
+            if (s->perip_rst_en1 & BIT(9)) {
+                s->perip_rst_en0 |= BIT(9);
+            } else {
+                s->perip_rst_en0 &= ~BIT(9);
+            }
+            break;
+        case A_SYSTEM_BT_LPCK_DIV_INT:
+            s->bt_lpck_div_int = (uint32_t)value & 0xfffU;
+            break;
+        case A_SYSTEM_BT_LPCK_DIV_FRAC:
+            /*
+             * BTDM programs the LP clock source and dividers here, then
+             * immediately reads the register back before returning success.
+             * Preserve the implemented fields so those hardware handshakes
+             * observe the same write/read semantics as the real SoC.
+             */
+            s->bt_lpck_div_frac = (uint32_t)value & 0x1fffffffU;
+            break;

         case A_SYSTEM_CPU_INTR_FROM_CPU_1:

--- a/hw/xtensa/esp32s3_intc.c

 #define IRQ_MAP(cpu, input) s->irq_map[cpu][input]
 
+static int esp32s3_intmatrix_output_index(Esp32s3IntMatrixState *s,
+                                          int cpu_index, int out_index)
+{
+    for (int int_index = 0;
+         int_index < s->cpu[cpu_index]->env.config->nextint; ++int_index) {
+        if (s->cpu[cpu_index]->env.config->extint[int_index] == out_index) {
+            return int_index;
+        }
+    }
+    return -1;
+}
+
+static void esp32s3_intmatrix_update_line(Esp32s3IntMatrixState *s,
+                                          int cpu_index, int out_index)
+{
+    if (s->outputs[cpu_index] == NULL || out_index == INTMATRIX_UNINT_VALUE) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    int int_index = esp32s3_intmatrix_output_index(s, cpu_index, out_index);
+    if (int_index < 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    int level = 0;
+    for (int source = 0; source < ESP32S3_INT_MATRIX_INPUTS; ++source) {
+        if (IRQ_MAP(cpu_index, source) == out_index &&
+            s->input_levels[source]) {
+            level = 1;
+            break;
+        }
+    }
+    qemu_set_irq(s->outputs[cpu_index][int_index], level);
+}
+
+static void esp32s3_intmatrix_set_route(Esp32s3IntMatrixState *s,
+                                        int cpu_index, int source_index,
+                                        int out_index)
+{
+    int old_out = s->source_out[cpu_index][source_index];
+
+    IRQ_MAP(cpu_index, source_index) = out_index;
+    s->source_out[cpu_index][source_index] = out_index;
+    if (old_out != out_index) {
+        esp32s3_intmatrix_update_line(s, cpu_index, old_out);
+    }
+    esp32s3_intmatrix_update_line(s, cpu_index, out_index);
+}

+/*
+ * "Unimplemented" device with default value
+ *
+ * Copyright Linaro Limited, 2017
+ * Written by Peter Maydell
+ * Modified by redfast00 2023
+ */
+
+#ifndef HW_MISC_UNIMP_DEFAULT_H
+#define HW_MISC_UNIMP_DEFAULT_H
+
+#include "hw/qdev-properties.h"
+#include "hw/sysbus.h"
+#include "qapi/error.h"
+#include "qom/object.h"
+
+#define TYPE_UNIMPLEMENTED_DEFAULT_DEVICE "unimplemented-default-device"
+
+OBJECT_DECLARE_SIMPLE_TYPE(UnimplementedDefaultDeviceState, UNIMPLEMENTED_DEFAULT_DEVICE)
+
+struct UnimplementedDefaultDeviceState {
+    SysBusDevice parent_obj;
+    MemoryRegion iomem;
+    unsigned offset_fmt_width;
+    char *name;
+    uint64_t size;
+    uint64_t default_value;
+};
+
+/**
+ * create_unimplemented_default_device: create and map a dummy device with default value
+ * @name: name of the device for debug logging
+ * @base: base address of the device's MMIO region
+ * @size: size of the device's MMIO region
+ * @default_value: value returned for read operations
+ *
+ * This utility function creates and maps an instance of unimplemented-device,
+ * which is a dummy device which simply logs all guest accesses to
+ * it via the qemu_log LOG_UNIMP debug log.
+ * The device is mapped at priority -1000, which means that you can
+ * use it to cover a large region and then map other devices on top of it
+ * if necessary.
+ */
+static inline void create_unimplemented_default_device(const char *name,
+                                               hwaddr base,
+                                               hwaddr size, uint64_t default_value)
+{
+    DeviceState *dev = qdev_new(TYPE_UNIMPLEMENTED_DEFAULT_DEVICE);
+
+    qdev_prop_set_string(dev, "name", name);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint64(dev, "size", size);
+    qdev_prop_set_uint64(dev, "default_value", default_value);
+    sysbus_realize_and_unref(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), &error_fatal);
+
+    sysbus_mmio_map_overlap(SYS_BUS_DEVICE(dev), 0, base, -1000);
+}
+
+#endif

+/*
+ * Classic ESP32 native HCI transport.
+ *
+ * This frontend owns H4 packet framing, bounded host/guest queues and the
+ * classic ESP32 VHCI shared-memory adapter.
+ *
+ * SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later
+ */
+#ifndef HW_XTENSA_ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_H
+#define HW_XTENSA_ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_H
+
+#include "chardev/char-fe.h"
+#include "exec/memory.h"
+#include "hw/qdev-core.h"
+#include "qemu/timer.h"
+#include "qom/object.h"
+
+#define TYPE_ESP32_NATIVE_HCI "esp32-native-hci"
+OBJECT_DECLARE_SIMPLE_TYPE(Esp32NativeHciState, ESP32_NATIVE_HCI)
+
+#define ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_QUEUE_DEPTH 32
+#define ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_PACKET_MAX 2048
+#define ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_RX_BUFFER_SIZE 8192
+#define ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_TRACE_SLOTS 21
+/*
+ * Four simultaneous cold starts can consume the initial HCI command ACKs
+ * before CPU0 reaches another safe idle point. Keep enough real guest
+ * transport objects to cover the startup burst without nested guest calls.
+ */
+#define ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_HLI_EVENT_PREALLOC 8
+#define ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_HLI_ACL_PREALLOC 4
+
+typedef struct Esp32NativeHciPacket {
+    uint16_t length;
+    uint8_t data[ESP32_NATIVE_HCI_PACKET_MAX];
+} Esp32NativeHciPacket;
+

+                "[xtensa-debug] pc=%08x cause=%08x ps=%08x "
+                "intlevel=%u excm=%u\n",
+                env->pc, cause, env->sregs[PS],
+                xtensa_get_cintlevel(env),
+                !!(env->sregs[PS] & PS_EXCM));
+    }
     if (xtensa_get_cintlevel(env) < env->config->debug_level) {
         HELPER(debug_exception)(env, env->pc, cause);
     }

+void HELPER(native_hci_send_packet)(CPUXtensaState *env,
+                                    uint32_t data, uint32_t length)
+{
+    if (env->native_hci_send_callback) {
+        env->native_hci_send_callback(
+            env->native_hci_send_opaque, env_cpu(env), data, length);
+    }
+}
+
+void HELPER(native_hci_semaphore_take_bypass)(CPUXtensaState *env)
+{
+    env->regs[10] = 1;
+}
+
+void HELPER(native_hci_trace_pc)(CPUXtensaState *env, uint32_t pc)
+{
+    if (env->native_hci_trace_callback) {
+        env->native_hci_trace_callback(
+            env->native_hci_trace_opaque, env_cpu(env), pc,
+            env->regs[10], env->regs[11], env->regs[12], env->regs[13]);
+    }
+}
+
 void HELPER(update_ccount)(CPUXtensaState *env)
 {
     XtensaCPU *cpu = env_archcpu(env);
     uint64_t now = qemu_clock_get_ns(QEMU_CLOCK_VIRTUAL);
+    static unsigned int trace_count;
 
     env->ccount_time = now;
     env->sregs[CCOUNT] = env->ccount_base +
         (uint32_t)clock_ns_to_ticks(cpu->clock, now - env->time_base);
+    if (getenv("M5SW_CCOUNT_TRACE") && now > 1000000000
+        && (trace_count++ & 4095) == 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "[ccount] cpu=%d now=%" PRIu64 " value=%u\n",
+                env_cpu(env)->cpu_index, now, env->sregs[CCOUNT]);
+    }
 }
